  Our evaluation on Freebase, WikiData, and DBpedia shows that COREKG delivers higher query-answering accuracy and structural coverage than the state-of-the-art methods, such as GLIMPSE, PPR, iSummary, PEGASUS and APEX$^2$ while requiring only a tiny fraction of the original graph.

</details>


### [108] [Explainable Detection of Depression Status Shifts from User Digital Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14995)
*Loris Belcastro,Francesco Gervino,Fabrizio Marozzo,Domenico Talia,Paolo Trunfio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合BERT多维度特征提取与LLM解释功能的可解释框架，通过分析社交媒体数字足迹的时间轨迹，有效捕捉并总结心理健康状况的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用社交媒体等数字足迹中蕴含的时间戳信息，通过分析用户心理健康信号的演变（改善、恶化或稳定），为检测和分析抑郁相关状态的转变提供一种可解释的方法。

Method: 提出一个可解释的框架：1.利用多个基于BERT的模型从情感、情绪和抑郁严重程度等多个维度提取信号；2.将信号在时间轴上聚合，构建用户轨迹并识别关键变化点；3.整合大语言模型（LLM）生成简洁、可读的总结报告。

Result: 在两个社交媒体数据集上的实验表明，该方法比直接使用LLM生成报告具有更强的连贯性、更高的历史覆盖率、更好的时间一致性和对变化点的更佳敏感度。消融实验证实了时间建模和分割组件的必要性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结合BERT的多维度特征提取与LLM的总结能力，实现了对心理健康状况变化轨迹的可解释性分析，为心理健康研究提供了辅助工具，而非临床诊断工具。

Abstract: Every day, users generate digital traces (e.g., social media posts, chats, and online interactions) that are inherently timestamped and may reflect aspects of their mental state. These traces can be organized into temporal trajectories that capture how a user's mental health signals evolve, including phases of improvement, deterioration, or stability. In this work, we propose an explainable framework for detecting and analyzing depression-related status shifts in user digital traces. The approach combines multiple BERT-based models to extract complementary signals across different dimensions (e.g., sentiment, emotion, and depression severity). Such signals are then aggregated over time to construct user-level trajectories that are analyzed to identify meaningful change points. To enhance interpretability, the framework integrates a large language model to generate concise and human-readable reports that describe the evolution of mental-health signals and highlight key transitions. We evaluate the framework on two social media datasets. Results show that the approach produces more coherent and informative summaries than direct LLM-based reporting, achieving higher coverage of user history, stronger temporal coherence, and improved sensitivity to change points. An ablation study confirms the contribution of each component, particularly temporal modeling and segmentation. Overall, the method provides an interpretable view of mental health signals over time, supporting research and decision making without aiming at clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [109] [From Sycophantic Consensus to Pluralistic Repair: Why AI Alignment Must Surface Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14912)
*Varad Vishwarupe,Nigel Shadbolt,Marina Jirotka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对AI对齐中的“谄媚式共识”问题，本文提出从单纯的偏好聚合转向包含冲突显现与原则性修复的交互设计，并引入PRS指标衡量模型在争议中是否进行原则性修正而非一味迎合用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批判当前基于偏好聚合的对齐范式，指出其导致了“谄媚式共识”（sycophantic consensus），使得AI在处理复杂价值观争端时丧失独立性，造成结构性失效。

Method: 基于格赖斯会话准则提出三种交互机制：Scoping（范围限定）、Signalling（冲突揭示）、Repair（原则修正）；并提出PRS（多元化修正得分）指标量化评估模型的抗诱导和原则性修正能力。

Result: 通过对 Claude Sonnet 4.5 和 GPT-4o 的评估，证实当前前沿模型在面对价值观争议提示词时，普遍表现出“顺应（agreeing）”高于“深度修正（repair-quality）”的倾向。

Conclusion: 多元化对齐不能仅依赖聚合，必须从交互层面引入明确的对抗、原则性修正机制，并将重心转向部署治理（接口、数据流水线和审计架构）。

Abstract: Pluralistic alignment is typically operationalised as preference aggregation: producing responses that span (Overton), steer toward (Steerable), or proportionally represent (Distributional) diverse human values. We argue that aggregation alone is an incomplete primitive for deployed pluralistic alignment. Under genuine value pluralism, the failure mode of contemporary RLHF-trained assistants is not insufficient coverage but sycophantic consensus: a learned tendency to agree with, validate, and minimise friction with the immediate interlocutor. Because deployed AI systems now mediate consequential deliberation across health, civic life, labour, and governance, the collapse of disagreement at the interaction layer is not a narrow technical concern but a structural failure with distributive consequences. We reframe pluralistic alignment around three conversational mechanisms drawn from Grice's maxims: scoping (acknowledging the limits of one's perspective), signalling (surfacing value-conflict rather than smoothing it over), and repair (revising one's position on principled grounds, not on user pressure). We formalise a metric, the Pluralistic Repair Score (PRS), distinguishing principled revision from capitulation, and present a small-scale empirical illustration on two frontier RLHF-trained models (Claude Sonnet 4.5, N=198; GPT-4o, N=100) showing that, for both, agreement-following coexists with low repair-quality on contested-value prompts. PRS measures an interactional precondition for pluralism (visible disagreement; principled revision) rather than pluralism in full; we discuss the difference, take seriously the reflexive question of whose "principled" counts, and argue that pluralism is most decisively made or unmade at the deployment-governance layer: interfaces, preference-data pipelines, and audit infrastructure.

</details>


### [110] [GraphFlow: An Architecture for Formally Verifiable Visual Workflows Enabling Reliable Agentic AI Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14968)
*Drewry H. Morris,Luis Valles,Reza Hosseini Ghomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphFlow 是一个旨在提升多步骤智能体自动化可靠性的工作流系统，通过引入“可执行声明式规范”和“合约验证机制”，有效解决了复杂流程中的逻辑脆弱性与审计难题。在临床试点中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体自动化系统在处理多步骤关键任务时，由于步骤间的错误累积（Error Propagation），导致整体可靠性极低；且现有平台缺乏语义上的正确性保证，智能体的推理行为具有不可预测性及难以审计的问题。

Method: 1. 架构设计：将工作流图作为定义数据范围、执行语义和监控的可执行规范（Single Artifact）。
2. 合约机制：引入预处理、后处理和组合义务的合约模型，并在 Admission 时进行形式化证明验证。
3. 运行时增强：利用持久化引擎记录日志，支持重试、回放与审计；通过 Swimlanes（泳道）划分信任边界，明确区分逻辑验证与外部交互。

Result: 在长达一年的三个临床站点试点中，通过 8,728 次流程运行，原型系统实现了 97.08% 的完成率，且故障主要源于外部系统集成，而非工作流逻辑本身。

Conclusion: GraphFlow 通过将工作流图作为可执行规范并引入合约验证机制，为智能体自动化提供了可靠性保障。尽管目前形式化验证部分尚在开发中，但原型系统在临床场景的实地部署表现出了极高的任务完成率，证明了其架构设计的有效性。

Abstract: GraphFlow is a visual workflow system designed to improve the reliability of agentic AI automation in multi-step, mission-critical processes. In these workflows, small errors compound rapidly: under an idealized model of independent steps, a ten-step process with 90% per-step reliability completes successfully only 35% of the time. Existing workflow platforms provide durable execution and observability but offer few semantic correctness guarantees, while agentic systems plan at inference time, making behavior sensitive to prompt variation and difficult to audit.
  GraphFlow is designed to address this gap by treating workflow diagrams as the executable specification, a single artifact defining data scope, execution semantics, and monitoring. At compile time, a restricted class of diagrams is specified to produce reusable automations whose contracts (preconditions, postconditions, and composition obligations) are intended to be proof-checked before admission to a shared library. At runtime, a durable engine records outcomes in an append-only event log and can enforce contracts at system boundaries, supporting replay, retries, and audit. Swimlanes make trust boundaries explicit, separating verified logic from external systems, human judgment, and AI decisions.
  A year-long pilot across three clinical sites executed 8,728 cohort-enrolled workflow runs with a 97.08% completion rate under an early prototype without the verified-core subsystem; observed failures were localized primarily to external integrations. The formal semantics and proof-checked admission model described here are specified and under active development. Evaluation of the verified core is reserved for future work.

</details>


### [111] [Small, Private Language Models as Teammates for Educational Assessment Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15015)
*Chris Davis Jaldi,Anmol Saini,Shan Zhang,Noah Schroeder,Cogan Shimizu,Eleni Ilkou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过对比大模型与小模型在教育评估中的表现，证明了小模型在隐私敏感场景下的可行性，但指出模型自动评估与专家评价之间存在偏差，强调了教育生成任务中“人在回路”的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注大型专有模型，缺乏对生成质量及教育场景落地限制的系统性考察；同时，小语言模型（SLM）在教育评估中的有效性及隐私优势尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 通过对比实验系统评估大语言模型（LLM）和小语言模型（SLM）在基于布鲁姆分类法（Bloom's taxonomy）生成评估题目的质量；采用可重复、基于教学法的评价指标；并对比模型自动评估与专家评估的一致性与可靠性。

Result: SLM在关键教学质量维度表现出极具竞争力的性能；模型自动评价与专家评价之间存在系统性不一致和偏见。

Conclusion: 语言模型在教育评估任务中可作为“有界辅助（bounded assistants）”，必须引入“人在回路（Human-in-the-Loop）”机制以监控评估偏差；研究验证了小语言模型在保护隐私与资源受限场景下具备与大型模型竞争的潜力。

Abstract: Generative AI increasingly supports educational design tasks, e.g., through Large Language Models (LLMs), demonstrating the capability to design assessment questions that are aligned with pedagogical frameworks (e.g., Bloom's taxonomy). However, they often rely on subjective or limited evaluation methods; focus primarily on proprietary models; or rarely systematically examine generation, evaluation, or deployment constraints in real educational settings. Meanwhile, Small Language Models (SLMs) have emerged as local alternatives that better address privacy and resource limitations; yet their effectiveness for assessment tasks remains underexplored. To address this gap, we systematically compare LLMs and SLMs for assessment question design; evaluate generation quality across Bloom's taxonomy levels using reproducible, pedagogically grounded metrics; and further assess model-based judging against expert-informed evaluation by analyzing reliability and agreement patterns. Results show that SLMs achieve competitive performance across key pedagogically motivated quality dimensions while enabling local, privacy-sensitive deployment. However, model-based evaluations also exhibit systematic inconsistencies and bias relative to expert ratings. These findings provide evidence to posit language models as bounded assistants in assessment workflows; underscore the necessity of Human-in-the-Loop; and advance the automated educational question generation field by examining quality, reliability, and deployment-aware trade-offs.

</details>


### [112] [Orchard: An Open-Source Agentic Modeling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15040)
*Baolin Peng,Wenlin Yao,Qianhui Wu,Hao Cheng,Xiao Yu,Rui Yang,Tao Ge,Alessandrio Sordoni,Xingdi Yuan,Yelong Shen,Pengcheng He,Tong Zhang,Zhou Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Orchard 是一个开源智能体建模框架，通过引入轻量级环境层 Orchard Env，在编码、GUI 操作和个人助理任务中验证了高效的训练配方，使开源智能体在性能上具备了与专有系统竞争的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源智能体框架多关注编排和评估，缺乏可扩展的智能体训练方法；同时高水平智能体多依赖于封闭系统，限制了开源领域的研究与创新。

Method: 1. 核心架构：Orchard Env，一种用于沙箱生命周期管理的轻量级环境服务。<br>2. 关键方法：<br>   - Orchard-SWE: 提出信用分配 SFT（学习未解决轨迹中的生产性片段）和平衡自适应回放（BAR）强化学习。<br>   - Orchard-GUI: 利用少量蒸馏轨迹和开放式任务训练视觉语言智能体。<br>   - Orchard-Claw: 使用极少量合成任务训练个人助理智能体。

Result: 1. Orchard-SWE：在 SWE-bench Verified 上达到 67.5% 的成功率，刷新了同等规模开源模型记录。<br>2. Orchard-GUI：在多个网页浏览器基准测试中表现最优，成为目前最强的开源计算机操作模型。<br>3. Orchard-Claw：仅用 0.2K 合成数据即在个人助理评估中表现优异。

Conclusion: Orchard 通过构建轻量级、与编排框架无关的环境层（Orchard Env），证明了即使在有限的数据和计算规模下，通过合理的训练配方（SFT+RL、信用分配等），开源模型也能在编码、桌面操作和个人助理任务上达到甚至超过专有系统的性能。

Abstract: Agentic modeling aims to transform LLMs into autonomous agents capable of solving complex tasks through planning, reasoning, tool use, and multi-turn interaction with environments. Despite major investment, open research remains constrained by infrastructure and training gaps. Many high-performing systems rely on proprietary codebases, models, or services, while most open-source frameworks focus on orchestration and evaluation rather than scalable agent training. We present Orchard, an open-source framework for scalable agentic modeling. At its core is Orchard Env, a lightweight environment service providing reusable primitives for sandbox lifecycle management across task domains, agent harnesses, and pipeline stages. On top of Orchard Env, we build three agentic modeling recipes. Orchard-SWE targets coding agents. We distill 107K trajectories from MiniMax-M2.5 and Qwen3.5-397B, introduce credit-assignment SFT to learn from productive segments of unresolved trajectories, and apply Balanced Adaptive Rollout for RL. Starting from Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking, Orchard-SWE achieves 64.3% on SWE-bench Verified after SFT and 67.5% after SFT+RL, setting a new state of the art among open-source models of comparable size. Orchard-GUI trains a 4B vision-language computer-use agent using only 0.4K distilled trajectories and 2.2K open-ended tasks. It achieves 74.1%, 67.0%, and 64.0% success rates on WebVoyager, Online-Mind2Web, and DeepShop, respectively, making it the strongest open-source model while remaining competitive with proprietary systems. Orchard-Claw targets personal assistant agents. Trained with only 0.2K synthetic tasks, it achieves 59.6% pass@3 on Claw-Eval and 73.9% when paired with a stronger ZeroClaw harness. Collectively, these results show that a lightweight, open, harness-agnostic environment layer enables reusable agentic data, training recipes, and evaluations across domains.

</details>


### [113] [Learning Developmental Scaffoldings to Guide Self-Organisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14998)
*Milton L. Montero,Elias Najarro,Jakob Schauser,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过结合神经元胞自动机和SIREN，证明了生物发育中“初始条件（记忆）”与“自组织规则（计算）”的协同优化能够显著提升复杂体系生成和演化的效能与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物发育中的复杂组织不仅依赖自组织，还高度依赖存储于初始条件中的预先存在的对称性破缺信息（母本预模式）。现有研究常忽略了这种“信息卸载”现象，作者旨在揭示并模型化初始条件与自组织动力学之间的这种权衡关系。

Method: 提出了一种联合学习框架，将神经元胞自动机（NCA，负责自组织规则）与基于坐标的模式生成器（SIREN，负责预模式/初始条件）结合。通过信息论分析量化预模式与自组织过程之间的信息分配，并在受控环境下进行训练与评估。

Result: 协同学习预模式与自组织规则显著提升了系统对目标模式生成的稳健性、编码容量及对称性破缺效率；预模式并非简单地逼近目标，而是通过偏置动力学过程来显著加速系统的收敛。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了初始条件（预模式）与自组织动力学之间深刻的非平凡关系，证明了通过协同优化这两者，系统能够实现更高的稳定性、编码容量和对称性破缺能力，从而在复杂形态发生中实现最优的“记忆-计算”权衡。

Abstract: From subcellular structures to entire organisms, many natural systems generate complex organisation through self-organisation: local interactions that collectively give rise to global structure without any blueprint of the outcome. Yet a significant portion of the information driving such processes is not produced by self-organisation itself, instead, it is often offloaded to initial conditions of the system. Biological development is a prime example, where maternal pre-patterns encode positional and symmetry-breaking information that scaffolds the self-organising process. From maternal morphogen gradients in early embryogenesis to tissue-level morphogenetic pre-patterns guiding organ formation, this transfer of information to initial conditions, analogous to a memory-compute trade-off in computational systems, is a fundamental part of developmental processes. In this work, we study this offloading phenomenon by introducing a model that jointly learns both the self-organisation rules and the pre-patterns, allowing their interplay to be varied and measured under controlled conditions: a Neural Cellular Automaton (NCA) paired with a learned coordinate-based pattern generator (SIREN), both trained simultaneously to generate a set of patterns. We provide information-theoretic analyses of how information is distributed between pre-patterns and the self-organising process, and show that jointly learning both components yields improvements in robustness, encoding capacity, and symmetry breaking over purely self-organising alternatives. Our analysis further suggests that effective pre-patterns do not simply approximate their targets; rather, they bias the developmental dynamics in ways that facilitate convergence, pointing to a non-trivial relationship between the structure of initial conditions and the dynamics of self-organisation.

</details>


### [114] [Case-Based Calibration of Adaptive Reasoning and Execution for LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15041)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Piao Tong,Sheng Cao,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAST是一种通过利用历史案例来优化工具调用的框架，它通过分析案例提取复杂度与失败画像，指导模型进行自适应推理，从而在提升准确率的同时降低了不必要的推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在工具使用中难以平衡“推理深度”与“结构有效性”，传统方法往往难以精准匹配任务要求，导致冗长推理或结构化错误。

Method: 提出了CAST框架：将历史执行轨迹结构化为案例；通过提取案例派生信号，识别任务复杂度配置与失败风险模式；将这些知识转化为细粒度的强化学习奖励设计，以支持模型进行自适应推理。

Result: 在BFCLv2和ToolBench基准测试中，CAST将整体执行准确率提升了5.85个百分点，平均推理长度减少了26%，并显著降低了高影响力的结构性错误。

Conclusion: CAST框架证明了历史执行案例可为大语言模型的工具调用提供可复用的适应性知识，通过案例驱动的策略能有效平衡推理深度与执行准确性。

Abstract: Tool use extends large language models beyond parametric knowledge, but reliable execution requires balancing appropriate reasoning depth with strict structural validity. We approach this problem from a case-based perspective to present CAST, a case-driven framework that treats historical execution trajectories as structured cases. Instead of reusing raw exemplar outputs, CAST extracts case-derived signals to identify complexity profiles for estimating optimal reasoning strategies, alongside failure profiles to map likely structural breakdowns. The framework translates this knowledge into a fine-grained reward design and adaptive reasoning, enabling the model to autonomously internalize case-based strategies during reinforcement learning. Experiments on BFCLv2 and ToolBench demonstrate that CAST improves both schema-faithful execution and task-level tool-use success while reducing unnecessary deliberation. The approach achieves up to 5.85 percentage points gain in overall execution accuracy and reduces average reasoning length by 26%, significantly mitigating high-impact structural errors. Ultimately, this demonstrates how historical execution cases can provide reusable adaptation knowledge for calibrated tool use.

</details>


### [115] [Dual-Dimensional Consistency: Balancing Budget and Quality in Adaptive Inference-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15100)
*Rongman Xu,Yifei Li,Tianzhe Zhao,Yanrui Wu,Bo Li,Hang Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DDC 是一种新型 LLM 推理框架，通过耦合置信度评估与趋势感知剪枝，有效解决了推理时扩展中计算效率与逻辑质量的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时扩展策略在采样广度（Width）与深度（Depth）的权衡上存在效率缺陷，广度共识易放大幻觉，深度剪枝则可能过早截断有效的复杂推理路径。

Method: 提出了一种双维一致性（DDC）框架，包含两个核心组件：1. 置信度加权贝叶斯协议（Confidence-Weighted Bayesian protocol），用于评估路径质量；2. 趋势感知分层剪枝（Trend-Aware Stratified Pruning），用于实现推理路径的动态终止与优化。

Result: 在五个权威基准测试中，DDC 在保持或超越现有强基线模型精度的前提下，将 Token 消耗量减少了 10 倍以上。核心能力在于聚焦高价值路径、过滤幻觉并加速共识形成。

Conclusion: DDC 框架通过将路径质量评估与自适应停止策略有机结合，在大幅降低推理计算成本（Token 消耗减少 10 倍以上）的同时，保证了推理的高准确性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable abilities in reasoning. However, maximizing their potential through inference-time scaling faces challenges in trade-off between sampling budget and reasoning quality. Current strategies remain inefficient as they typically treat sampling width and depth as orthogonal objectives, where width consensus methods risk reinforcing hallucinations, while depth pruning mechanisms prematurely truncate complex yet valid reasoning chains. Therefore, we propose Dual-Dimensional Consistency (DDC), a unified framework that bridges path quality with adaptive termination. By coupling Confidence-Weighted Bayesian protocol with a Trend-Aware Stratified Pruning, our method ensures that computational resources are concentrated on high quality reasoning paths, filtering hallucinations while accelerating consensus. Evaluations across five benchmarks demonstrate that this approach reduces token consumption by over 10 times while maintaining or exceeding the accuracy of strong baselines across various LLMs.

</details>


### [116] [OpenDeepThink: Parallel Reasoning via Bradley--Terry Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15177)
*Shang Zhou,Wenhao Chai,Kaiyuan Liu,Huanzhi Mao,Qiuyang Mang,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为了解决 LLM 测试时推理中候选解难以准确评估的问题，OpenDeepThink 引入了一种基于群体进化和成对 Bradley-Terry 比较的评估与优化算法，显著提升了模型在代码竞赛等硬推理任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 推理扩展主要集中于单条链路的深度扩展，而广度扩展（多采样）受限于缺乏高质量的验证器来选择最优解。单点评分方法噪声大且存在偏见，因此需要一种更可靠的候选策略与优化方法。

Method: 提出了一种名为 OpenDeepThink 的框架，通过“成对比较-Bradley-Terry 聚合-进化变异”的循环机制进行测试时推理。具体包括：1. 给定问题生成多个候选项；2. 使用 LLM 进行成对比较并生成自然语言批评；3. 利用 Bradley-Terry 模型聚合投票结果进行排序；4. 保留高分候选并通过批评进行变异，淘汰低分候选。

Result: 在 Gemini 3.1 Pro 上，该方法在 Codeforces 上的 Elo 分数提升了 +405 分；在多领域 HLE 基准测试中，在客观可验证领域表现出色；发布了包含 73 个专家级标注问题的 CF-73 数据集。

Conclusion: OpenDeepThink 是一种有效的基于种群的测试时计算框架，通过成对比较和进化策略显著提升了 LLM 在复杂推理任务中的性能。该框架在客观可验证领域效果显著，且具有良好的模型可迁移性。

Abstract: Test-time compute scaling is a primary axis for improving LLM reasoning. Existing methods primarily scale depth by extending a single reasoning trace. Scaling breadth by sampling multiple candidates in parallel is straightforward, but introduces a selection bottleneck: choosing the best candidate without a ground-truth verifier, since pointwise LLM judging is noisy and biased. To address this, we introduce OpenDeepThink, a population-based test-time compute framework that selects via pairwise Bradley-Terry comparison. Each generation, the LLM judges random pairs of candidates and aggregates votes via Bradley-Terry into a global ranking; top-ranked candidates are preserved and the top three quarters are mutated using the natural-language critiques produced during comparison; the bottom quarter is discarded. OpenDeepThink raises Gemini 3.1 Pro's effective Codeforces Elo by +405 points in eight sequential LLM-call rounds (~27 minutes wall-clock). The pipeline transfers across weaker and stronger models without retuning, and on the multi-domain HLE benchmark, gains appear concentrated in objectively verifiable domains and reverse in subjective ones. We release CF-73, a curated set of 73 expert-rated Codeforces problems with International Grandmaster annotation and 99% local-evaluation agreement against the official verdict.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [SemaTune: Semantic-Aware Online OS Tuning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15026)
*Georgios Liargkovas,Mihir Nitin Joshi,Hubertus Franke,Kostis Kaffes*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: SemaTune 是一种利用 LLM 指导的操作系统参数调优框架，通过引入语义约束和分层验证机制，在保障系统稳定性的前提下，显著提升了长期运行服务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 OS 调优工具倾向于将系统参数视为黑盒，缺乏对参数语义的理解，且依赖单一的标量奖励，在缺乏应用级反馈时容易导致性能退化且难以恢复。

Method: 提出名为 SemaTune 的主机侧框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）提供指导。该方法将内核参数配置、遥测数据、动作历史及先验知识转化为上下文；采用双重循环架构（快循环更新、慢循环策略修订）；引入类型化验证机制（Typed Validation）以确保参数修改的安全性，避免系统进入退化状态。

Result: 在 13 种真实工作负载和 41 个 Linux 参数测试中，SemaTune 的稳定阶段性能较默认设置提升了 72.5%，较最强非 LLM 基线提升了 153.3%；即便仅使用主机级指标，其表现也优于使用应用级指标的对比基线（领先 93.7 个百分点）。

Conclusion: SemaTune 通过引入语义感知的验证机制和分层控制策略，成功解决了黑盒 OS 调优在性能稳定性、安全性和指标依赖性方面的痛点，在保持低开销的同时大幅提升了长期运行服务的系统性能。

Abstract: Online OS tuning can improve long-running services, but existing controllers are poorly matched to live hosts. They treat scheduler, power, memory, and I/O controls as black-box variables and optimize a scalar reward. This view ignores cross-knob policy structure, breaks down when application metrics are unavailable, and can send a running service into degraded regions that persist after the bad setting is removed. We present SemaTune, a host-side framework for steady-state OS tuning with bounded language-model guidance. SemaTune turns knob schemas, telemetry, current configuration, recent action--response history, and retrieved prior runs into a compact decision context. A fast loop proposes low-latency updates, a slower loop periodically revises the search strategy, and every proposed change passes through typed validation before reaching kernel or sysctl interfaces. This lets the controller reason about OS-control meaning and indirect performance signals while keeping model cost, latency, and authority constrained. We evaluate SemaTune on 13 live workloads from five benchmark suites while tuning up to 41 Linux parameters. Across the suite, SemaTune improves stable-phase performance by 72.5\% over default settings and by 153.3\% relative to the strongest non-LLM baseline. A 30-window session costs about \$0.20 in model calls. With only host-level metrics, SemaTune still outperforms baselines given direct application objectives by 93.7 percentage points, while avoiding severe degraded regions reached by structure-blind exploration.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Clustering with Locally Bounded Ignorance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13917)
*Jaroslav Garvardt,Christian Komusiewicz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了模糊边图结构对相关聚类（Correlation Clustering）问题参数化复杂度的影响，证明了在模糊边图的退化度或闭包作为额外参数时，该问题承认多项式核，并分析了该问题在受限图结构下的计算硬度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关聚类问题（Correlation Clustering）在一般参数 k 下不存在多项式核（假定 FPT），因此研究引入模糊边图的特定结构属性是否能使其在参数化复杂度上获得更好的处理结果（如多项式核）。

Method: 研究模糊边图（权重为0的边构成的图）的结构属性；结合参数化复杂度理论，分析以 k+d（k+退化度）和 k+c（k+闭包）为参数时的核心化（kernelization），并证明相关受限图结构下的计算硬度。

Result: 当参数为 k+d（模糊边图的退化度）或 k+c（模糊边图的闭包）时，相关聚类问题承认多项式核；同时证明了在多种高度受限的图结构设定中，该问题仍具有复杂性（Hardness）。

Conclusion: 相关聚类问题在模糊边图结构受限的情况下（如退化度或闭包受限）具有更优的参数算法复杂度，且存在多项式核，但在更一般的限制条件下仍保持计算难度。

Abstract: In Correlation Clustering, the input is a graph $G=(V,E)$ with weight function $ω: {V \choose 2}\to Z$
  and the task is to partition the vertex set into clusters such that
  the total weight of edges between clusters and missing edges
  inside clusters is minimized. Due to close connections
  between Correlation Clustering and Edge Multicut,
  deciding whether there is a partition with total cost at most $k$ is
  FPT with respect to $k$ but a polynomial kernel is presumably
  impossible. We study the influence of the structure of the fuzzy
  edge graph, that is, the graph induced by the weight-0 edges, on the
  problem complexity. We show in particular that Correlation
  Clustering admits a polynomial problem kernel when parameterized
  by $k+d$, where $d$ is the degeneracy of the fuzzy edge graph, and when
  parameterized by $k+c$, where $c$ is the closure of the fuzzy edge
  graph. We complement these positive results by showing hardness for
  several settings where the graph induced by the edges and nonedges has very restricted structure.

</details>


### [119] [Non-Redundancy of Low-Arity Symmetric Boolean CSPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14007)
*Amatya Sharma,Santhoshini Velusamy*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了对称布尔CSP的非冗余度（NRD），通过引入t-平衡性及利用复杂性约筒框架，几乎完全分类了元数至多为5的所有对称布尔谓词的NRD渐近增长情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非冗余度（Non-redundancy）是衡量约束满足问题（CSP）核化与复杂性的关键结构参数，尽管在大类上有所研究，但对于具体布尔对称谓词的渐近行为缺乏系统性分类。本研究旨在填补这一空白，刻画元数≤5的对称布尔谓词的NRD增长规律。

Method: 1. 理论分析：引入“t-平衡性”（t-balancedness），证明其等价于存在捕捉关系R的度为t的多线性多项式，从而推导出NRD上界。
2. 下界构造：利用Carbonnel的约简框架，将k-元OR的下界推广至特定谓词。
3. 实验验证：结合计算实验与代数判据，对元数≤4及部分元数=5的谓词进行逐一分析。

Result: 完成了算数≤4的所有谓词以及算数=5中绝大多数谓词的NRD渐近分类。证明了t-平衡性意味着NRD为O(n^t)，并确定了元数5中剩余两个未解谓词的上下界范围（Ω(n^2)及O(n^3)），将其归约为极值集合系统问题。

Conclusion: 研究通过引入t-平衡性概念和利用约简框架，成功阐明了几乎所有元数不超过5的对称布尔谓词的非冗余度（NRD）的渐近增长情况，为理解CSP的核化与复杂性提供了结构化分类。

Abstract: Non-redundancy, introduced by Bessiere, Carbonnel, and Katsirelos (AAAI 2020), is a structural parameter for Constraint Satisfaction Problems ($\mathsf{CSPs}$) that governs kernelization, exact and approximate sparsification, and exact streaming complexity. It is the largest size of a $\mathsf{CSP}$ instance admitting no smaller subinstance with the same satisfying assignments.
  We study non-redundancy $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)$ for Boolean symmetric $\mathsf{CSPs}$ defined by an $r$-ary relation $R$ whose value depends only on Hamming weight. An instance of $\mathsf{CSP}(R)$ has $n$ variables and constraints given by $r$-tuples; a constraint is satisfied exactly when the induced tuple lies in $R$. This class includes natural predicates such as cuts and $k$-SAT clauses. Our main result is a near-complete classification of the asymptotic growth of $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)$ for symmetric Boolean predicates of arity at most $5$. Using computational experiments and algebraic upper- and lower-bound criteria, we resolve every predicate of arity at most $4$ and all but two predicates of arity $5$.
  For upper bounds, we introduce $t$-balancedness, a lifted, higher-degree version of the balancedness notion of Chen, Jansen, and Pieterse (Algorithmica 2020). We prove that $t$-balancedness is equivalent to the existence of degree-$t$ multilinear polynomials capturing $R$, and hence implies $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)=O(n^t)$. For lower bounds, we use Carbonnel's (CP 2022) framework: predicates admitting a special reduction from $k$-ary OR inherit OR's lower bound $Ω(n^k)$. The only unresolved arity-$5$ predicates in our framework have bounds $Ω(n^2)$ and $O(n^3)$; we reduce their exact classification to natural extremal set-system questions.

</details>


### [120] [New Algorithms for Parity-SAT and Its Bounded-Occurrence Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14093)
*Sanjay Jain,Junqiang Peng,Frank Stephan,Haoyun Tang,Mingyu Xiao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究Parity-SAT问题，通过引入变量出现次数受限（d-occ）的结构化约束，开发了一系列多项式空间的算法，成功突破了经典指数时间门槛，并展示了奇偶性在求解效率上优于精确模型计数的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Parity-SAT作为 $\oplus$P-完全问题，根据强指数时间假设（SETH），在变量数 $n$ 或子句数 $m$ 的指数级别上难以突破 $2^{\Omega(n)}$ 或 $2^{\Omega(m)}$ 的界限。研究旨在探索如何通过变量出现次数等结构限制和深度利用奇偶性逻辑来规避这一限制。

Method: 1. 针对Parity-d-occ-SAT问题，设计了基于随机化的 $O^*(2^{m(1-1/O(d))})$ 时间复杂度算法；2. 针对 $d=2$ 的特殊情况，开发了基于分支定界（branching）的优化算法；3. 将 $d=2$ 的结构见解推广，提出了基于公式长度 $L$ 的 $O^*(1.1052^L)$ 时间复杂度算法。所有算法均采用多项式空间复杂度。

Result: 1. 对于固定的 $d$，成功打破了 $2^m$ 的算法门槛；2. 给出了 $d=2$ 时 $O^*(1.1193^n)$ 或 $O^*(1.3248^m)$ 的优异性能；3. 获得了通用Parity-SAT在公式长度 $L$ 下的 $O^*(1.1052^L)$ 算法。这些结果在时间复杂度上全面超越了对应的精确模型计数算法。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用结构约束（如变量受限 $d$）和奇偶性（parity）的特性，可以显著提升针对Parity-SAT问题的求解效率，突破传统的指数级算法壁垒，且其表现优于等价的精确模型计数算法。

Abstract: Parity-SAT is the problem of determining whether a given CNF formula has an odd number of satisfying assignments. As a canonical $\oplus$P-complete problem, it represents a fundamental variant of the exact model counting problem (#SAT). Under the Strong Exponential Time Hypothesis (SETH), Parity-SAT admits no $O^*((2-\varepsilon)^n)$-time or $O^*((2-\varepsilon)^m)$-time algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon>0$, where $n$ and $m$ denote the numbers of variables and clauses, respectively. Thus, breaking the $2^n$ or $2^m$ barrier appears impossible in full generality.
  In this work, we revisit this barrier through structural restrictions and a refined exploitation of parity. We study Parity-$d$-occ-SAT, where each variable appears in at most $d$ clauses, and obtain three main results. First, we design {a randomized} $O^*(2^{m(1-1/O(d))})$-time algorithm, thereby breaking the $2^m$ barrier for every fixed $d$. Second, for the special case $d=2$, we develop a significantly sharper branching algorithm running in $O^*(1.1193^n)$ time or $O^*(1.3248^m)$ time. Third, leveraging the structural insights underlying the $d=2$ case, we obtain an $O^*(1.1052^L)$-time algorithm for general Parity-SAT, where $L$ denotes the formula length. All algorithms use only polynomial space. Notably, our running-time bounds are better than the best known bounds for the corresponding exact counting counterparts, highlighting a genuine algorithmic advantage of parity over counting. Conceptually, our results demonstrate that parity admits finer structural reductions and more efficient branching than exact model counting, and that bounded occurrence can be systematically leveraged to circumvent classical exponential barriers.

</details>


### [121] [Fast Leaf-to-Ancestor Minimum Query in the Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14112)
*Aleksey Upirvitskiy,Aleksandr Levin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对树上叶子到祖先的路径最小值查询问题，设计了一种在 O(n log h) 预处理后，能实现 O(1) 恒定时间查询的静态数据结构，且在比较模型下该方法达到了最优的预处理复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预言机比较模型下，解决有权根树中从叶子到祖先路径的最小值查询问题，并追求查询时的最优性能。

Method: 结合了四种核心技术：(I) 将边权转换为节点权并结合确定性平局决胜机制构建全序；(II) 长路径分解 (Ladder Decomposition)；(III) 二进制提升 (Binary Lifting)；(IV) 基于长路径数组构建稀疏表 (Sparse-Table) 进行范围最小值查询 (RMQ)，并在预处理阶段预先存储比较结果。

Result: 实现了预处理复杂度为 O(n log h)，查询复杂度为 O(1) 的静态数据结构，其中 n 为节点数，h 为树高。查询阶段不需要进行任何额外的比较操作。

Conclusion: 本文提出的数据结构在确定性比较模型下，实现了预处理开销（时间、空间、预言机调用）与查询效率的最佳权衡，且预处理阶段的预言机调用次数下界已达到理论最优。

Abstract: We study leaf-to-ancestor path-minimum queries on a rooted, weighted tree in the oracle model, where the only allowed value operation is a comparison oracle on edge (or node) weights. We give a static data structure that, after O(n log h) preprocessing time, space, and oracle calls (where $n$ is the number of nodes and $h$ is the tree height), answers any leaf-to-ancestor query in O(1) worst-case time with zero oracle calls at query time. The method combines (I) an edge-to-node weight conversion with a deterministic tie-break to obtain a total order; (II) ladder (longest-path) decomposition; (III) binary lifting; and (IV) sparse-table RMQ built over ladder arrays, storing indices selected via the oracle during preprocessing. We also show that the preprocessing oracle-comparison bound is tight in the deterministic comparison model.

</details>


### [122] [Stochastic Matching via Local Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14195)
*Sara Ahmadian,Edith Cohen,Mohammad Roghani*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对去中心化系统通信带宽受限的问题，论文提出了两阶段局部稀疏化框架，通过在全局优化前限制每个请求的匹配候选集规模，实现了接近最优的匹配效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线随机匹配问题假设即时决策，但现代去中心化系统（如网约车、分布式计算）的主要瓶颈在于局部通信带宽，而非匹配的时机。需要建立一个在局部信息约束和全局匹配效率之间的中间模型。

Method: 提出了一种由预期实例分数解参数化的局部选择策略（Local Selection Strategy），通过量化解的“传播度（Spread）”来分析近似比，并采用该策略对实时到达的请求进行边缘数限制（修剪为k条边）。

Result: 理论上证明了在充分的“传播度”下，该稀疏化方法能维持最大匹配的期望大小；实证研究表明在纽约市网约车数据集和对抗性合成基准上，该方法显著优于标准的在线匹配基线。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的两阶段局部稀疏化（Local Sparsification）框架成功在局部通信限制与全局优化效用之间取得了平衡，证明了即使在极度受限的局部预算（k）下，通过合理的选择策略依然能实现接近最优的全局匹配。

Abstract: The classic online stochastic matching problem typically requires immediate and irrevocable matching decisions. However, in many modern decentralized systems such as real-time ride-hailing and distributed cloud computing, the primary bottleneck is often local communication bandwidth rather than the timing of the match itself. We formalize this challenge by introducing a two-stage local sparsification framework. In this setting, arriving requests must prune their realized compatibility sets to a strict budget of $k$ edges before a central coordinator optimizes the global matching. This creates a "middle ground" between local information constraints and global optimization utility.
  We propose a local selection strategy, parametrized by a fractional solution of the expected instance. Theoretically, we quantify the approximation ratio as a function of the solution's {\em spread}. We prove that under sufficient spread, our sparsifier globally preserves the expected size of the maximum matching. Empirically, we demonstrate the robustness of our approach using the New York City ride-hailing datasets and adversarial synthetic benchmarks. Our results show that near-optimal global matching is achievable even with highly constrained local budgets, significantly outperforming standard online baselines.

</details>


### [123] [Hybrid Sketching Methods for Dynamic Connectivity on Sparse Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15173)
*Quinten De Man,Gilvir Gill,Michael A. Bender,Laxman Dhulipala,David Tench*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对动态连通性问题，提出HybridSCALE混合策略，结合高效的BalloonSketch，在保持理论性能的同时，首次实现了在现实图结构中较无损方案更优的空间效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于草图的动态连通性算法虽然在极稠密图上具有优异的理论空间复杂度（Θ(V log² V)），但由于单个定点草图开销过大（数千字节），在大多数现实世界的稀疏图中反而比传统的无损存储方法（Θ(V+E)）占用空间更多，缺乏实用性。

Method: 1. 混合草图策略（Hybrid Sketching）：仅对图中的稠密核心部分进行线性草图编码，对稀疏外围部分采用无损存储；2. BalloonSketch：一种新型的l0-采样算法，将每个顶点的草图大小减少了8倍；3. 构建HybridSCALE系统：将无损存储与草图算法集成，根据图的密度自适应调配存储方案。

Result: 在多种图密度下均表现优异：在稀疏图（平均度<100）上节省空间达15%，在中等密度图上节省高达92%，在稠密图上节省达97%；理论空间复杂度达到O(min{V+E, V log V log(2+E/V)})。

Conclusion: HybridSCALE是首个在实际稀疏及中等密度图上实现显著空间优化的基于草图的动态连通性系统，通过混合存储策略成功克服了传统草图算法在稀疏图中空间开销过大的瓶颈。

Abstract: Dynamic connectivity is a fundamental dynamic graph problem, and recent algorithmic breakthroughs on dynamic graph sketching have reshaped what is theoretically possible: by encoding the graph as per-vertex linear sketches, these algorithms solve dynamic connectivity in only $Θ(V \log^2 V)$ space, independent of the number of edges,outperforming lossless $Θ(V+E)$-space structures that grow as the graph becomes denser. Prior to this work, no practical dynamic connectivity algorithm has been able to translate these theoretical breakthroughs into space savings on real-world graphs. The main obstacle is that per-vertex sketches cost thousands of bytes per vertex, so sketching only pays off once the graph becomes extremely dense.
  We observe that sparse real-world graphs are often not uniformly sparse, these graphs can contain dense cores on a small subset of vertices that account for a large fraction of edges. We exploit this structure via hybrid sketching: sketch only the dense core, and store the sparse periphery losslessly.
  We design new hybrid algorithms for fully-dynamic and semi-streaming connectivity with space $O(\min\{V+E, V \log V \log(2+E/V)\})$ w.h.p., simultaneously matching the lossless bound on sparse graphs, the sketching bound on dense graphs, and improving on both in an intermediate regime. A key component is BalloonSketch, a new l0-sampler reducing per-vertex sketch sizes by up to 8x. We implement HybridSCALE, a modular system treating the lossless and sketch-based components as subroutines. HybridSCALE is the first sketch-based dynamic connectivity system to save space on common real-world graphs. Compared to the state-of-the-art lossless baseline, HybridSCALE saves up to 15% space on sparse graphs (average degree < 100), up to 92% on intermediate density graphs (average degree ~ 100-1000), and up to 97% on dense graphs (average degree > 1000).

</details>


### [124] [Semi-Streaming Algorithms for Submodular Maximization under Random Arrival Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14296)
*Niv Buchbinder,Moran Feldman,Siyue Liu,Sherry Sarkar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机序下的亚线性空间次模函数最大化问题，通过提出两种新的技术工具，在多种组合约束下取得了比对抗序环境更好的近似结果，并大幅提升了受约束拟阵优化的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决次模函数最大化问题在半流模型下的局限性，特别是通过利用随机序信息来克服对抗序下已知的界限，并实现在处理复杂组合约束时的性能提升。

Method: 1. 开发了一种将离线算法转换为随机序半流算法的通用框架；2. 设计了一种针对拟阵约束的离线算法的半流变体；3. 结合这些技术工具针对不同约束类（拟阵、p-系统等）构建特定算法。

Result: 1. 在多种通用约束类（如拟阵、$p$-交换系统、$p$-系统等）上取得了优于对抗序环境的结果；2. 在拟阵约束下，将获得 $1 - 1/e - \varepsilon$ 近似所需的趟数（passes）呈指数级降低；3. 给出了针对 $p$-系统随机序与对抗序之间存在性能差距的硬度证明。

Conclusion: 本文通过两个关键技术工具，在随机序半流（random order semi-streaming）环境下显著改善了次模函数最大化在多种组合约束下的近似界限，并证明了随机序与对抗序在处理此类问题时的性能鸿沟。

Abstract: We study random order semi-streaming algorithms for submodular maximization under a wide range of combinatorial constraint classes, including matroids, matroid $p$-parity, $p$-exchange systems and $p$-systems. For most of these classes of constraints, our results are the first improvement over what is known to be achievable for adversarial order. For matroids, matching and $p$-matchoids, previous random order results were known, and we improve over some of these as well. In the case of matroids, our improved results show a separation between adversarial and random order semi-streaming algorithms, and exponentially improve the number of passes necessary for getting $1 - 1/e - \varepsilon$ approximation for maximizing a monotone submodular function subject to a matroid constraint. We also prove a new hardness result showing a similar separation for $p$-systems. Our results are based on two new technical tools. One tool provides a general way to translate offline algorithms for many classes of constraints into random order semi-streaming algorithms. The other tool is a semi-streaming variant of a recently proposed offline algorithm for matroid constraints.

</details>


### [125] [zSort: Stable Distribution Sort using Z-Score Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14419)
*Hriday Jain,Ketan Sabale,Aditya Shastri,Hiren Kumar Thakkar,Ashutosh Londhe*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 zSort 的自适应稳定排序算法，通过消除“稳定性税”，在保持算法稳定性的同时，实现了与高性能非稳定排序算法相当的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排序算法存在“稳定性税”（Stability Tax）问题，即从业者必须在保留顺序的稳定性与高吞吐性能或执行速度之间进行权衡。

Method: 提出了一种基于自适应 z-score 的分布排序算法（zSort），该算法在保证稳定性的同时，避免了随键值宽度线性增加的遍历复杂度。

Result: zSort 在微架构分析中表现出较低的错误预测开销（19.7%）和 1.44 的 IPC；在实验中比主流稳定排序算法快 3-4.5 倍，且在重重复数据和部分有序数据上表现优于 LSD 基数排序，性能可媲美高性能不稳定算法（如 Skasort）。

Conclusion: zSort 成功缩小了稳定排序与非稳定排序之间的性能差距，作为一种高效的稳定排序算法，能够满足现代高性能数据流水线的需求。

Abstract: Sorting is a foundational primitive in modern data processing, influencing the execution speed of high-performance data pipelines. However, the algorithmic landscape is currently bifurcated by a pervasive "Stability Tax": practitioners must sacrifice either order preservation for high throughput or execution speed for stability. To address these limitations, this paper introduces, zSort, an adaptive z-score based distribution sorting algorithm that guarantees stability while avoiding pass complexity that scales with key-width. The performance of the proposed technique is evaluated using Microarchitectural analysis and experimental results. Microarchitectural analysis shows that zSort achieves a lower bad-speculation overhead (19.7%) than both stable baselines and several high-performance unstable algorithms and sustains a competitive IPC of 1.44. Empirical evaluation across diverse input distributions and datasets of up to 10^7 elements (64 bit) demonstrates that zSort consistently outperforms widely used comparison based stable sorting algorithms, achieving up to 3x-4.5x speedups, and a relatively better performance compared to LSD Radix, with larger gains on duplicate heavy and partially ordered inputs. Despite providing stability, zSort achieves comparable throughput as compared to high-performance unstable algorithms such as Skasort. It also maintains this performance on adaptive workloads where methods like Pdqsort typically excel and doesn't exhibit any extreme worst case. These results indicate that zSort substantially narrows the traditional performance gap between stable and unstable sorting and provides an efficient, stable sorting alternative.

</details>


### [126] [Branch-width of represented matroids in matrix multiplication time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14428)
*Mujin Choi,Tuukka Korhonen,Sang-il Oum*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的拟阵分支宽度算法，在有限域上实现了 $O(n^ω)$ 的复杂度，打破了该问题长期陷于 $\Omega(n^3)$ 的瓶颈，并证实了该复杂度在矩阵乘法假设下是理论最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算拟阵分支宽度（branch-width）的算法时间复杂度均为 $\Omega(n^3)$ 量级，未能有效利用矩阵乘法的加速机制。作者旨在通过更精细的算法设计，实现更高效的计算（特别是逼近 $O(n^ω)$ 的线性复杂度指标）。

Method: 提出了一种能在 $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2) + O(n^ω)$ 时间内计算拟阵分支宽度的算法。该方法依赖于将矩阵转化为标准型，利用矩阵乘法（Exponent $\omega < 2.3714$）来优化计算过程，并将其扩展至有向图的列宽（rank-width）和拟阵路径宽度（path-width）的计算。

Result: 相比以往的最优算法，本算法在通用矩阵表示下实现了更低的时间复杂度；若输入矩阵已为标准型，则时间复杂度进一步降低至 $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)$。实验结果证明了该方法在有向图列宽等相关问题上的应用优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法（在有限域下判断拟阵分支宽度的算法）在 $O(n^ω)$ 的运算复杂度下达到了理论最优性。此外，本文证明了分支宽度计算的复杂度下界与矩阵奇异性测试一致，支持了算法的最优性结论。

Abstract: For an $n$-element matroid $M$ given by an $n \times n$ matrix representation over a finite field $\mathbb F$ and an integer $k$, we present an $(O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)+O(n^ω))$-time algorithm that either finds a branch-decomposition of $M$ of width at most $k$, or confirms that the branch-width of $M$ is more than $k$, where $ω< 2.3714$ is the matrix multiplication exponent, and the $O_{k,\mathbb F}(\cdot)$-notation hides factors that depend on $k$ and $\mathbb F$ in a computable manner. All previous algorithms including Hliněný and Oum [SIAM J. Comput. (2008)] and Jeong, Kim, and Oum [SIAM J. Discrete Math. (2021)] run in at least $Ω(n^3)$ time. Moreover, if the input matrix representation is given by a standard form, our algorithm runs in $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)$-time, since $O(n^ω)$-time is only needed for finding a standard form of the input matrix. When $M$ is given by an $m \times n$ matrix, the overhead for finding a standard form is $O(mn \min(m,n)^{ω-2})$.
  As corollaries, we obtain faster algorithms for rank-width of directed graphs and path-width of matroids represented over a fixed finite field. Furthermore, we also present an approximation algorithm for finding branch-width that works on infinite fields provided that the input matrix is of a standard form and contains a bounded number of distinct values of entries.
  To suggest that our algorithm is optimal, we observe that for every field $\mathbb F$, deciding whether the branch-width of a matroid represented over $\mathbb F$ is $0$ is as hard as deciding whether a square matrix over $\mathbb F$ is singular. Under the assumption that singularity testing requires $Ω(n^ω)$-time, this implies that the overhead of $O(n^ω)$ is unavoidable. We also show strengthenings of this observation to rule out some approximations under this assumption.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [A Hardware-Aware, Per-Layer Methodology for Post-Training Quantization of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14929)
*Earl Killian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOP 是一种适用于 LLM 的后训练量化方案，通过结合自适应码本、块缩放和名为 HIF 的新型硬件 LUT 格式，在显著降低位宽（4.5-6 bpw）的同时，实现了优于传统 8-bit 量化的重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有大模型权重量化过程中的精度损失问题，旨在实现 4.5--6 bits/weight (bpw) 下的近无损保真度，同时平衡硬件实现的性能、能效与成本。

Method: 提出 Scaled Outer Product (SOP) 方法，包含：1. 基于 per-layer 的码本搜索（支持固定码本如 NF4, BOF4 及 LUT 优化的 DD4）；2. 采用带符号的块缩放；3. 使用基于激活加权余弦相似度的算法选择；4. 引入多项选择背包算法进行敏感层提升，并结合离群值和稀疏残差校正；5. 设计高性能硬件 LUT 输出格式 (HIF)。

Result: 在六个开源模型系列中，SOP 的 FP6 配置（6.5 bpw）实现了比传统的 per-layer-POT FP8 (8.0 bpw) 更低的权重重建误差，且节省了 1.5 bpw 的存储空间。证明了块缩放小原子（block-scaled small atoms）可以有效替代常规的 FP8 量化。

Conclusion: SOP 通过结合块缩放、优化的码本选择以及硬件友好的 HIF 格式，证明了在 4.5-6 bpw 的压缩范围内，其性能可以优于传统的 8-bit 量化方案，为大模型的低位宽部署提供了高效、低误差的解决方案。

Abstract: Scaled Outer Product (SOP) is a post-training quantization methodology for large language model weights, designed to deliver near-lossless fidelity at 4.5--6 bits per weight on hardware with per-layer LUT decode. The methodology combines per-layer search of fixed and dynamic codebook pairs selected by a per-block selection bit, signed per-block scales, activation-weighted cosine selection, and multiple-choice knapsack promotion of sensitive layers with outlier and sparse-residual correction. Fixed codebooks include NF4, BOF4, Split87, and SH4; per-layer optimized codebooks (DD4) are hosted in LUT SRAM. A new hardware-efficient LUT output format (HIF) is proposed to improve performance, energy, and cost. Across six open model families, the recommended FP6 operating point (E2M3sUE4M4, 6.5 bpw) achieves lower weight reconstruction error than the conventional per-layer-POT FP8 baseline (E4M3, 8.0 bpw) at 1.5 bpw lower storage cost, demonstrating that block-scaled small atoms with carefully chosen scale precision can replace conventionally-deployed FP8. Full evaluation across the 4.5--6 bpw range, including layer promotion and sparse residual correction, is reported in a companion paper.

</details>


### [128] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于视觉环境下的ptSTL监控方法，通过确立“语义基”作为最优预测目标，实现了无需针对单一公式重新训练的通用化监控，并引入滚动预测器以灵活应对不同时段的安全验证需求，在真实自动驾驶数据中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的视觉环境下，实现高质量、可重用且具有有限样本保证的Past-Time Signal Temporal Logic (ptSTL) 运行时监控，以解决复杂场景下的安全验证问题。

Method: 1. 定义语义基（semantic basis）：即原子鲁棒性分数的向量，作为最小预测目标；2. 采用针对ptSTL的确定性解码器，通过共形预测（conformal prediction）进行一次性校准以实现无联合界（union bound）的认证；3. 提出一种针对短时域优化的滚动预测监控器（rolling prediction monitor）；4. 在Waymo数据集上进行实证评估。

Result: 在行人过马路基准测试中，滚动预测监控器在短时域表现更优，而语义基监控器在长时域内鲁棒性界限紧致度提升高达4倍；在Waymo真实数据中，两者均成功满足了共形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一种针对ptSTL的认证监控方法，证明了语义基（semantic basis）在可重用接口中的最优性，并对比了滚动预测监控器和语义基监控器在不同时段下的表现，验证了该方法在真实世界自动驾驶数据中的有效性与覆盖保证。

Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

</details>


### [129] [An Interpretable Latency Model for Speculative Decoding in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15051)
*Linghao Kong,Megan Flynn,Michael Peng,Nir Shavit,Mark Kurtz,Alexandre Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个旨在理解推测解码（SD）在生产服务器环境中性能表现的延迟模型，通过利用Little's Law和计算需求拆解，量化了不同负载下SD的加速瓶颈及参数配置的影响，为实际部署提供了理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于推测解码（Speculative Decoding）的研究多集中在隔离或固定批处理场景，缺乏对实际生产部署环境（负载动态变化、有效批处理大小不可控）下的系统化理解和性能预测能力。

Method: 1. 基于Little's Law从请求速率推导有效批处理大小；2. 将请求的计算需求拆解为与负载无关及负载相关的各部分（包含预填充、草稿生成、验证阶段）；3. 在vLLM中进行了广泛的实证测量，覆盖多种模型规模、序列长度、请求速率及硬件配置。

Result: 构建了一个简单且可解释的推理延迟模型；实验证实该模型能精确描述观察到的延迟，并解释了为何SD在重载下加速比会下降；验证了该框架在混合专家模型（MoE）中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个结构化的建模框架，揭示了推测解码（SD）在生产环境中的性能表现规律。研究表明，由于服务器负载的增加，SD带来的加速效果会边际递减；该模型能准确指导生产系统中超参数（如草稿长度、模型规模配置）的优化选择。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by using a smaller draft model to propose multiple tokens that are verified by a larger target model in parallel. While prior work demonstrates substantial speedups in isolated or fixed-batch settings, the behavior of SD in production serving systems remains poorly understood: request load varies over time, and effective batch size emerges from the serving system rather than being directly controlled or observed. In this work, we develop a simple and interpretable latency model for SD in LLM serving. We infer effective batch size from request rate using Little's Law and decompose per-request demand into load-independent and load-dependent components for prefill, drafting, and verification. We validate our model using extensive measurements from vLLM across verifier and drafter model sizes, prefill and decode lengths, request rates, draft lengths, and acceptance probabilities. The model accurately describes observed latency, explains why speedups often diminish as server load increases, and characterizes how draft length, acceptance rate, and verifier-drafter size shape latency across serving conditions, with implications for configuring SD in deployed systems. We further show how the framework extends to mixture of experts models, where sparse expert activation changes the effective service costs across load regimes. Together, our results provide a structured framework for understanding SD in real LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [130] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于稀疏自编码器（SAEs）的概念可解释性框架，用于揭示EEG基础模型内部的临床特征表示，不仅能定量评估模型的特征解耦程度，还能通过语义引导干预实现生理意义上的模型输出操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管EEG基础模型在临床表现上达到顶尖，但其黑盒特性阻碍了临床信任，目前缺乏对其内部计算机制及表示逻辑的深度理解与解释方法。

Method: 使用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAEs）从三种不同的EEG Transformer（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）中提取稀疏特征字典；设计了基于“目标vs非目标”度量的概念引导（Concept Steering）技术，并结合谱解码器（Spectral Decoder）将潜在特征映射回可解释的生理频谱信号。

Result: 成功识别了模型的三种操作状态（可选择性引导、编码但解耦、未编码）；揭示了模型存在的“破坏性”干预（Wrecking-ball interventions）及临床混杂（如年龄-病理耦合）等关键性失败模式；实现了通过潜在空间操控精确改变EEG谱特征（如抑制病理性慢波）。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个通用的可解释性框架，能够有效剖析EEG基础模型内部的特征表示，揭示临床相关的表征行为及潜在失效模式，为提升医疗AI模型的透明度与临床信任度提供了关键技术支撑。

Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

</details>


### [131] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 文章提出了一种无需手动调参的架构退火VAE框架，用于从大规模多中心dMRI连接组中解耦采集效应（离散变量）与生物差异（连续变量）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dMRI数据的多中心、多扫描仪及多协议采集导致了显著的变异性，干扰了结构连接组分析。现有的降维方法难以区分生物变异与采集效应，而现有的混合潜在空间模型（hybrid latent-space models）通常需要繁琐的人工参数调优。

Method: 引入了一种新型的无监督框架，通过在编码器输出端应用架构退火（architectural annealing）机制，而非传统的损失函数退火，使模型在训练过程中能够自适应地平衡离散（代表采集效应）和连续（代表生物变异）潜在变量。对照模型包括标准VAE、PCA+k-means以及基于损失退火的混合模型。

Result: 通过7,416组dMRI结构连接组的大规模数据集验证，该方法在采集点识别上表现更优（ARI=0.53，p<0.05），成功在混合潜空间中分离出与扫描仪及协议差异对应的聚类，同时保留了生物学上的潜在特征。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于架构退火（architectural annealing）的无监督学习框架，能够有效并自适应地从dMRI连接组中分离出采集效应（acquisition-related effects）与生物变异，避免了传统模型对离散分量参数的人工调优。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [132] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用联邦学习进行大模型参数高效微调的框架，成功在保护数据隐私和克服数据分布异构性的前提下，增强了模型在医疗和金融领域的专业应用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量高价值数据受隐私、法规和合规限制无法共享，且分布于不同机构呈现非独立同分布（non-IID）特性，阻碍了通用大模型在特定领域的深层应用。

Method: 基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台，提出了一种联合微调架构，在不交换私有数据的前提下，比较了三种参数高效微调策略（LoRA、QLoRA、IA3）在医疗和金融领域跨机构数据分布下的表现。

Result: 实验表明联邦微调效果接近集中式训练，显著优于孤立的单机构学习；QLoRA和IA3在保证性能的同时显著提升了训练效率。

Conclusion: 联邦参数高效微调（Federated PEFT）是一种在保护私有数据隐私、应对跨机构数据异构性（non-IID）的同时，有效提升大模型（LLM）领域性能的可行方案，且具备良好的绿色AI（能效）潜力。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [133] [EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941)
*Jiaqi Liu,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveMem是一种支持检索机制自演化的LLM记忆系统，通过LLM驱动的诊断与架构调整，实现了检索策略的自动优化与发现，显著提升了智能体在长期记忆任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体记忆系统将检索配置视为固定不变的，无法适应不断变化的存储内容和任务需求，因此需要一种能让存储内容与检索机制协同演化的自适应记忆方案。

Method: 提出一种名为EvolveMem的自演化记忆架构，将完整的检索配置暴露为结构化动作空间，并设计一个LLM驱动的诊断模块。该模块根据失败日志分析根因并调整配置，配合包含回滚和探索机制的防护Meta-Analyzer，实现检索架构的自动演化。

Result: 在LoCoMo基准测试中，相比最强Baseline提升25.7%，相比最小基线提升78.0%；在MemBench中提升18.9%。演化后的配置在不同任务间表现出正向迁移，表明其习得了通用的检索原则。

Conclusion: EvolveMem通过闭环自演化机制，摆脱了对人工配置检索系统的依赖，并能发现通用的检索策略，优于人工设计的Baseline，且具备良好的配置迁移性。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that operate across multiple sessions, yet existing memory systems treat retrieval infrastructure as fixed: stored content evolves while scoring functions, fusion strategies, and answer-generation policies remain frozen at deployment. We argue that truly adaptive memory requires co-evolution at two levels: the stored knowledge and the retrieval mechanism that queries it. We present EvolveMem, a self-evolving memory architecture that exposes its full retrieval configuration as a structured action space optimized by an LLM-powered diagnosis module. In each evolution round, the module reads per-question failure logs, identifies root causes, and proposes targeted configuration adjustments; a guarded meta-analyzer applies them with automatic revert-on-regression and explore-on-stagnation safeguards. This closed-loop self-evolution realizes an AutoResearch process: the system autonomously conducts iterative research cycles on its own architecture, replacing manual configuration tuning. Starting from a minimal baseline, the process converges autonomously, discovering effective retrieval strategies including entirely new configuration dimensions not present in the original action space. On LoCoMo, EvolveMem outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.7% relative and achieves a 78.0% relative improvement over the minimal baseline. On MemBench, EvolveMem exceeds the strongest baseline by 18.9% relative. Evolved configurations transfer across benchmarks with positive rather than catastrophic transfer, indicating that the self-evolution process captures universal retrieval principles rather than benchmark-specific heuristics. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem.

</details>


### [134] [The Geometry of LLM Quantization: GPTQ as Babai's Nearest Plane Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.18553)
*Jiale Chen,Yalda Shabanzadeh,Elvir Crnčević,Torsten Hoefler,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了GPTQ量化算法在数学上等同于格论中的Babai最近平面算法，补齐了其理论缺失，并利用这一联系提出了性能更佳的量化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPTQ虽然是LLM量化的标准方法，但其数学本质（代数更新序列）模糊了其几何意义和最坏情况下的理论保证，缺乏深层的理论支撑。

Method: 通过数学证明将GPTQ的逆序（从后往前）执行过程与格论中的Babai最近平面算法对应；推导了基于Hessian矩阵的误差上界；以此为基础设计了避开权重裁剪的量化优化方案，并开发了高效的GPU推理算子。

Result: 揭示了GPTQ的几何意义，证明了其满足Babai算法的误差上界，所改进的无裁剪量化算法在性能上优于原版GPTQ，并提供了配套的高效GPU推理支持。

Conclusion: 通过建立GPTQ与格论中Babai最近平面算法的数学等价性，为大型语言模型的量化提供了坚实的理论基础，并启发了更优的无裁剪量化算法。

Abstract: Quantizing the weights of large language models (LLMs) from 16-bit to lower bitwidth is the de facto approach to deploy massive transformers onto more affordable accelerators. While GPTQ emerged as one of the standard methods for one-shot post-training quantization at LLM scale, its inner workings are described as a sequence of algebraic updates that obscure geometric meaning or worst-case guarantees. In this work, we show that, when executed back-to-front (from the last to first dimension) for a linear layer, GPTQ is mathematically identical to Babai's nearest plane algorithm for the classical closest vector problem (CVP) on a lattice defined by the Hessian matrix of the layer's inputs. This equivalence is based on a sophisticated mathematical argument, and has two analytical consequences: first, the GPTQ error propagation step gains an intuitive geometric interpretation; second, GPTQ inherits the error upper bound of Babai's algorithm under the assumption that no weights are clipped. Leveraging this bound, we design post-training quantization methods that avoid clipping, and outperform the original GPTQ. In addition, we provide efficient GPU inference kernels for the resulting representation. Taken together, these results place GPTQ on a firm theoretical footing and open the door to importing decades of progress in lattice algorithms towards the design of future quantization algorithms for billion-parameter models. Source code is available at https://github.com/IST-DASLab/GPTQ-Babai.

</details>


### [135] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文通过距离几何视角揭示了对比学习隐含的嵌入空间几何结构，证明了损失函数的权重方案直接影响表征的几何分布及对数据偏斜（如类不平衡）或标签分布失配的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习旨在通过学习表征来反映样本间的相似性图，但目前人们对这些学习得到的嵌入（embeddings）的背后几何结构缺乏清晰的理解，导致设计目标函数时缺乏理论支撑。

Method: 利用距离几何问题（Distance Geometry Problems）的视角，将加权 InfoNCE 目标诠释为特定的几何优化问题，通过数学推导刻画了监督和弱监督学习下对比表示的最优几何结构。

Result: 证明了 SupCon 在类不平衡情况下会产生非均匀的类间相似度，而 Soft SupCon 能保持正则单纯形（regular simplex）结构；指出了 y-Aware CL 在标签未分布于超球面上时存在几何失配，并提出了满足几何一致性的改进方案。

Conclusion: 权重方案的选择直接决定了对比学习的几何构型是否可实现、退化或不一致。该框架为对比学习目标的设计提供了理论指导，特别是解决类不平衡和标签空间与潜在空间几何不匹配对算法性能的负面影响。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

</details>


### [136] [Finite Sample Bounds for Learning with Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14168)
*Devin Smedira,Abhijith Jayakumar,Sidhant Misra,Marc Vuffray,Andrey Y. Lokhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过非渐近分析，首次为多项式指数族模型的分数匹配学习提供了具有多项式维度的样本复杂性界，弥补了该领域理论研究的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然分数匹配在连续变量指数族学习中应用广泛，但其统计特性的理论理解（特别是样本复杂性）相对匮乏，且现有研究多仅限于渐近分析。

Method: 针对多项式指数族分布，利用分数匹配（Score Matching）方法进行模型结构和参数学习，并进行非渐近（non-asymptotic）样本复杂性分析。

Result: 得出了分数匹配学习多项式指数族模型的非渐近样本复杂性界，显示其对模型维度具有多项式依赖性。

Conclusion: 本文首次推导了多项式指数族模型在分数匹配下的非渐近样本复杂性界，证明了其与模型维度呈多项式关系，为该领域提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: Learning of continuous exponential family distributions with unbounded support remains an important area of research for both theory and applications in high-dimensional statistics. In recent years, score matching has become a widely used method for learning exponential families with continuous variables due to its computational ease when compared against maximum likelihood estimation. However, theoretical understanding of the statistical properties of score matching is still lacking. In this work, we provide a non-asymptotic sample complexity analysis for learning the structure of exponential families of polynomials with score matching. The derived sample bounds show a polynomial dependence on the model dimension. These bounds are the first of its kind, as all prior work has shown only asymptotic bounds on the sample complexity.

</details>


### [137] [Collider-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents with Particle Physics Analysis Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13950)
*Darius A. Faroughy,Sofia Palacios Schweitzer,Ian Pang,Siddharth Mishra-Sharma,David Shih*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了科研级基准 Collider-Bench，用于评估 AI 智能体复现复杂物理实验分析的能力；结果显示目前智能体仍落后于人类专家，在该领域仍面临巨大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试缺乏科学研究的复杂度和深度，无法有效评估模型在真实科学实验分析（如处理不完整的实现细节、近似工具链）中的表现。

Method: 提出了 Collider-Bench 基准测试，要求智能体从公开论文和开源软件出发，通过物理推理、领域知识及尝试-纠错机制，复现大型强子对撞机（LHC）的实验分析流程；采用标准化直方图指标评估预测准确性，并结合 LLM 判定器评估定性故障模式。

Result: 在实验中，没有智能体能够稳定超越“人在回路（physicist-in-the-loop）”的物理学家表现，且模型在复现过程中常出现幻觉、伪造数据等问题。

Conclusion: 当前的通用大模型智能体在处理复杂的科学实验复现任务时尚未达到人类物理学家的水平，该基准测试揭示了自动化科学分析在领域知识、推理能力及工具链整合方面的重大挑战。

Abstract: Autonomous language-model agents are increasingly evaluated on long-horizon tool-use tasks, but existing benchmarks rarely capture the complexity and nuance of real scientific work. To address this gap, we introduce Collider-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating whether LLM agents can reproduce experimental analyses from the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) using only public papers and open scientific software. Such analyses are often difficult to reproduce because the public toolchain only approximates the software used internally by the experimental collaborations, while the published papers inevitably omit implementation details needed for a faithful reconstruction. Agents must therefore rely on physical reasoning, domain knowledge, and trial-and-error to fill these gaps. Each task requires the agent to turn a published analysis into an executable simulation-and-selection pipeline and submit predicted collision event yields in specified signal regions. These predictions are evaluated with standard histogram metrics that provide continuous fidelity scores without a hand-written rubric. We also report the computational cost incurred by each agent per task. Finally, we evaluate the codebase and full session trace using an LLM judge to catch qualitative failure modes such as fabrications, hallucinations and duplications. We release an initial set of tasks drawn from LHC searches, together with a containerized sandbox and event simulation tools. We evaluate across a capability ladder of general purpose coding agents. Our results show that on average no agent reliably beats the physicist-in-the-loop solution.

</details>


### [138] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一项针对机器人生成式控制的改进，通过使用“WarmPrior”（基于近期动作历史的先验）替代标准高斯源分布，有效简化了概率转移路径，在操作任务中实现了更高的成功率与更好的强化学习样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散和流匹配的生成式策略通常采用简单的标准高斯分布作为源分布，未能充分利用机器人执行任务时的时序动作相关性，限制了性能表现。

Method: 引入 WarmPrior：一种利用近期时间动作历史（recent action history）构建的、具有时间基础的简单先验。将其替代生成式模型中的标准高斯源分布。

Result: 在机器人操作任务中，WarmPrior 显著提升了成功率、提高了样本效率（在强化学习中），并使概率轨迹（probability paths）更加平直，从而优化了模型训练与生成的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了源分布（source distribution）作为生成式机器人控制中的关键设计维度，此前被严重忽视。引入 WarmPrior 能显著提升扩散和流匹配策略的性能。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [139] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对蒸馏方法能效评估不全面（忽视教师端开销）的问题，本文提出了一个端到端的能效核算框架，评估了主流蒸馏策略的真实生态代价，并为节能模型开发提供了标准化的衡量工具与设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型蒸馏研究往往忽视了完整的端到端能源与资源成本（尤其是教师端的计算负担），缺乏对蒸馏流水线整体能耗的量化评估，导致无法真实衡量其在降低能耗方面的实际效益。

Method: 构建了一个综合的能耗核算框架，通过详细跟踪GPU设备在蒸馏流水线各个阶段（包括数据生成、Logit缓存、评估等）的功率消耗，系统性测量了基于Logit的知识蒸馏和合成数据监督微调的能耗，并构建了能源-质量帕累托边界（Pareto frontiers）。

Result: 研究揭示了此前被忽视的蒸馏过程中的隐藏能源成本；发现蒸馏方法的能源开销在不同场景下差异巨大；通过实验确立了能源效率与模型质量之间的折中关系，并提供了可量化的决策指南。

Conclusion: 蒸馏并非总是节能的最佳选择，其综合能耗高度依赖于教师模型的使用方式；研究提出了实用的设计准则及基准测量框架，强调了在资源受限场景下评估完整蒸馏流水线（而非仅关注学生模型）的必要性。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [140] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3 是表格数据领域的一款大规模基础模型，通过测试时计算扩展和高性能架构优化，在百万行数据级别实现了 SOTA 性能，同时在推理速度上实现数量级提升，无需外部模型、训练数据或 LLM 即可在多项任务中实现领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格数据学习方法（如梯度提升树）在处理大规模数据时耗时较长或需要大量的调优；TabPFN-2.5 在处理百万行规模数据时存在性能瓶颈，旨在进一步提升模型在大规模数据下的表现、推理速度以及在多样化任务（时间序列、关系型、表格-文本）中的适用性。

Method: 引入测试时计算扩展（test-time compute scaling）；采用 row-chunking 和缩减的 KV 缓存技术；模型完全基于合成数据预训练；开发了 TabPFN-3-Plus（Thinking）API；针对时间序列、关系型数据和带文本数据的表格进行特定优化。

Result: TabPFN-3 在 TabArena 基准测试中超越所有基准模型（包括调优 ensemble 模型）；在百万行、200 特征数据集上击败经过 8 小时调优的梯度提升树模型；TabPFN-3-Plus 在 TabArena 上实现 200+ Elo 的领先优势；推理速度最高提升 20 倍，SHAP 值计算提升 120 倍。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3 标志着表格数据基础模型的重大飞跃，通过引入测试时计算扩展（test-time compute scaling）和架构优化，实现了在百万级数据规模上对传统梯度提升树基准的超越，同时保持了极高的推理速度。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [141] [Neural Fields for NV-Center Inverse Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13988)
*Zhixuan Zhao,Tao Zhong,Yixun Hu,Nathalie P. de Leon,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对NV量子传感中的非线性反演难题，本文提出了NeTMY框架，利用坐标神经场耦合可微物理模型，有效解决了模型优化中的“中心坍缩”问题，实现了高精度的稀疏源重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反问题求解方法（手工正则化或有监督学习）在面对非线性、频谱耦合及物理敏感的逆向问题时（如NV中心磁噪声传感）失效，传统的标量近似会导致反演失败（中心坍缩）。

Method: 提出NeTMY架构：1. 采用无摊销（amortization-free）的坐标神经场方法；2. 构建基于张量幂和偶极算子的可微NV正向模型；3. 集成退火位置编码、多尺度优化、稀疏性/门控机制及频谱保真度损失函数。

Result: 在合成数据测试中，NeTMY在定位精度和分布指标上均优于基准方法；机制分析显示其通过参数化平滑和重新分配更新步骤，解决了中心坍缩路径问题。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为NeTMY的物理感知神经反问题求解方法，该方法通过坐标神经场和可微的正向物理模型有效缓解了以往方法中存在的“中心坍缩”（center-collapse）现象，为NV量子传感提供了一种高精度的反演方案，并验证了其在物理忠实性反演任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Inverse problems in scientific sensing are often solved with either hand-designed regularizers or supervised networks trained on simulated labels, yet both can fail when the forward model is nonlinear, spectrally coupled, and physically delicate. We study this issue for noise sensing based on nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond, where a quantum sensor measures magnetic-noise spectra generated by sparse spin sources. We show that replacing a common scalar/coherent forward approximation with a tensor power-summed dipolar operator changes the inverse landscape and exposes a center-collapse failure mode in free-density optimization. We propose NeTMY, an amortization-free coordinate neural field coupled to the differentiable NV forward model, with annealed positional encoding, multiscale optimization, sparsity/gating, and spectrum-fidelity losses. Across sparse synthetic reconstructions generated by the corrected operator, NeTMY achieves the best localization and distributional metrics in the tested benchmark. Mechanism experiments show that NeTMY does not directly execute the raw density-space gradient; its parameterization smooths and redistributes updates, mitigating the center-collapse pathology. These results position NV quantum sensing as a useful testbed for physics-faithful neural inverse problems.

</details>
