Abstract: Accurately identifying student misconceptions is crucial for personalized education but faces three challenges: (1) data scarcity with long-tail distribution, where authentic student reasoning is difficult to synthesize; (2) fuzzy boundaries between error categories with high annotation noise; (3) deployment parado-large models overlook unconventional approaches due to pretraining bias and cannot be deployed on edge, while small models overfit to noise. Unlike traditional methods that increase diversity through large-scale data synthesis, we propose a two-stage knowledge distillation framework that mines high-value samples from existing data. The first stage performs standard distillation to transfer task capabilities. The second stage introduces a dual-layer marginal selection mechanism based on cognitive uncertainty, identifying four types of critical samples based on teacher model uncertainty and confidence differences. For different data subsets, we design difficulty-adaptive mechanism to balance hard/soft label contributions, enabling student models to inherit inter-class relationships from teacher soft labels while distinguishing ambiguous error types. Experiments show that with augmented training on only 10.30% of filtered samples, we achieve MAP@3 of 0.9585 (+17.8%) on the MAP-Charting dataset, and using only a 4B parameter model, we attain 84.38% accuracy on cross-topic tests of middle school algebra misconception benchmarks, significantly outperforming sota LLM (67.73%) and standard fine-tuned 72B models (81.25%). Our code is available at https://github.com/RoschildRui/acl2026_map.

</details>


### [218] [Crys-JEPA: Accelerating Crystal Discovery via Embedding Screening and Generative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14759)
*Nian Liu,Nikita Kazeev,Stephen Gregory Dale,Artem Maevskiy,Yuwei Zeng,Ryoji Kubo,Pengru Huang,Thomas Laurent,Yann LeCun,Kostya S. Novoselov,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对晶体生成中稳定性与新颖性难以兼顾的难题，提出了能量感知的 Crys-JEPA 架构及迭代细化流程，显著提升了新发现材料的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型多基于极大似然估计，导致生成的晶体难以同时兼顾“稳定性”与“新颖性”，且在该二者之间存在明显的权衡困境（Trade-off）。

Method: 提出了 Crys-JEPA（一种晶体联合嵌入预测架构），用于学习能够保留形成能差异的能量感知潜在空间；并结合一套“筛选与细化”流程，利用生成的高质量晶体对模型进行迭代优化。

Result: 在 MP-20 和 Alex-MP-20 数据集上，该方法在 V.S.U.N 指标上分别取得了 81.4% 和 82.6% 的性能提升。

Conclusion: Crys-JEPA 成功克服了晶体生成中“稳定性-新颖性”的困境。通过学习能量感知的潜在空间，该框架实现了更高效的生成与筛选，显著提升了生成材料的综合质量。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation seeks to discover materials that are not merely realistic, but also stable and novel. However, most existing generative models are trained to maximize the likelihood of observed crystals, which encourages samples to stay close to known materials yet not necessarily align with the criteria that matter in discovery. Through an empirical investigation, we show that current crystal generative models are caught in a pronounced stability--novelty trade-off: moving toward the observed distribution preserves stability but limits novelty, whereas moving away from it quickly destroys stability. This suggests that the useful region for discovering crystals that are both stable and novel is extremely narrow. To escape the trade-off, we introduce Crys-JEPA, a joint embedding predictive architecture for crystals that learns an energy-aware latent space preserving formation-energy differences. In this space, stability assessment can be reformulated as an embedding-based comparison against accessible training crystals, reducing the reliance on expensive energy evaluation and task-specific external references. Building on Crys-JEPA, we further develop a screening-and-refinement pipeline that identifies promising generated crystals and reintroduces them to refine the generative model. On MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 datasets, we achieve improvements over baselines up to 81.4% and 82.6% on V.S.U.N metric, respectively.

</details>


### [219] [Composable Crystals: Controllable Materials Discovery via Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14769)
*Nian Liu,Yuwei Zeng,Ryoji Kubo,Nikita Kazeev,Stephen Gregory Dale,Artem Maevskiy,Pengru Huang,Thomas Laurent,Kostya S. Novoselov,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概念组合的晶体生成架构，通过学习可重用的原子和结构特征（概念），实现了比传统黑盒随机采样更精细、可控且更具新颖性的晶体结构生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的晶体生成方法主要依赖黑盒随机采样，缺乏对结构生成的显式控制，难以有效生成训练分布之外的新颖结构。

Method: 1. 利用向量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）从晶体中学习并发现可重用的“晶体概念”（包含局部原子环境和全局对称性信息）；2. 设计了一个组合生成器（Composition Generator），通过模型自生成的优质样本进行迭代精炼，以提升概念组合的效率；3. 利用组合后的概念作为条件，指导晶体的生成过程。

Result: 在 MP-20 和 Alex-MP-20 数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在 V.S.U.N. 指标上相比基准模型分别提升了高达 53.2% 和 51.7%，特别是在新颖性方面取得了显著增长。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了通过学习和重组可解释的晶体“概念”，可以显著提升生成模型在有效性、稳定性、独特性和新颖性（V.S.U.N.）指标上的表现，并实现了在训练分布之外的可控探索。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation, a central task in materials discovery, aims to generate crystals that are simultaneously valid, stable, unique, and novel. Existing methods mainly rely on black-box stochastic sampling, providing limited control over how generated structures move beyond the observed distribution. In this paper, we introduce a concept-based compositional framework for crystal generation. We train a vector-quantized variational autoencoder to automatically discover a shared set of reusable crystal concepts, which serve as building blocks for guided generation. These learned concepts naturally exhibit interpretability from both local atomic environments and global symmetry patterns, and generalize to crystals from different distributions. By recombining such concepts, our framework enables controllable exploration of novel crystals beyond the training distribution, rather than relying solely on unconstrained random sampling. To further improve composition efficiency, we introduce a composition generator and iteratively refine it using high-quality samples generated by the model itself. The resulting concept compositions are then used to condition downstream crystal generation. Numerical experiments on MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 show that compositing concepts separately increase base model up to 53.2% and 51.7% on V.S.U.N metric, with particular gains in novelty.

</details>


### [220] [Beyond What to Select: A Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-Volume Scheduling for Efficient Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14773)
*Suorong Yang,Hanqi Zhu,Hai Gan,Fangjian Su,Guang Li,Furao Shen,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有数据选择方法仅在样本内容上做文章的局限，本文提出了PODS框架，通过动态震荡控制训练中的数据选取比例，在利用选择诱导的正则化效应的同时保证了训练效率与模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据选择方法多关注“选什么”（样本评分指标），而忽视了“选多少”（数据量）的动态变化。作者从优化视角发现，选取的样本量会产生隐藏的正则化效应，且该效应与选取比例直接相关。

Method: 提出了名为PODS（Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-volume Scheduling）的插件式框架。PODS不依赖于特定的样本评分指标，而是通过在训练过程中在“低比例正则化阶段”和“高比例恢复阶段”之间交替切换，实现数据选取量的动态调度。

Result: PODS具有轻量级、任务无关和即插即用的特性。实验表明，PODS能显著提升效率-泛化权衡，在ImageNet-1k训练中降低50%计算成本并提升精度，在LLM指令微调中实现超过2倍的加速且不损失性能。

Conclusion: PODS通过动态调度选取比例（震荡式调度），在利用数据选择带来的正则化效应与保证训练优化保真度之间取得了更优的平衡，证明了调整“数据量”与调整“数据选择策略”同样重要。

Abstract: Data selection accelerates training by identifying representative training data while preserving model performance. However, existing methods mainly focus on designing sample-importance criteria, i.e., deciding what to select, while typically fixing the selected data volume as the target ratio throughout training. Thus, they are often dynamic in sample identity but static in data volume. In this work, we revisit data selection from an optimization perspective and show that selected-data training induces an implicit regularization effect modulated by the instantaneous selection ratio. This reveals a key trade-off: lower ratios amplify selection-induced regularization, whereas higher ratios preserve data coverage and optimization fidelity. Motivated by this insight, we propose PODS, a Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-volume Scheduling framework. Rather than introducing another sample-scoring metric, PODS serves as a lightweight module that dynamically schedules how much data to select over training. Under the target selection ratio, PODS alternates between low-ratio regularization phases and high-ratio recovery phases to exploit selection-induced regularization without sacrificing optimization stability. With its lightweight, ratio-level, and task-agnostic design, PODS is compatible with existing static and dynamic selection methods and broadly applicable across training paradigms. Experiments across various datasets, architectures, and tasks show that PODS consistently improves the efficiency-generalization trade-off, e.g., reducing ImageNet-1k training cost by 50% with improved accuracy and accelerating LLM instruction tuning by over 2x without performance degradation.

</details>


### [221] [Peng's Q($λ$) for Conservative Value Estimation in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14779)
*Byeongchan Kim,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CPQL算法，通过采用多步Peng's Q($\lambda$)算子进行保守值估计，解决了离线RL中过悲观的问题，并在离线学习及后续的在线微调中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线RL保守方法往往存在值估计过度悲观的问题，且难以同时实现超越行为策略的性能和接近最优的理论保证。

Method: 提出了一种无模型的离线多步强化学习算法——保守Peng's Q($\lambda$)（CPQL），将PQL算子适配用于保守值估计，并利用离线轨迹实现隐式行为正则化。

Result: CPQL在D4RL基准测试中显著优于现有的单步离线RL基线；在离线到在线微调任务中，能有效防止微调初期的性能下降并实现更好的收敛。

Conclusion: CPQL通过引入多步算子有效解决了离线RL中的值估计过保守问题，并证明了其在离线到在线迁移学习中的稳健性，是离线RL领域的一个重要研究进展。

Abstract: We propose a model-free offline multi-step reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, Conservative Peng's Q($λ$) (CPQL). Our algorithm adapts the Peng's Q($λ$) (PQL) operator for conservative value estimation as an alternative to the Bellman operator. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work in offline RL to theoretically and empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of conservative value estimation with a \textit{multi-step} operator by fully leveraging offline trajectories. The fixed point of the PQL operator in offline RL lies closer to the value function of the behavior policy, thereby naturally inducing implicit behavior regularization. CPQL simultaneously mitigates over-pessimistic value estimation, achieves performance greater than (or equal to) that of the behavior policy, and provides near-optimal performance guarantees -- a milestone that previous conservative approaches could not achieve. Extensive numerical experiments on the D4RL benchmark demonstrate that CPQL consistently and significantly outperforms existing offline single-step baselines. In addition to the contributions of CPQL in offline RL, our proposed method also contributes to the offline-to-online learning framework. Using the Q-function pre-trained by CPQL in offline settings enables the online PQL agent to avoid the performance drop typically observed at the start of fine-tuning and to attain robust performance improvements. Our code is available at https://github.com/oh-lab/CPQL.

</details>


### [222] [Understanding Imbalanced Forgetting in Rehearsal-Based Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14785)
*Alberto Tamajo,Srinandan Dasmahapatra,Rahman Attar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析最后一层梯度干扰，揭示了重放式类增量学习中类别遗忘不均衡的机制，发现自我干扰和新类干扰是导致遗忘差异的核心动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在类增量学习（CIL）中，尽管使用重放（Rehearsal）策略，但仍存在显著的各类遗忘程度不均衡现象，现有研究对该现象的成因缺乏深入解释。

Method: 构建了三个基于最后一层参数的系数，用于量化分析增量学习过程中各过去类别遭受的梯度级干扰，并验证这些系数预测遗忘排名的有效性。

Result: 研究表明，这三个系数能可靠预测过去类别在增量步骤后的遗忘排名，其中“自我干扰”系数是遗忘的最强预测因子，且该因子受到“新类干扰”的影响。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了类别间遗忘不均衡的机制，即“自我干扰”和“新类干扰”是导致遗忘的主要原因，为通过平衡干扰源来缓解灾难性遗忘提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Neural networks suffer from catastrophic forgetting in class-incremental learning (CIL) settings. Rehearsal$\unicode{x2013}$replaying a subset of past samples$\unicode{x2013}$is a well-established mitigation strategy. However, recent results suggest that, despite balanced rehearsal allocation, some classes are forgotten substantially more than others. Despite its relevance, this imbalanced forgetting phenomenon remains underexplored. This work shows that imbalanced forgetting arises systematically and severely in rehearsal-based CIL and investigates it extensively. Specifically, we construct, from a principled analysis, three last-layer coefficients that capture different gradient-level sources of interference affecting each past class during an incremental step. We then demonstrate that, together, they reliably predict how past classes will rank in terms of forgetting at the end of that step. While predictive performance alone does not establish causality, these results support the interpretation of the coefficients as a plausible mechanistic account linking last-layer gradient-level interactions during training to class-level forgetting outcomes. Notably, one coefficient$\unicode{x2013}$capturing self-induced interference$\unicode{x2013}$emerges as the strongest predictor, with controlled experiments providing evidence consistent with this coefficient being influenced by the new-class interference coefficient. Overall, our findings provide valuable insights and suggest promising directions for mitigating imbalanced forgetting by reducing class-wise disparities in the identified sources of interference.

</details>


### [223] [GFMate: Empowering Graph Foundation Models with Test-time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14809)
*Yan Jiang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GFMate，一种针对图基础模型（GFMs）的预训练无关测试时图提示微调方法，通过解耦源域和利用未标注目标域数据，显著增强了模型的泛化能力和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图提示学习方法过度依赖源域信息，导致其泛化能力受限；且GFMs在实际应用中仅依赖少样本微调，未能充分挖掘未标注测试数据的价值。

Method: 提出GFMate框架，由两部分组成：1) 引入在目标域上应用的中心点提示（centroid prompts）和层提示（layer prompts），实现与特定源域和预训练阶段的解耦；2) 设计测试时互补学习目标（test-time complementary learning objective），同时利用标注和未标注的目标域数据进行提示微调。

Result: 在12个基准数据集上进行了大量实验，性能提升高达30.63%，证明了该方法在性能和计算效率方面的优越性。

Conclusion: GFMate通过引入与预训练无关的测试时图提示微调方法，有效解决了GFMs在跨领域泛化和利用未标注目标域数据方面的不足，在提升性能和效率方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Graph prompt tuning has shown great potential in graph learning by introducing trainable prompts to enhance the model performance in conventional single-domain scenarios. Recent research has extended graph prompts to improve Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) by few-shot tuning auxiliary prompts. Despite their progress, most existing methods embed source-domain information into prompts, which serve either as input to GFMs or encoded during model pre-training. Such prompt entanglement with specific source domains and GFM pre-training strategy restricts their generalisability to other domains and different GFMs. Furthermore, existing GFM prompts merely rely on few-shot tuning for adaptation, neglecting the rich information in unlabelled target domain test data. Motivated by these insights, this paper aims to empower GFMs with pre-training-agnostic test-time graph prompt tuning, named GFMate. GFMate introduces centroid and layer prompts applied after pre-training on target domains, avoiding entanglement with specific source domains and model pre-training. In addition, a test-time complementary learning objective is devised to exploit both labelled and unlabelled target domain data for effective test-time prompt tuning. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmark datasets demonstrate the superior performance and efficiency of GFMate, achieving improvements of up to 30.63%. Code is available at https://github.com/YanJiangJerry/GFMate.

</details>


### [224] [ToMAToMP: Robust and Multi-Parameter Topological Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14824)
*Ludo Andrianirina,Mathieu Carrière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ToMAToMP算法，这是首个利用多参数持久同调（MMA分解）实现多函数协同聚类的TDA方法，不仅解决了经典ToMATo对图参数的依赖和对异常值的敏感问题，还提升了聚类效果与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典ToMATo算法存在三项局限：1. 严重依赖用户输入的图结构超参数；2. 对函数值的异常值高度敏感；3. 无法同时处理多个数据函数（如多维特征或条件）。

Method: 引入ToMAToMP算法，利用多参数持久同调中的MMA分解（Minimal Multinomial Approximation decomposition）工具，实现对多个函数的同时处理，并证明了该方法的鲁棒性。

Result: 数值实验表明，ToMAToMP在多种数据集上均优于现有的拓扑与非拓扑聚类基准方法，且成功攻克了图参数调节和异常值敏感等关键技术难题。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ToMAToMP通过多参数持久同调技术成功扩展了经典的ToMATo聚类算法，在多函数处理、图结构依赖及异常值鲁棒性方面表现优异，确立了其在拓扑数据分析领域的实用价值与理论优势。

Abstract: Topological clustering, and its main algorithm ToMATo, is a clustering method from Topological Data Analysis (TDA) which has been applied successfully in several applications during the last few years. This is due to its high versatility, as clusters are detected from the persistent components in the sublevel sets of any user-defined function (gene expression, pixel values, etc), and efficiency, as topological clustering enjoys robustness guarantees. However, ToMATo is also limited in several ways. First, a graph on the data points needs to be provided as a hyper-parameter of the method (whose fine-tuning is left to the user). Second, ToMATo is known to be very sensitive to outlier values in the function range. Finally, and most importantly, ToMATo can only handle one function at a time, whereas it is critical to use several functions in various applications. In this article, we introduce ToMAToMP: the first topological clustering method able to handle several functions at the same time with theoretical guarantees. More specifically, we leverage a recent tool from multi-parameter persistent homology, called MMA decomposition, to design our clustering algorithm, and prove that it enjoys robustness properties. As corollaries, we show that it can be used to make ToMATo independent of graph tuning, and robust to outliers. Finally, we provide a set of numerical experiments showcasing the efficiency and quality of the clusterings produced by ToMAToMP, by showing strong improvement over non-topological and topological baselines for various datasets.

</details>


### [225] [GenAI for Energy-Efficient and Interference-Aware Compressed Sensing of GNSS Signals on a Google Edge TPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14839)
*Thorben Wegner,Lucas Heublein,Tobias Feigl,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Alexander Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生成式AI（VAE）的GNSS干扰实时识别架构，通过Google Edge TPU进行模型部署与8位量化，在实现超过42倍数据压缩的同时，维持了对多种干扰类型的高精度识别，有效解决了低功耗边缘环境下的GNSS监测难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNSS干扰分类方法依赖云端处理，存在数据传输复杂、成本高及功耗大的痛点，难以满足实时性及功率受限的应用场景需求。因此，研究旨在开发一种低功耗、边缘侧的在线GNSS干扰压缩与识别方案。

Method: 1. 使用变分自编码器（VAE）及相关变体对GNSS信号进行压缩与特征重构；
2. 将模型部署于Google Edge TPU并通过8位量化（8-bit quantization）进行模型优化；
3. 对比多种自动编码器（AE）架构，并利用条件VAE及FactorVAE进行潜在特征解耦；
4. 在IQ数据、FFT数据和手动工程特征上验证模型对约72种干扰类型的检测性能。

Result: 1. 系统实现了超过42倍的数据压缩率；
2. 基于重构信号的干扰分类F2得分为0.915，与处理原始信号所得的0.923非常接近；
3. 实现了对约72种不同干扰类型的精准识别；
4. 边缘侧部署显著降低了干扰信号传输的开销。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了利用生成式AI（尤其是变分自编码器）在边缘硬件上进行高效GNSS干扰信号压缩与实时分类的可行性。该方法不仅大幅降低了数据传输成本和功耗，且通过模型量化实现了在资源受限设备上的高性能部署，为GNSS干扰检测提供了一项实用且稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional methods for classifying global navigation satellite system (GNSS) jamming signals typically involve post-processing raw or spectral data streams, requiring complex and costly data transmission to cloud-based interference classification systems. In contrast, our proposed approach efficiently compresses GNSS data streams directly at the hardware receiver while simultaneously classifying jamming and spoofing attacks in real time. Given the growing prevalence of GNSS jamming, there is a critical need for real-time solutions suitable for power-constrained environments. This paper introduces a novel method for compressing and classifying GNSS jamming threats using generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), specifically variational autoencoders (VAEs), deployed on Google Edge tensor processing units (TPUs). The study evaluates various autoencoder (AE) architectures to compress and reconstruct GNSS signals, focusing on preserving interference characteristics while minimizing data size near the receiver hardware. The pipeline adapts large-scale AE models for Google Edge TPUs through 8-bit quantization to ensure energy-efficient deployment. Tests on raw in-phase and quadrature-phase (IQ) data, Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) data, and handcrafted features show the system achieves significant compression (>42x) and accurate classification of approximately 72 interference types on reconstructed signals (F2-score 0.915), closely matching the original signals (F2-score 0.923). The hardware-centric GenAI approach also substantially reduces jammer signal transmission costs, offering a practical solution for interference mitigation. Ablation studies on conditional and factorized VAEs (i.e., FactorVAE) explore latent feature disentanglement for data generation, enhancing model interpretability and fostering trust in machine learning (ML) solutions for sensitive interference applications.

</details>


### [226] [GPart: End-to-End Isometric Fine-Tuning via Global Parameter Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14841)
*Paolo Mandica,Michał Brzozowski,Zuzanna Dubanowska,Neo Christopher Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LoRA 等方法在低秩映射中存在的非等距和结构扭曲问题，GPart 通过全局等距随机投影将参数直接映射至全权重空间，在提升效率的同时消除了低秩结构的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 的双线性结构导致从可训练参数到权重更新的映射并非距离保持（非等距），从而扭曲了优化地形；现有方法仍未能解决这一核心瓶颈。

Method: 提出 GPart（全局分区微调），弃用了 LoRA 的双线性结构，采用一个单一的等距分区矩阵，将 d 维可训练向量直接映射到模型的完整权重空间，仅需保存 d 个值的向量和一个随机种子。

Result: GPart 在自然语言理解、计算机视觉任务和数学推理任务上，实现了与现有 PEFT 方法持平或更优的性能，同时在存储开销上达到了极致的简洁。

Conclusion: GPart 通过消除低秩矩阵结构约束，利用全局等距投影实现了高效的参数微调，证明了在无需强加低秩结构的前提下，通过随机低维子空间即可实现有效的模型适配，为 PEFT 提供了一种更简洁高效的范式。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become the dominant paradigm for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of large language models (LLMs). However, its bilinear structure introduces a critical limitation: the mapping from trainable parameters to weight updates is not distance-preserving, distorting the optimization landscape. Methods that project a low-dimensional vector into LoRA's parameter space, such as Uni-LoRA, improve parameter efficiency, but the subsequent bilinear LoRA map breaks end-to-end isometry, leaving the core distance-preservation problem unresolved. We propose GPart (Global Partition fine-tuning), a highly parameter-efficient fine-tuning method which removes the low-rank bottleneck entirely. Our method uses a single isometric partition matrix to map a $d$-dimensional trainable vector directly into the full weight space of the model. The result is an extremely minimal fine-tuning pipeline: one random projection, end-to-end isometric, with a single clean hyperparameter ($d$) and storage cost of $d+1$ values (the trainable vector plus a random seed). GPart builds on the theoretical premise that effective fine-tuning can emerge from random low-dimensional subspaces of the full weight space, without imposing low-rank matrix structure. We empirically demonstrate the superior or comparable performance of GPart to existing PEFT methods on natural language understanding, computer vision tasks, and mathematical reasoning. Overall, GPart achieves state-of-the-art efficiency and performance by removing structural constraints, offering a straightforward and elegant path to PEFT.

</details>


### [227] [Exploitation of Hidden Context in Dynamic Movement Forecasting: A Neural Network Journey from Recurrent to Graph Neural Networks and General Purpose Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14855)
*Lukas Schelenz,Shobha Rajanna,Denis Gosalci,Lucas Heublein,Jonas Pirkl,Jonathan Ott,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Tobias Feigl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究评估了各类模型在 NBA 球员轨迹预测中的表现，发现引入环境上下文的混合 LSTM 模型在精度与训练效率上优于 GAT 和 Transformers，并指出模型选择应基于具体的时序预测任务需求进行权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性方法难以处理体育运动中非线性的突发轨迹变化；现有深度学习方法虽灵活但往往忽视了时序依赖与复杂环境上下文之间的关键交互，导致在 NBA 此类高动态环境下的预测准确性受限。

Method: 对比评估了包括传统方法（ARIMA, KF, PF）与机器学习方法（LSTM, GNNs, Transformers）在内的多种模型。提出并验证了一种引入上下文信息的混合 LSTM 模型，并分析了输入历史长度、泛化能力及上下文融合对性能的影响。

Result: 引入上下文信息的混合 LSTM 模型在 2 秒视距内的预测表现最佳，最终位移误差（FDE）最低（1.51m），且相比 GAT 和 Transformers 具有更高的训练效率与更低的数据需求。机器学习方法整体优于线性模型。

Conclusion: 在快节奏、动态的 NBA 比赛轨迹预测任务中，不存在单一的最优架构。模型选择需根据具体应用场景（如精度要求、计算资源、数据可用性）进行权衡。该研究强调了结合时序依赖与环境上下文信息的必要性。

Abstract: Forecasting within signal processing pipelines is crucial for mitigating delays, particularly in predicting the dynamic movements of objects such as NBA players. This task poses significant challenges due to the inherently interactive and unpredictable nature of sports, where abrupt changes in velocity and direction are prevalent. Traditional approaches, including (S)ARIMA(X), Kalman filters (KF), and Particle filters (PF), often struggle to model the non-linear dynamics present in such scenarios. Machine learning (ML) methods, such as long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, graph neural networks (GNNs), and Transformers, offer greater flexibility and accuracy but frequently fail to explicitly capture the interplay between temporal dependencies and contextual interactions, which are critical in chaotic sports environments. In this paper, we evaluate these models and assess their strengths and weaknesses. Experimental results reveal key performance trade-offs across input history length, generalizability, and the ability to incorporate contextual information. ML-based methods demonstrated substantial improvements over linear models across forecast horizons of up to 2s. Among the tested architectures, our hybrid LSTM augmented with contextual information achieved the lowest final displacement error (FDE) of 1.51m, outperforming temporal convolutional neural network (TCNN), graph attention network (GAT), and Transformers, while also requiring less data and training time compared to GAT and Transformers. Our findings indicate that no single architecture excels across all metrics, emphasizing the need for task-specific considerations in trajectory prediction for fast-paced, dynamic environments such as NBA gameplay.

</details>


### [228] [REALM: Retrospective Encoder Alignment for LFP Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14867)
*Peicheng Wu,Zhenyu Bu,Runze Ma,Lin Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为 REALM 的回顾性蒸馏框架，将离线、高性能的双向 LFP 解码模型知识转化为紧凑的因果模型，实现了高效、高精度的实时神经解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 峰值信号带宽要求高，限制了高通道数无线 BCI 的发展；现有的 LFP 解码模型精度较低且多依赖于无法实时部署的非因果架构。

Method: 提出了一种名为 REALM 的回顾性蒸馏框架：1. 使用掩码自编码目标预训练一个双向 Mamba-2 教师模型；2. 通过结合表示对齐和任务监督的目标，将教师模型蒸馏为一个紧凑的因果（实时）学生模型。

Result: REALM 在行为解码任务中持续优于现有因果与非因果 LFP 解码 SOTA 方法；同时实现了参数量减少 2 倍、训练时间缩短 10 倍。

Conclusion: REALM 通过回顾性蒸馏技术有效弥补了离线与实时神经解码之间的性能差异，证明了基于 LFP 的模型无需依赖峰值信号即可实现具有竞争力的解码性能，为下一代无线植入式 BCI 提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Spike activity has been the dominant neural signal for behavior decoding due to its high spatial and temporal resolution. However, as brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) move toward high channel counts and wireless operation, the high sampling frequency of spike signals becomes a bottleneck due to high power and bandwidth requirements. Local field potentials (LFPs) represent a different spatial-temporal scale of brain activity compared to spikes, offering key advantages including improved long-term stability, reduced energy consumption, and lower bandwidth requirement. Despite these benefits, LFP-based decoding models typically show reduced accuracy and often rely on non-causal architectures that are unsuitable for real-time deployment. To address these challenges, we propose REALM: a retrospective distillation framework that enables causal LFP decoding. Inspired by offline-to-online distillation strategies in speech recognition, REALM transfers representational knowledge from a pretrained multi-session bidirectional LFP model to a causal version for real-time deployment. We first pretrain a bidirectional Mamba-2 teacher model using a masked autoencoding objective. We then distill this teacher model into a compact student model via a combined objective of representation alignment and task supervision. REALM consistently outperforms both causal and non-causal LFP-based SOTA methods for behavior decoding. Notably, our REALM improves decoding performance while achieving a $2\times$ reduction in parameter count and a $10\times$ reduction in training time. These results demonstrate that retrospective distillation effectively bridges the gap between offline and real-time neural decoding. REALM shows that LFP-only models can achieve competitive decoding performance without reliance on spike signals, offering a practical and scalable alternative for next-generation wireless implantable BCIs.

</details>


### [229] [TILBench: A Systematic Benchmark for Tabular Imbalanced Learning Across Data Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14915)
*Ruizhe Liu,Jiaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 TILBench，通过对 40 多种不平衡学习算法在 57 个数据集上的大规模实证研究，指出不存在万能算法，并据此为实际应用提供方法选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前针对表格数据不平衡学习缺乏系统的实证研究，尚不清楚不同方法在预测性能、鲁棒性和计算可扩展性方面的对比情况。

Method: 构建了大规模基准测试平台 TILBench，涵盖 40 多种代表性算法和 57 个多样的表格数据集，执行了超过 20 万次受控实验。

Result: 通过大量实验数据揭示了算法表现与数据特性之间的强相关性，并总结了各方法的优缺点。

Conclusion: 不存在单一的最优方法，算法表现高度依赖于具体数据集的统计特征和计算约束。研究提供了针对不同应用场景的选择建议。

Abstract: Imbalanced learning remains a fundamental challenge in tabular data applications. Despite decades of research and numerous proposed algorithms, a systematic empirical understanding of how different imbalanced learning methods behave across diverse data characteristics is still lacking. In particular, it remains unclear how different method families compare in predictive performance, robustness under varying data characteristics, and computational scalability. In this work, we present Tabular Imbalanced Learning Benchmark (TILBench), a large-scale empirical benchmark for tabular imbalanced learning. TILBench evaluates more than 40 representative algorithms across 57 diverse tabular datasets, resulting in over 200000 controlled experiments across a wide range of data characteristics. Our findings show that no single method consistently dominates across all settings; instead, the effectiveness of imbalanced learning methods depends strongly on dataset characteristics and computational constraints. Based on these findings, we provide practical recommendations for selecting appropriate methods in real-world applications.

</details>


### [230] [Octopus: History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization for Continual Learning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14938)
*Yuehao Liu,Shanyan Guan,Weijia Zhang,Xuanming Shang,Yanhao Ge,Wei Li,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态大模型的持续学习困境，Octopus 提出了一种无需历史数据的梯度正交化（HiFGO）框架，通过两阶段微调策略，在保证隐私和计算效率的同时，显著缓解了灾难性遗忘并提升了模型在新旧任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有持续学习方法在 MLLMs 中的局限性：架构方法计算开销大、泛化差；回放方法存在隐私与存储问题；常规正则化方法在防止参数干扰方面效果不足。

Method: 提出名为 Octopus 的两阶段持续学习框架，核心技术为“无历史梯度正交化”（HiFGO），通过解耦任务适配与正则化，在没有历史数据的前提下强制执行梯度级别的正交约束。

Result: 在 UCIT 基准测试中达到最先进（SOTA）水平，相比前作平均性能（Avg）提升 2.14%，最终任务性能（Last）提升 6.82%。

Conclusion: Octopus 框架通过 HiFGO 技术证明了在无需历史数据的情况下，通过梯度正交化能有效缓解多模态大模型的灾难性遗忘，在保持模型可塑性的同时提升了稳定性。

Abstract: Continual learning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) aims to sequentially acquire knowledge while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, yet existing methods face inherent limitations: architecture-based approaches incur additional computational overhead and often generalize poorly to new tasks, rehearsal-based methods rely on storing historical data, raising privacy and storage concerns, and conventional regularization-based strategies alone are insufficient to fully prevent parameter interference. We propose Octopus, a two-stage continual learning framework based on History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization (HiFGO), which enforces gradient-level orthogonality without historical task data. Our proposed two-stage finetuning strategy decouples task adaptation from regularization, achieving a principled balance between plasticity and stability. Experiments on UCIT show that Octopus establishes state-of-the-art performance, surpassing prior SOTA by 2.14% and 6.82% in terms of Avg and Last.

</details>


### [231] [Not All Symbols Are Equal: Importance-Aware Constellation Design for Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14940)
*Albert Shaju,Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Mayukh Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种联合语义-物理层的通信框架，通过动态分配星座图符号位置优先保护高价值语义信息，实现了比传统星座调制更优的抗干扰性能和传输效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信将语义编码与物理层星座设计解耦，导致任务关键信息与无关信息受到同等程度的信道干扰，无法实现最优的目标导向传输。

Method: 1. 采用VQ-VAE提取离散潜在概念；2. 引入语义关键性指标（SCI）评估任务相关性；3. 利用深度强化学习（DRL）动态选择传输子集；4. 设计语义感知M-QAM星座图，根据语义权重和共现统计信息动态映射符号位置。

Result: 在大规模调制（4-QAM到1024-QAM）下实现了近100%的语义保护概率（SPP），优于传统方案的50%；实现了21:1的压缩比，且在多个权威数据集（MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, FSDD）上表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 提出了一种联合语义-物理层框架，证明了在存在非均匀语义重要性的情况下，传统的格雷码调制方案在语义保护方面是次优的。所提方案在保证高压缩比和语义质量的同时，显著提高了任务关键信息的抗干扰能力。

Abstract: Semantic communication systems for goal-oriented transmission must protect task-relevant information not only through source compression but also via physical layer mapping. Existing approaches decouple constellation design and semantic encoding, exposing critical symbols to channel errors at the same rate as irrelevant ones. Contrary to this, in this paper, a joint semantic-physical layer framework is proposed, which is composed of a vector quantized-variational autoencoder that extracts discrete latent concepts, a semantic criticality indicator (SCI) that scores each concept by task relevance, and a deep reinforcement learning agent that dynamically selects the transmission subset based on instantaneous channel conditions. At the physical layer, a learned semantic-aware M -QAM constellation assigns symbol positions according to joint co-occurrence statistics and SCI scores, departing from the uniform spacing and Gray coding of standard M -QAM which minimizes average BER without regard for semantic content. We introduce a novel semantic symbol vulnerability (SSV) metric and a semantic protection probability (SPP) to quantify the exposure of task-critical symbols to decoding errors, and prove that any Gray-coded constellation is strictly suboptimal in SCI-Weighted SSV whenever the source exhibits non-uniform semantic importance and co-occurrence statistics. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed constellation achieves near 100% SPP across modulation orders from 4-QAM to 1024-QAM versus 50% for standard constellations at high spectral efficiency, a 21:1 compression ratio with semantic quality above 0.9, generalizing across MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and FSDD without modification.

</details>


### [232] [Efficient Online Conformal Selection with Limited Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14953)
*Sreenivas Gollapudi,Kostas Kollias,Kamesh Munagala,Ali Sinop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了简单的自适应共形推断（ACI）规则在复杂的bandit feedback环境下，既能保证预测的有效性，又能实现最优的选择效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在在线共形预测中，如何在仅有有限（bandit）反馈的情况下，在满足预定成功率（覆盖率）的同时，最小化选择的集合大小（提高效率）。

Method: 利用Lyapunov函数分析框架，将自适应共形推断（ACI）应用于控制参数或对偶变量更新，通过统一的算法技术处理bandit和semi-bandit反馈场景。

Result: 证明了ACI在任意输入序列下是对抗性有效的（满足覆盖率约束），且在i.i.d.输入下具有次线性的效率悔值，优于现有方法，且对反馈量的需求更低。

Conclusion: 在受限反馈（如截断式/部分反馈）环境下，ACI更新规则在保证对抗性效力（保证覆盖概率）的同时，实现了针对随机输入的次线性效率悔值（stochastic efficiency regret），为在线机器学习与无分布不确定性量化建立了理论联系。

Abstract: We address the problem of conformal selection, where an agent must select a minimal subset of options to ensure that at least one ``success'' is identified with a pre-specified target probability $φ$. While traditional online conformal prediction focuses on maintaining validity for the observed sequence, minimizing the resource cost (efficiency) of such selections, especially under limited feedback, remains a significant challenge. In this work, we consider settings with the most limited ``bandit'' feedback, and demonstrate that the simple Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI) update rule, when applied to the appropriate control parameter or dual variable, is both adversarially valid, ensuring the success target is met on average for any input sequence (and hence under distribution shifts), and stochastically efficient, achieving sublinear efficiency regret for $i.i.d.$ inputs against an appropriate stochastic benchmark. We show such guarantees under canonical models capturing bandit and semi-bandit feedback to the agent via a unifying algorithmic technique, and analytic framework involving Lyapunov functions. Our approach handles more complex settings than prior work, while requiring significantly less feedback, and our results provide a new theoretical bridge between efficient online learning with limited feedback and distribution-free uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [233] [Distance-Matrix Wasserstein Statistics for Scalable Gromov--Wasserstein Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14981)
*Ao Xu,Tieru Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Distance-Matrix Wasserstein (DMW) 来近似 GW 距离，通过比较随机采样点对的距离矩阵分布替代复杂的全局点对齐，既解决了 GW 的大规模计算难题，又提供了明确的理论收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) 距离计算复杂（非凸二次优化问题），在处理大规模数据集时难以扩展。

Method: 提出 Distance-Matrix Wasserstein (DMW) 方法，通过对原始空间进行随机采样，比较采样点间距离矩阵的分布（而非直接对齐点），并引入了 Sliced 和 Multi-scale 优化以提升计算效率。

Result: 理论证明了 DMW 是 GW 的松弛和下界，并给出了收敛性分析和有限样本误差界；实验验证了该方法在大规模基准测试、图分类和两样本检验中的高效性和准确性。

Conclusion: DMW 提供了一种可扩展、具有理论保证且无需显式全局对齐的 GW 距离有效替代方案，尤其适用于大规模图数据和几何形状的比较分析。

Abstract: Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) distances compare graphs, shapes, and point clouds through internal distances, without requiring a common coordinate system. This invariance is powerful, but discrete GW is a nonconvex quadratic optimal transport problem and is difficult to estimate at scale. We propose \emph{Distance-Matrix Wasserstein} (DMW), a hierarchy of Wasserstein statistics comparing laws of random finite distance matrices. Rather than optimizing a global point-level alignment, DMW samples $n$ points from each space, records their pairwise distances, and transports the resulting matrix laws. We prove that DMW is a relaxation and lower bound of GW, and establish a reverse approximation inequality: the GW--DMW gap is controlled by the Wasserstein error of approximating each original measure with $n$ samples. Hence population DMW converges to GW as sampled subspaces become dense. We further give finite-sample bounds, including intrinsic-dimensional rates that depend on the data manifold rather than the ambient matrix dimension $\binom n2$. For scalable computation, we introduce sliced and multi-scale DMW; for $p=1$, the sliced multi-scale dissimilarity yields positive-definite exponential kernels. Experiments on synthetic metric spaces, scalability benchmarks, graph classification, and two-sample testing validate the theory and demonstrate an interpretable GW-style proxy for structural comparison.

</details>


### [234] [Second-Order Actor-Critic Methods for Discounted MDPs via Policy Hessian Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14982)
*Sanjeev Manivannan,Shuban V*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双时间尺度的二阶执行者-评论家强化学习算法，通过HVP技术克服了传统二阶方法在计算和稳定性上的障碍，实现了更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决强化学习中传统的一阶策略梯度方法收敛慢的问题，以及二阶优化方法在处理复杂价值函数近似时面临的计算复杂度高和稳定性差的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种利用黑塞矩阵-向量积（HVP）的二阶执行者-评论家（Actor-Critic）算法，该算法采用双时间尺度框架（快尺度评论家、慢尺度执行者），有效缓解了传统二阶方法在强化学习中的计算复杂度和不稳定性问题。

Result: 该算法成功结合了二阶优化加速收敛的优势和HVP计算的计算效率，在折扣奖励设置下实现了更准确且稳定的参数更新。

Conclusion: 通过双时间尺度框架，证明了在执行参与者更新时将动作价值函数视为拟恒定是合理的，从而消除了二阶更新中的不稳定性，实现了一种高效且稳定的二阶执行者-评论家算法。

Abstract: We address the discounted reward setting in reinforcement learning (RL). To mitigate the value approximation challenges in policy gradient methods, actor-critic approaches have been developed and are known to converge to stationary points under suitable assumptions. However, these methods rely on first-order updates. In contrast, second-order optimization provides principled curvature-aware updates that are proven to accelerate convergence, but its application in RL is limited by the computational complexity of Hessian estimation. In this work, we analyze second-order approximations for the actor update that leverage the full curvature information of the objective as much as possible. A stable approximation requires treating the action-value function as locally constant with respect to policy parameters, which does not generally hold in policy gradient methods. We show that this approximation becomes well-justified under a two-timescale actor-critic framework, where the critic evolves on a faster timescale and can be treated as quasi-stationary during actor updates. Building on this insight, we formulate a second-order actor-critic method for the discounted reward setting that leverages Hessian-vector product (HVP) computations, resulting in a computationally efficient and stable second-order update.

</details>


### [235] [DeepTokenEEG Enhancing Mild Cognitive Impairment and Alzheimers Classification via Tokenized EEG Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15009)
*Thinh Nguyen-Quang,Minh Long Ngo,Ngoc-Son Nguyen,Nguyen Thanh Vinh,Huy-Dung Han,Bui Thanh Tung,Nguyen Quang Linh,Khuong Vo,Manoj Vishwanath,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种名为 DeepTokenEEG 的轻量化模型（0.29M 参数），通过时空分词技术有效提升了基于 EEG 的阿尔茨海默病诊断精度，在测试集上达到了 100% 的准确率，兼具高效能与部署便捷性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决阿尔茨海默病（AD）早期诊断中面临的数据可用性限制、现有深度学习方法性能不足以及人工解读耗时的问题，寻求一种非侵入式、低成本且高效的医疗解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种名为 DeepTokenEEG 的模型，引入了空间和时间分词器（spatial and temporal tokenizer），旨在捕捉时域和频域中与阿尔茨海默病相关的生物标志物，且总参数量仅为 0.29 百万。

Result: 该模型在包含 274 名受试者（180 名 AD 患者，94 名健康对照）的数据集上进行了训练，在特定频段上实现了 100% 的最高分类准确率，较现有最先进方法提高了 1.41-15.35%。

Conclusion: DeepTokenEEG 是一种轻量级、高性能的阿尔茨海默病诊断模型，因其紧凑的参数规模，在临床早期检测和大规模筛查中具有极高的应用前景。

Abstract: The detection of Alzheimers disease (AD) is considered crucial, as timely intervention can improve patient outcomes. Electroencephalogram (EEG)-based diagnosis has been recognized as a non-invasive, accessible, and cost-effective approach for AD detection; however, it faces challenges related to data availability, accuracy of modern deep learning methods, and the time-consuming nature of expert-based interpretation. In this study, a novel lightweight and high-performance model, DeepTokenEEG, was designed for the diagnosis of AD and the classification of EEG signals from AD patients, individuals with other neurological conditions, and healthy subjects. Unlike traditional heavy-weight models, DeepTokenEEG ultilizes spatial and temporal tokenizer that effectively captures AD-related biomarkers in both temporal and frequency domain with only 0.29 million paramaters. Trained in a combined dataset of 274 subjects, including 180 AD cases, and 94 healthy controls, the proposed method achieves a maximum recorded accuracy of 100% on specific frequency bands, representing an improvement of 1.41-15.35% over state-of-the-art methods on the same dataset. These results indicate the potential of DeepTokenEEG for early detection and screening of AD, with promising applicability for deployment due to its compact size.

</details>


### [236] [Generalized Priority-Aware Shapley Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15018)
*Kiljae Lee,Ziqi Liu,Weijing Tang,Yuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GPASV，突破了传统优先感知 Shapley 值对数据必须无环的限制，通过引入加权惩罚机制，实现了对复杂循环优先级场景（如 LLM 竞技场）下的样本贡献评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优先感知 Shapley 值方法要求优先关系必须是二元且无环的（DAG），无法处理实际应用中（如人类行为偏好、多标准评估）常见的循环和复杂冲突关系。

Method: 提出广义优先感知 Shapley 值 (GPASV)，采用随机排序值框架，将二元无环限制放宽为带有惩罚机制的任意加权有向图；提供公理化刻画、计算方法及优先扫描诊断工具。

Result: 成功将 GPASV 应用于 Chatbot Arena 的循环偏好图评估，证明了该方法能够有效量化 LLM 集合中各模型的贡献，并揭示了图中加权边与个体软优先权之间的权衡对估值结果的决定性影响。

Conclusion: GPASV 扩展了 Shapley 值在复杂优先关系下的应用，证明了在存在环路和冲突权重的数据集（如模型竞技场）中，优先权感知估值并非单一结果，其灵敏度受参数平衡的影响。

Abstract: Shapley value and its priority-aware extensions are widely used for valuation in machine learning, but existing methods require pairwise priority to be binary and acyclic, a restriction spectacularly violated in real-data examples such as aggregated human preferences and multi-criterion comparisons. We introduce the generalized priority-aware Shapley value (GPASV), a random order value defined on arbitrary directed weighted priority graphs, in which pairwise edges penalize rather than forbid order violations. GPASV covers a range of classical models as boundary cases. We establish GPASV through an axiomatic characterization, develop the associated computational methods, and introduce a priority sweeping diagnostic extending PASV's. We apply GPASV to LLM ensemble valuation on the cyclic Chatbot Arena preference graph, illustrating that priority-aware valuation is not a one-button operation: different balances of pairwise graph priority versus individual soft priority produce substantively different valuations of the same data.

</details>


### [237] [Separating Intrinsic Ambiguity from Estimation Uncertainty in Deep Generative Models for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15050)
*Yuxin Guo,Dongrui Deng,Pulkit Grover*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对深度生成模型在反问题中不确定性难以解释的问题，提出了一种结构分解方法，通过分离固有模糊性与推断不确定性，提升了高风险领域模型的可解释性与校准精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度生成模型在反问题中的后验不确定性难以解释，因为它混淆了前向算子的固有模糊性和推断过程中的不确定性，而在医疗影像等高风险领域，准确理解这种不确定性至关重要。

Method: 提出一种后验不确定性的结构分解方法，结合级联公式（cascade formulation）实现模糊性分离，并引入模拟驱动的校准测试（simulation-based calibration tests）进行定性诊断。

Result: 成功在具有解析后验结构的Gaussian示例中进行了验证，并在加速核磁共振成像（MRI）和脑电图（EEG）源成像任务中证明了该方法能够揭示仅靠重构指标无法发现的模型失效模式。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种分解后验不确定性的框架，该框架能够有效区分模型不确定性与固有的物理模糊性，并通过校准分析改进了模型诊断，解决了仅凭重构质量选择模型导致的潜在风险。

Abstract: Recently, deep generative models have been used for posterior inference in inverse problems, including high-stakes applications in medical imaging and scientific discovery, where the uncertainty of a prediction can matter as much as the prediction itself. However, posterior uncertainty is difficult to interpret because it can mix ambiguity inherent to the forward operator with uncertainty propagated through inference. We introduce a structural decomposition of posterior uncertainty that isolates intrinsic ambiguity. A cascade formulation makes this ambiguity accessible for calibration analysis, enabling qualitative diagnostics and simulation-based calibration tests that reveal failure modes that remain hidden when models are selected by reconstruction quality alone. We first validate the approach on a Gaussian example with analytical posterior structure, then illustrate the decomposition on accelerated magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), and finally apply the calibration diagnostics to electroencephalography (EEG) source imaging.

</details>


### [238] [TFGN: Task-Free, Replay-Free Continual Pre-Training Without Catastrophic Forgetting at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15053)
*Anurup Ganguli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TFGN架构，通过独特的读/写参数更新分解技术，解决了大语言模型在异构数据持续预训练中的灾难性遗忘问题，并实现了高效的跨域迁移与自主规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大模型在无重放、无任务标签、资源受限等挑战下，进行异构文本域持续预训练时的灾难性遗忘问题，克服现有方法（重放缓冲区、惩罚项等）扩展性差的缺陷。

Method: 提出TFGN架构，即一种针对Transformer语言模型的覆盖设计（architectural overlay）。它采用“读/写分解”策略：前向计算保持全密集（dense）模式，而跨域参数更新被结构化地限制在特定子空间，确保先验域的知识空间不被重写。同时引入了闭环元控制层和算子级规划向量作为扩展。

Result: 在六个异构文本域、多种模型规模和预训练模式下，TFGN在无需重放和任务标识的情况下，实现了极低的后向迁移损失（如LLaMA 3.1 8B为-0.007），保留了极高的评估基准性能，展现了出色的正向跨域迁移能力，且梯度分离效果（L2-orthogonal）达到99.59%以上。

Conclusion: TFGN通过读/写分解机制，首次在大语言模型规模下同时实现了防遗忘、闭环自主学习和算子级潜在规划，为解决大模型在异构域上的持续预训练难题提供了高效架构方案。

Abstract: Continually pre-training a large language model on heterogeneous text domains, without replay or task labels, has remained an unsolved architectural problem at LLM scale. Existing methods rely on replay buffers, task identifiers, regularization penalties that scale poorly, or sentence-classification-scale evaluation.
  We introduce TFGN, an architectural overlay for transformer language models that produces input-conditioned, parameter-efficient updates while leaving the rest of the transformer unchanged. On six heterogeneous text domains (Prose, Python, Math, Biomedical, Chinese, JavaScript) at 1B tokens per phase across three model scales (~398M, ~739M, ~9B) and two regimes (From-Scratch and Retrofit), TFGN achieves backward transfer of -0.007 at LLaMA 3.1 8B Retrofit, HellaSwag retention 0.506/0.504/0.510, and >=99.59% L2-orthogonal gradient separation between domain pairs - with no replay, no task IDs, no Fisher penalty.
  The same matrices show positive cross-domain forward transfer: held-out JavaScript PPL drops 26.8% at LLaMA-8B Retrofit and 62.0% at GPT-2 Medium From-Scratch purely from Python training. Two extensions on the same substrate close further open problems. A closed-loop meta-control layer (Extension A) reduces forgetting by an additional 81% at ~398M, mapping onto the System A and System M roles of Dupoux et al. (arXiv:2603.15381). An operator-level plan vector (Extension B) reshapes forward-pass behavior at 99.96% cosine fidelity over 30 source->target pairs.
  The architectural insight is a Read/Write decomposition: the forward pass is fully dense, while cross-domain parameter updates are structured so prior-domain subspaces are not written to. To our knowledge, TFGN is the first architecture that simultaneously closes catastrophic forgetting at LLM scale, realizes a closed-loop autonomous-learning meta-controller, and carries an operator-level latent planner.

</details>


### [239] [DiffusionOPD: A Unified Perspective of On-Policy Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15055)
*Quanhao Li,Junqiu Yu,Kaixun Jiang,Yujie Wei,Zhen Xing,Pandeng Li,Ruihang Chu,Shiwei Zhang,Yu Liu,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiffusionOPD，一种通过独立训练教师模型再在线蒸馏至统一学生模型的扩散模型多任务训练范式，解决了传统多任务RL中的优化冲突与遗忘问题，并推导了基于均值匹配的闭式梯度计算方法以提升训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习（RL）优化扩散模型的方法多局限于单任务，而将RL扩展至多任务时存在联合优化干扰、任务不平衡、级联训练繁琐及灾难性遗忘等挑战。

Method: 提出了一种名为DiffusionOPD的多任务训练范式：1) 先独立训练各任务专家（Teacher）；2) 将专家能力通过在线策略蒸馏（Online Policy Distillation）整合至统一学生模型（Student）；3) 在理论上将OPD扩展至连续状态马尔可夫决策过程，推导出了闭式（closed-form）每步KL散度目标函数，并通过均值匹配统一了SDE与ODE微调方法。

Result: 实验表明，DiffusionOPD在训练效率和最终模型性能上均优于现有的多奖励RL和级联RL基线方法，在所有评估基准上均达到了最先进（SOTAs）的水平。

Conclusion: DiffusionOPD通过在线策略蒸馏成功实现了扩散模型的多任务优化，既克服了联合训练中的干扰与不平衡问题，也避免了级联RL带来的遗忘难题，为多目标扩散模型训练提供了一种高效且高性能的统一范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as a powerful tool for improving diffusion-based text-to-image models, but existing methods are largely limited to single-task optimization. Extending RL to multiple tasks is challenging: joint optimization suffers from cross-task interference and imbalance, while cascade RL is cumbersome and prone to catastrophic forgetting. We propose DiffusionOPD, a new multi-task training paradigm for diffusion models based on Online Policy Distillation (OPD). DiffusionOPD first trains task-specific teachers independently, then distills their capabilities into a unified student along the student own rollout trajectories. This decouples single-task exploration from multi-task integration and avoids the optimization burden of solving all tasks jointly from scratch. Theoretically, we lift the OPD framework from discrete tokens to continuous-state Markov processes, deriving a closed-form per-step KL objective that unifies both stochastic SDE and deterministic ODE refinement via mean-matching. We formally and empirically demonstrate that this analytic gradient provides lower variance and better generality compared to conventional PPO-style policy gradients. Extensive experiments show that DiffusionOPD consistently surpasses both multi-reward RL and cascade RL baselines in training efficiency and final performance, while achieving state-of-the-art results on all evaluated benchmarks.

</details>


### [240] [Learning from Language Feedback via Variational Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15113)
*Yang Li,Erik Nijkamp,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Rayhan Joty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过变分EM框架实现教师模型与学生模型的协同进化，解决了语言反馈中固定教师导致的认知瓶颈，从而提升了复杂任务中的强化学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于语言反馈的RLVR自我蒸馏方法依赖于固定、被动的教师模型，随着学生能力的提升，教师模型进入零样本能力瓶颈，导致学习停滞。因此需要一种能够随学生共同进化的机制来提供高质量的奖励监督。

Method: 提出变分策略蒸馏（VPD）框架，将从语言反馈中学习形式化为变分EM问题。在E步，通过自适应信任域更新对教师模型进行改进，将文本反馈转化为动态改进的目标token分布；在M步，学生模型在策略展开过程中内化这种密集的分布引导。

Result: 在科学推理和代码生成任务中，VPD持续优于标准RLVR和现有的自我蒸馏基线；通过对数学推理和冷启动场景的压力测试，证明了VPD在利用反馈信号方面的有效性。

Conclusion: VPD通过将教师策略与学生策略协同进化，有效克服了被动式蒸馏带来的性能瓶颈，在科学推理和代码生成任务中均优于标准RLVR和现有的自我蒸馏基线。同时，该研究揭示了基于反馈的蒸馏与纯环境驱动RL相比的性能边界。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) suffers from sparse outcome signals, creating severe exploration bottlenecks on complex reasoning tasks. Recent on-policy self-distillation methods attempt to address this by utilizing language feedback to generate dense, token-level supervision. However, these approaches rely on a fixed, passive teacher to interpret the feedback. As the student policy improves, the teacher's zero-shot assessment capabilities plateau, ultimately halting further learning. To overcome this, we propose Variational Policy Distillation (VPD), a framework that formalizes learning from language feedback as a Variational Expectation-Maximization (EM) problem. VPD co-evolves both policies: in the E-step, the teacher is actively refined on trajectory outcomes via an adaptive trust-region update, translating textual feedback into a dynamically improved target token distribution. In the M-step, the student internalizes this dense distributional guidance on its own on-policy rollouts. By continuously improving the teacher's ability to extract actionable signals from textual critique, VPD overcomes the limitations of passive distillation. Evaluated across diverse sources of diagnostic feedback on scientific reasoning and code generation tasks, VPD consistently outperforms both standard RLVR and existing self-distillation baselines. Finally, by stress-testing our framework on rigid mathematical reasoning and cold-start regimes, we illuminate the fundamental bounds of feedback-driven self-distillation compared to pure environment-driven RL.

</details>


### [241] [Natural Synthesis: Outperforming Reactive Synthesis Tools with Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15131)
*Frederik Schmitt,Matthias Cosler,Niklas Metzger,Julian Siber,Vladimir Krsmanovic,Mohamed Ghanem,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大模型与模型检测器的神经符号反应式合成方法，实现了从自然语言描述自动生成硬件电路，降低了规范编写难度并提升了系统合成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决反应式合成中的两大痛点：一是算法计算难度极大；二是人工编写形式化逻辑规范（temporal logic）的门槛过高、易出错，难以推广。

Method: 提出了一种神经符号（neuro-symbolic）方法：结合大语言推理模型与模型检测器（model checker），通过基于符号反馈的迭代机制来修复生成的 Verilog 实现；同时引入自动形式化（autoformalization）步骤，实现从自然语言到形式化逻辑的转换。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在反应式合成基准测试中的胜出率优于当前最优工具；能够处理参数化系统合成；且以自然语言作为输入的效果可媲美形式化规范输入。

Conclusion: 该研究成功验证了神经符号方法在反应式合成中的有效性，证明了将自然语言作为规范输入是实现端到端合成的可行方案。该方法不仅在基准测试中超越了现有专用工具，还为解决参数化系统合成这一 undecidable 问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reactive synthesis, the problem of automatically constructing a hardware circuit from a logical specification, is a long-standing challenge in formal verification. It is elusive for two reasons: It is algorithmically hard, and writing formal specifications by hand is notoriously difficult. In this paper, we tackle both sides of the problem. For the algorithmic side, we present a neuro-symbolic approach to reactive synthesis that couples large reasoning models with model checkers to iteratively repair a synthesized Verilog implementation via sound symbolic feedback. Our approach solves more benchmarks than the best dedicated tools in the annual synthesis competition and extends to constructing parameterized systems, a problem known to be undecidable. On the specification side, we introduce an autoformalization step that shifts the specification task from temporal logic to natural language by introducing a hand-authored dataset of natural-language specifications for evaluation. We demonstrate performance comparable to that of starting from formal specifications, establishing natural synthesis as a viable end-to-end workflow.

</details>


### [242] [Causal Foundation Models with Continuous Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15133)
*Christopher Stith,Medha Barath,Vahid Balazadeh,Jesse C. Cresswell,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对连续处理设置的因果基础模型，通过Transformer架构实现对处理-反应曲线的高效重构，具备强大的跨任务泛化与元学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果推断方法多集中于二元处理设置，而连续处理设置在多学科中具有重要意义且研究不足，缺乏能够跨任务处理连续处理变量的通用模型。

Method: 1. 设计了一种针对连续处理变量的数据生成过程先验，构建了丰富的因果训练语料库；2. 采用基于Transformer的架构，结合上下文学习（in-context learning）对个体处理-反应曲线进行重构，实现了贝叶斯后验推断的摊销。

Result: 该模型成功实现了因果效应的元学习，能够在未经额外训练或微调的情况下处理多种不可见任务，并在个体处理-反应曲线重构任务中超越了针对特定任务训练的传统因果模型。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的基于Transformer的基础模型在连续处理设置下的个体处理-反应曲线重构任务中达到了最先进的性能，证明了无需额外微调的元学习因果推断能力。

Abstract: Causal inference, estimating causal effects from observational data, is a fundamental tool in many disciplines. Of particular importance across a variety of domains is the continuous treatment setting, where the variable of intervention has a continuous range. This setting is far less explored and represents a substantial shift from the binary treatment setting, with models needing to represent effects across a continuum of treatment values. In this paper, we present the first causal foundation model for the continuous treatment setting. Our model meta-learns the ability to predict causal effects across a wide variety of unseen tasks without additional training or fine-tuning. First, we design a novel prior over data-generating processes with continuous treatment variables in order to generate a rich causal training corpus. We then train a transformer to reconstruct individual treatment-response curves given only observational data, leveraging in-context learning to amortize expensive Bayesian posterior inference. Our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on individual treatment-response curve reconstruction tasks compared to causal models which are trained specifically for those tasks.

</details>


### [243] [Training ML Models with Predictable Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15134)
*Will Schwarzer,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对模型部署规模下失效频率难以估计的问题，分析了现有基于失效得分外推法的偏差，并提出了一种“可预测性损失”微调方法，有效提升了预测准确度并降低了漏报风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习预部署安全性评估受限于验证集规模，难以预测大规模部署环境下的罕见失效事件。如何利用有限的验证集数据更准确地估算部署规模下的失效频率是当前的主要挑战。

Method: 1. 对已有评估规模下失效估计器的预测误差进行有限-k分解；2. 识别并分析该估计器在存在未覆盖的罕见故障模式时的低估倾向；3. 提出一种新的微调目标函数：可预测性损失（forecastability loss），以引导模型优化其失效分布的可预测性。

Result: 实验结果表明，在语言模型密码游戏和强化学习网格世界实验中，使用所提出的微调目标函数能显著降低留出（held-out）失效预测误差，同时维持了原始任务的性能，并达到了与监督学习基准相当的安全性水平。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的“可预测性损失”（forecastability loss）能够有效缓解模型在部署规模下因罕见高故障模式缺失导致的低估风险，在提升预测准确性的同时，不损害主任务性能并保持了良好的安全性。

Abstract: Estimating how often an ML model will fail at deployment scale is central to pre-deployment safety assessment, but a feasible evaluation set is rarely large enough to observe the failures that matter. Jones et al. (2025) address this by extrapolating from the largest k failure scores in an evaluation set to predict deployment-scale failure rates. We give a finite-k decomposition of this estimator's forecast error and show that it has a built-in bias toward over-prediction in the typical case, which is the safety-favorable direction. This bias is offset when the evaluation set misses a rare high-failure mode that the deployment set contains, leaving the forecast to under-predict at deployment scale. We propose a fine-tuning objective, the forecastability loss, that addresses this failure mode. In two proof-of-concept experiments, a language-model password game and an RL gridworld, fine-tuning substantially reduces held-out forecast error while preserving primary-task capability and achieving safety similar to that of supervised baselines.

</details>


### [244] [Widening the Gap: Exploiting LLM Quantization via Outlier Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15152)
*Xiaohua Zhan,Kazuki Egashira,Robin Staab,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种首个通用型量化感知后门攻击，通过注入权重离群值触发“权重坍缩”，成功在AWQ、GPTQ和GGUF等先进量化模型中植入并激活恶意行为，证明了量化部署的广泛安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化感知攻击仅限于简单的量化方法，无法突破AWQ、GPTQ等主流先进量化方案，攻击者的威胁能力受限，因此需要一种通用的、能覆盖广泛量化技术的攻击手段。

Method: 提出了一种基于“权重坍缩”的量化感知的后门攻击，通过向模型注入特定权重离群值（Outliers），利用现代量化算法中离群值会导致其他权重被截断为零的特性，在量化后触发预设的恶意行为。

Result: 该攻击在多种情境和主流LLM上均实现了极高的攻击成功率，成功突破了此前攻击无法攻破的复杂量化方案，验证了其普适性和威胁性。

Conclusion: 量化感知的攻击不再局限于简单的量化方法，通过利用权重离群值诱导权重坍缩（Weight Collapse），该攻击对AWQ、GPTQ和GGUF等主流先进量化方案同样有效，揭示了模型量化部署中普遍存在的安全隐患。

Abstract: LLM quantization has become essential for memory-efficient deployment. Recent work has shown that quantization schemes can pose critical security risks: an adversary may release a model that appears benign in full precision but exhibits malicious behavior once quantized by users. However, existing quantization-conditioned attacks have been limited to relatively simple quantization methods, where the attacker can estimate weight regions that remain invariant under the target quantization. Notably, prior attacks have consistently failed to compromise more popular and sophisticated schemes, limiting their practical impact. In this work, we introduce the first quantization-conditioned attack that consistently induces malicious behavior that can be triggered by a broad range of advanced quantization techniques, including AWQ, GPTQ, and GGUF I-quants. Our attack exploits a simple property shared by many modern quantization methods: large outliers can cause other weights to be rounded to zero. Consequently, by injecting outliers into specific weight blocks, an adversary can therefore induce a targeted, predictable weight collapse in the model. This effect can be used to craft seemingly benign full-precision models that exhibit a wide range of malicious behaviors after quantization. Through extensive evaluation across three attack scenarios and LLMs, we show that our attack achieves high success rates against a broad range of quantization methods on which prior attacks fail. Our results demonstrate, for the first time, that the security risks of quantization are not restricted to simpler schemes but are broadly relevant across complex, widely-used quantization methods.

</details>


### [245] [When Are Two Networks the Same? Tensor Similarity for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15183)
*ML Nissen Gonzalez,Melwina Albuquerque,Laurence Wroe,Jacob Meyer Cohen,Logan Riggs Smith,Thomas Dooms*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对模型机理解释性中“如何验证不同部分执行相同计算”的问题，提出了一种抗对称性干扰的度量方式——“张量相似度”，将机理相似性验证转化为精确的代数计算问题，提升了对模型训练动态的观测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型相似性度量方法存在局限性：一是基于经验行为的方法无法衡量分布外（OOD）机制；二是基于参数的方法过分依赖基底，忽视了权重空间的对称性。如何准确验证模型不同部分是否执行相同的计算成为瓶颈。

Method: 引入了一种基于权重的度量方法——“张量相似度（Tensor Similarity）”。该方法具备对称不变性（symmetry-invariant），能够捕捉全局函数等价性，并利用高效的递归算法处理跨层机制。

Result: 实证研究表明，“张量相似度”在追踪模型训练动态（如Grokking过程、后门植入）方面，比现有指标具有更高的保真度。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种通用的代数评估框架，证明了模型机理相似性可以通过代数方法精确计算，而非依赖于经验性的近似，为模型机理的可解释性验证提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to break models into meaningful parts; verifying that two such parts implement the same computation is a prerequisite. Existing similarity measures evaluate either empirical behaviour, leaving them blind to out-of-distribution mechanisms, or basis-dependent parameters, meaning they disregard weight-space symmetries. To address these issues for the class of tensor-based models, we introduce a weight-based metric, tensor similarity, that is invariant to such symmetries. This metric captures global functional equivalence and accounts for cross-layer mechanisms using an efficient recursive algorithm. Empirically, tensor similarity tracks functional training dynamics, such as grokking and backdoor insertion, with higher fidelity than existing metrics. This reduces measuring similarity and verifying faithfulness into a solved algebraic problem rather than one of empirical approximation.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [Distributed Statistical Zero-Knowledge Proofs via Sumcheck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14015)
*Benjamin Jauregui,Masayuki Miyamoto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过引入分布式统计零知识概念及分布式Sumcheck原语，填补了分布式通用零知识证明的空白，并成功应用于非k-可着色性和子图计数问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式交互式证明发展迅速，但通用的分布式零知识证明技术仍非常有限且多为特定问题专用，缺乏一种模块化的通用解决框架。

Method: 1. 引入分布式统计零知识（DSZK）概念；2. 将经典的Sumcheck协议转化为模态分布式原语；3. 设计了针对多项式F的分布式Sumcheck协议；4. 应用该原语解决非k-可着色性与子图计数问题；5. 利用复杂度理论分析非3-可着色性的轮数下界。

Result: 1. 设计了实现分布式Sumcheck协议，能在$O(N)$轮内完成；2. 实现了非k-可着色性的$O(n)$轮统计零知识证明（首个非平凡解）；3. 优化了子图计数的分布式统计零知识证明（$O(k \log n)$轮）；4. 证明了在特定模型下，非3-可着色性问题的轮数下界为$\Omega(n/\log n)$。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种通用的分布式统计零知识证明框架，并通过将Sumcheck协议分布式化，为非k-可着色性问题和子图计数问题提供了首个或更优的分布式零知识证明方案，同时指出了该协议在特定条件下的轮数下界。

Abstract: We study distributed zero-knowledge proofs, introduced by Bick, Kol, and Oshman (SODA 2022). While distributed interactive proofs have advanced rapidly, general-purpose techniques for distributed zero-knowledge remain limited and mostly problem-specific. We address this gap by introducing distributed statistical zero-knowledge, requiring that each node's view be simulatable within negligible statistical distance, and by lifting the classical Sumcheck protocol (Lund, Fortnow, Karloff, and Nisan, FOCS 1990) into a modular primitive for distributed zero-knowledge proofs.
  Our main contribution is a distributed zero-knowledge implementation of Sumcheck. Given oracle access to a polynomial F over a finite field $\mathbb{F}$ with N variables, we design a protocol verifying claims of the form $\sum_{x\in\mathbb{F}} F(x)=a$ using $O(N)$ rounds of $O(\log |\mathbb{F}|)$-bit messages, while achieving statistical zero-knowledge and small soundness error.
  We apply this primitive to two problems. For non-k-colorability, we obtain an $O(n)$-round distributed statistical zero-knowledge proof deciding whether a graph is not k-colorable, for any constant k, using $O(log^{1+o(1)} n)$-bit messages. This is the first nontrivial distributed interactive proof for this problem, even without zero-knowledge guarantees. For Subgraph Counting, we obtain an $O(k \log n)$-round, $O(k \log n)$-bit distributed statistical zero-knowledge proof for counting copies of a given k-node pattern, improving previous distributed interactive proofs while additionally providing statistical zero-knowledge. Finally, we show that additional round compression of Sumcheck is problem-dependent: for non-3-colorability on constant-degree graphs, we prove a lower bound excluding $o(n/\log n)$ rounds under polynomial-time local computation.

</details>


### [247] [Analysis of wireless network access logs for a hierarchical characterization of user mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14540)
*Francisco Talavera,Isaac Lera,Carlos Guerrero*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Wi-Fi数据的层次化用户移动建模方法，通过对接入点进行分层与聚类用户画像，有效降低了大规模场景下的建模复杂度，适用于雾计算等领域的移动轨迹模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大规模场景下用户移动模型复杂性高、跨场景适配性差的问题，从而为雾计算等场景提供高效的移动性模拟。

Method: 基于Wi-Fi接入点（AP）的地理空间特征进行递归聚类，构建层次化移动模型。利用聚类算法对用户进行画像，并为每类用户建立覆盖区域间的转化矩阵及停留时间向量来模拟用户移动。

Result: 在巴利阿里群岛大学校园数据集上的实验显示，层次化模型相较于非层次化模型显著降低了系统复杂度；模型能较准确地预测用户状态转移，但在时间向量定义上精度不足。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的层次化移动模型能有效降低高尺度场景的建模复杂度，并增强不同地理空间特征场景间的适配性。实验表明，该方法在用户状态转移矩阵的建模上表现良好，但在停留时间向量的精确度方面仍有待提升。

Abstract: This paper presents a method that generates a hierarchical user mobility model from the analysis of the data available from Wi-Fi connections. The data obtained from the Wi-Fi infrastructure is defined in terms of the coverage areas of the access points that the users move through. These access points are recursively grouped into different levels of granularity based on their geospatial features. The track of a user is defined as a sequence of Wi-Fi access points, which is enough to simulate user mobility in, for example, fog scenarios. The hierarchical definition of the region under study is proposed to reduce the complexity of the model in high-scale scenarios and to increase the adaptability between scenarios with different geospatial features. The model creation is based on a user profiling method that uses a clustering algorithm and each user type is defined with a transition matrix between coverage areas and a time length vector for the areas. The method is applied to the case of the campus of the University of the Balearic Islands. From the analysis of the mean square error of the results, we determined that the proposed method obtains good results for the transition matrices, but that the time vector definition should be improved. The results also show lower complexity in the case of the hierarchical model, with one area for each building and three levels, in regard to a non-hierarchical model, with only one area and one level for the whole campus.

</details>


### [248] [Malleable Molecular Dynamics Simulations with GROMACS and DMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14655)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Íñigo Aréjula-Aísa,Berk Hess,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对HPC静态资源分配效率低的问题，该研究通过DMR中间件使GROMACS具备了MPI进程动态伸缩能力，从而有效优化了突发性负载下的资源利用率与运行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HPC的静态资源分配方式在面对时变负载时会导致资源闲置、排队延迟以及高昂的节点小时成本，亟需一种动态调整机制以提高资源利用率。

Method: 1. 提出了动态资源管理软件（DMR），作为Slurm的中间件，通过API解耦实现MPI进程的可塑性；2. 将DMR集成到GROMACS分子动力学引擎中；3. 结合“通信效率感知”的重配置策略与GROMACS原生检查点/重启（Checkpoint/Restart）机制，实现进程数的动态伸缩。

Result: 在MareNostrum 5超级计算机上的评估表明，与静态执行相比，该动态方案在处理突发负载时，能够显著降低计算开销（节点小时成本），并量化了重配置的额外开销与最终时间收益（Time-to-solution）。

Conclusion: 通过在GROMACS中集成DMR，论文证明了在HPC环境中实现MPI进程的可塑性是切实可行的。该方案能有效应对负载波动，在保证计算效率的同时显著降低节点小时成本，并提供了一种不依赖于修改调度器内核的系统优化实现路径。

Abstract: Static resource allocations in high-performance computing (HPC) lead to inefficiencies for time-varying workloads, causing idle resources, queue delays, and higher node-hour costs. The Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) middleware enables MPI process malleability in Slurm via a simple API decoupled from scheduler internals. In this work, we integrate DMR into the GROMACS molecular dynamics engine to obtain a malleable variant that can dynamically adapt its MPI process count by combining communication-efficiency-aware reconfiguration with GROMACS' native checkpoint/restart mechanism. We evaluate this design on the MareNostrum~5 supercomputer, comparing dynamic runs against static executions and quantifying reconfiguration overheads, time-to-solution, and node-hour savings for bursty GROMACS workloads.

</details>


### [249] [Supervised Distributed Computing: Efficiency and Robustness under a Majority of Adversarial Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14784)
*John Augustine,Henning Hillebrandt,Manish Kumar,Christian Scheideler,Julian Werthmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对分布式计算中的容错问题，提出了一种新型监督式计算方案，在允许高达接近 100% 的不可信工作者参与的情况下，通过外包验证机制保证了计算的高效性与健壮性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主从分布式模型中，主管负责验证任务正确性，成为性能瓶颈；而已有的监督式计算方案仅适用于较小的对抗比例（β），需要一种能扩展至任意常数 β < 1 的高效容错模型。

Method: 利用监督式计算范式（Supervised Distributed Computing），由主管（Supervisor）协调任务图执行，并将任务结果的正确性验证外包给工作者（Workers），而非由主管直接进行集中式验证。

Result: 在任意常数 β < 1 的对抗场景下，实现了高效、健壮的分布式计算，诚实工作者的开销接近最优（单次执行），解决了可扩展性和验证瓶颈问题。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种适用于任意常数对抗比例 β < 1 的监督式分布式计算方案，证明了在存在轻量级验证机制的前提下，诚实工作者的期望工作量接近单次任务执行，显著优于现有的容错主从模型及点对点计算方案。

Abstract: We consider a recently proposed \emph{supervised distributed computing} paradigm \cite{augustine2025supervised} that extends and refines the standard master-worker paradigm for parallel computations. In this paradigm, there is a supervisor, a source, a target, and a collection of workers. The distributed computation is given as an acyclic task graph that is known to the supervisor. The source initially stores the input and the target is supposed to store the output of the computation. The individual tasks of the computation are supposed to be executed by the workers under the guidance of the supervisor. The source, target and supervisor are assumed to be reliable, while a $β$-fraction of the workers might be adversarial, for some $β\in [0,1)$. This covers, for example, the case where a supervisor has to work with untrusted volunteers. In the standard master-worker approach, the master checks whether the workers correctly execute the assigned tasks, creating a severe bottleneck, whereas in the supervised approach, the supervisor outsources this checking to the workers. Prior to this work, only supervised solutions were known for the case that $β$ is a sufficiently small constant. We show that robust and efficient supervised solutions are possible for \emph{any} constant $β<1$ while the expected work for the honest workers is close to a \emph{single} execution per task, given that there is a lightweight verification mechanism that allows honest workers to check the correctness of task outputs, which is significantly better than all robust master-worker as well as peer-to-peer approaches known so far.

</details>


### [250] [Embedded Made Easy -- Rethinking Embedded + Cloud Software Development (WIP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14863)
*Anthony Arnold,Mark Marron*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对边缘与云端开发中因环境异构和跨平台调试复杂带来的痛点，本文提出了一种统一语言与运行时的解决方案，支持应用在全架构下的无缝编排及集成化分布式调试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前边缘/嵌入式应用开发面临巨大的工程挑战，包括软件栈非同质化、跨语言/运行时架构带来的决策束缚，以及分布式环境下故障诊断与调试极其困难。

Method: 提出了一种统一的语言和运行时系统架构，支持代码在资源受限的边缘设备与云服务器之间无缝迁移（retargetable stack），并设计了集成的分布式事件记录与回放工具以辅助诊断。

Result: 提出了一项处于进展中的构想（work-in-progress vision），论证了通过统一栈来实现应用跨边缘至云端无缝伸缩的可行性，并描绘了集成化诊断工具的蓝图。

Conclusion: 该设想旨在通过单一语言和运行时环境解决边缘与云端架构中的异构性、开发复杂性以及分布式系统调试困难的问题，为边缘计算提供统一的开发与调试范式。

Abstract: The process of engineering and deploying applications in the edge/embedded space is massively complicated by the non-homogeneous nature of the software stack and the complexity of diagnostics & debugging. Often different languages and runtimes are used for different components of the system forcing designers to, irrevocably, make decisions about what components run on the periphery and what components run in the cloud. Further complications arise when handling and diagnosing failures in the system. Multiple stacks and, often, limited support for debugging complicate the already difficult task of analyzing distributed applications.
  This paper presents a work-in-progress vision for a unified language and runtime system that allows applications to scale seamlessly across the edge and cloud. Using a single language and runtime, applications can be developed and tested in a single environment, and then deployed to any component of the system -- from resource limited controllers to large cloud servers. Further, we outline how this retargetable stack can provide integrated diagnostics and debugging tools that allow developers to record and replay distributed events locally for analysis and debugging.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [251] [AIS: Adaptive Importance Sampling for Quantized RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13907)
*Jiajun Zhou,Wei Shao,Lingchao Zheng,Yuwei Fan,Ngai Wong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 为了解决LLM强化学习中低精度（FP8）采样导致训练不稳定的问题，本文提出了自适应重要性采样（AIS），通过实时动态调节梯度校正策略，在实现大幅度加速的同时保证了训练的稳定性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对LLM强化学习中为了提高吞吐量而采用低精度（如FP8）采样所带来的“采样-训练不匹配”问题。这种不匹配会导致策略梯度偏差，进而引发模型训练崩溃，尤其是在推理任务中。

Method: 提出自适应重要性采样（Adaptive Importance Sampling, AIS）框架。该方法通过三个实时诊断指标（权重可靠性、散度严重性、方差放大率）计算混合系数，动态调整未校正梯度与重要性加权梯度之间的权重。

Result: AIS在LLaDA-8B、Qwen3-8B及Qwen3.5-9B模型上进行了验证。结果表明，该方法既能利用低精度采样带来的加速优势，又能通过抑制偏差保持训练稳定性，在数学推理和规划基准测试中表现出色。

Conclusion: AIS成功解决了低精度（FP8）采样带来的训练不稳定性问题，在保持FP8带来1.5-2.76倍生成加速的同时，性能与BF16基准相当。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models (LLMs) is dominated by the cost of rollout generation, which has motivated the use of low-precision rollouts (e.g., FP8) paired with a BF16 trainer to improve throughput and reduce memory pressure. This introduces a rollout-training mismatch that biases the policy gradient and can cause training to collapse outright on reasoning benchmarks. We show that the mismatch is non-stationary and acts as a double-edged sword: early in training it provides a stochastic exploration bonus, exposing the gradient to trajectories the trainer would otherwise under-sample, but the same perturbation transitions into a destabilizing source of bias as the policy concentrates.
  To solve this, we propose Adaptive Importance Sampling (AIS), a correction framework that adjusts the strength of its intervention on a per-batch basis. AIS combines three real-time diagnostics, namely weight reliability, divergence severity, and variance amplification, into a single mixing coefficient that interpolates between the uncorrected and fully importance-weighted gradients, suppressing the destabilizing component of the mismatch while preserving its exploratory benefit. We integrate AIS into GRPO and evaluate it on the diffusion-based LLaDA-8B-Instruct and the autoregressive Qwen3-8B and Qwen3.5-9B across mathematical reasoning and planning benchmarks. AIS matches the BF16 baseline on most tasks while retaining the 1.5 to 2.76x rollout speedup of FP8.

</details>


### [252] [Covariance-aware sampling for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13910)
*Andrea Schioppa,Tim Salimans*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 一项针对扩散模型少步采样优化的研究，通过显式建模逆过程并结合协方差感知技术，在极低计算开销下显著提升了图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在少步采样（few-step regime）下，现有扩散模型采样器仅依赖预测均值，忽略了逆过程的协方差，导致生成质量下降。

Method: 基于DDIM扩展，利用Tweedie公式估计逆过程协方差，并采用高效的傅里叶空间结构化分解来处理协方差矩阵，仅需每步额外执行一次雅可比向量积（JVP）。

Result: 实验证明在像素空间扩散模型中，该采样器在少步数下表现优于当前最先进的二阶采样器。

Conclusion: 通过引入协方差建模，该方法能在相同的采样步数（NFE）下显著提升像素空间扩散模型的生成质量，优于现有的二阶采样器（如Heun、DPM-Solver++）和aDDIM。

Abstract: We present a covariance-aware sampler that improves the quality of pixel-space Diffusion Model (DM) sampling in the few-step regime. We hypothesize that in the few-step regime samplers fail because they rely solely on the predicted mean of the reverse distribution, while our solution explicitly models the reverse-process covariance. Our method combines Tweedie's formula to estimate the covariance with an efficient, structured Fourier-space decomposition of the covariance matrix. Implemented as an extension of DDIM, our method requires only a minimal overhead: one extra Jacobian-Vector Product (JVP) per step. We demonstrate that for pixel-based DMs, our method consistently produces superior samples compared to state-of-the-art second order samplers (Heun, DPM-Solver++) and the recent aDDIM sampler, at an identical number of function evaluations (NFE).

</details>


### [253] [Multi-Scale Dequant: Eliminating Dequantization Bottleneck via Activation Decomposition for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13915)
*Lingchao Zheng,Yuwei Fan,Jun Li,Chengqiu Hu,Qichen Liao,Junyi Fan,Rui Shi,Fangzheng Miao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 推理中反量化步骤造成的性能瓶颈，MSD 通过将高精度激活值分解并进行多尺度矩阵乘法，消除了 GEMM 关键路径上的反量化需求，在维持高精度的同时提升了推理效率与存储利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代 AI 加速器（如昇腾 NPU）上，LLM 推理中的反量化（将低位权重转换为高精度进行矩阵乘法）已成为计算瓶颈，导致算力单元利用率低下。

Method: 提出多尺度反量化（MSD）框架：将高精度激活值（BF16）分解为多个低精度组件，直接与量化权重进行原生 GEMM 计算，从而规避了从低位权重到高精度权重的反量化过程。该方法涵盖了 INT8 和 MXFP4 两种量化格式，并提供了相应的误差理论边界。

Result: 1. 在 INT8 格式下实现了近乎 16 位有效精度；2. 在 MXFP4 格式下实现了近乎 6.6 位有效精度，优于单次 MXFP8，且计算时间相同；3. 将 KV Cache 的 HBM 访存带宽需求降低了近 2.5 倍，有效消除了计算管线停滞。

Conclusion: MSD 通过移除非必要的反量化计算路径，有效缓解了算力瓶颈。实验证明，该方法在不牺牲准确率的情况下，显著降低了计算延时及 KV Cache 的 HBM 访存压力，甚至在某些场景下获得更好的数值精度。

Abstract: Quantization is essential for efficient large language model (LLM) inference, yet the dequantization step-converting low-bit weights back to high-precision for matrix multiplication has become a critical bottleneck on modern AI accelerators. On architectures with decoupled compute units (e.g., Ascend NPUs), dequantization operations can consume more cycles than the matrix multiplication itself, leaving the high-throughput tensor cores underutilized. This paper presents Multi-Scale Dequant (MSD), a quantization framework that removes weight/KV dequantization from the GEMM critical path. Instead of lifting low-bit weights to BF16 precision, MSD decomposes high-precision BF16 activations into multiple low-precision components, each of which can be multiplied directly with quantized weights via native hardware-accelerated GEMM.
