This approach shifts the computational paradigm from precision conversion to multi-scale approximation, avoiding INT8-to-BF16 weight conversion before GEMM. We instantiate MSD for two weight formats and derive tight error bounds for each. For INT8 weights (W4A16), two-pass INT8 decomposition achieves near 16 effective bits. For MXFP4 weights (W4A16), two-pass MXFP4 decomposition yields near 6.6 effective bits with error bound 1/64 per block surpassing single-pass MXFP8(5.24 bits) while maintaining the same effective GEMM compute time. We further derive closed-form latency and HBM traffic models showing that MSD avoids the Vector-Cube pipeline stall caused by dequantization and reduces KV cache HBM traffic by up to 2.5 times in attention. Numerical simulations on matrix multiplication and Flash Attention kernels confirm that MSD does not degrade accuracy compared to dequantization baselines, and in many settings achieves lower L2 error.

</details>


### [254] [A Regret Perspective on Online Multiple Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13916)
*Qingyang Hao,Kongchang Zhou,Fang Kong,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了“加权遗憾”评估指标，并开发了 Decoupled-OMT (DOMT) 算法。通过引入解耦的随机扰动缓解阈值耗尽，DOMT 能够在非平稳在线测试环境中，以最小的额外假阴性代价，显著降低系统的整体加权遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线多重假设检验（OMT）在评估 FDR 和统计功效时是割裂的，忽视了现代自动化流水线中假阳性和假阴性的非对称成本，且确定性算法在冷启动阶段面临严重的阈值耗尽问题。

Method: 引入“加权遗憾”（Weighted Regret）指标来统一评估 FDR 和统计功效；设计了 Decoupled-OMT (DOMT) 元包装器，通过引入与历史解耦的非负随机扰动，防止确定性基准算法在信号稀疏阶段的阈值耗尽。

Result: 证明了确定性程序存在 Ω(T) 的线性遗憾下界；证明了 DOMT 可在保持严格 FDR 控制的同时，在突发环境中实现 Ω(√T) 的遗憾降低，并推导出了刻画算法优越性相变的“冷启动税”（Cold-Start Tax）。

Conclusion: DOMT 成功解决了在线多重假设检验中 FDR 控制与统计功效之间的权衡问题，特别是在处理冷启动导致的阈值耗尽现象上，通过引入解耦的随机扰动，实现了次线性遗憾（sublinear regret），验证了其在非平稳环境下的有效性。

Abstract: Online Multiple Testing (OMT), a fundamental pillar of sequential statistical inference, traditionally evaluates the False Discovery Rate (FDR) and statistical power in isolation, obscuring the highly asymmetric costs of false positives and false negatives in modern automated pipelines. To unify this evaluation, we introduce $\textit{Weighted Regret}$. Under this metric, we prove the $\textit{Duality of Regret Conservation}$: purely deterministic procedures ensuring strict FDR control inevitably incur an $Ω(T)$ linear regret penalty, as threshold depletion during signal-sparse cold starts forces massive false negatives. Tailored for exogenous testing streams, we propose Decoupled-OMT (DOMT) as a baseline-agnostic meta-wrapper. By incorporating a history-decoupled, strictly non-negative random perturbation, DOMT rescues purely deterministic baselines from severe threshold depletion. Crucially, it preserves exact asymptotic safety in stationary environments and rigorously bounds finite-sample error inflation during cold-starts. Guaranteeing zero additional false negatives, it yields an order-optimal $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ regret reduction in bursty environments, with a derived ``Cold-Start Tax'' characterizing the exact phase transition of algorithmic superiority. Experiments validate that DOMT consistently curtails empirical weighted regret, achieving an order-optimal sublinear mitigation of threshold depletion to navigate the non-stationary Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [255] [To discretize continually: Mean shift interacting particle systems for Bayesian inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14142)
*Ayoub Belhadji,Daniel Sharp,Youssef M. Marzouk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过最小化最大均值差异（MMD）来生成加权样本点的交互式粒子系统方法，能够高效、准确地对复杂的非归一化概率分布进行积分近似，解决了高维和多模态分布采样中的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在贝叶斯推理中，针对仅给定非归一化密度的概率分布计算期望是一个核心难题。传统的采样方法在处理高维、多模态或各向异性分布时往往效率较低，因此需要开发更高效的求积规则（quadrature rule）。

Method: 提出了一种通过最小化目标分布与样本分布之间最大均值差异（MMD）的交互式粒子系统（Interacting Particle System）。该方法扩展了平均位移（mean shift）算法，使其适用于连续分布，并支持无梯度和有梯度的实现方式，且对未知的归一化常数具有不变性。

Result: 该方法在收敛速度、捕捉各向异性和多模态分布、避免模式崩塌以及高维扩展性方面表现出色。通过多模态混合模型、贝叶斯分层模型和PDE约束逆问题等基准测试，验证了该算法的优越性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于交互式粒子系统的MMD最小化方法，能够不依赖归一化常数，并在保持多模态分布特征的同时进行高效的积分近似，在大规模维度和复杂分布下展现了优越的性能，是贝叶斯推理中一种稳健的求积规则方案。

Abstract: Integration against a probability distribution given its unnormalized density is a central task in Bayesian inference and other fields. We introduce new methods for approximating such expectations with a small set of weighted samples -- i.e., a quadrature rule -- constructed via an interacting particle system that minimizes maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) to the target distribution. These methods extend the classical mean shift algorithm, as well as recent algorithms for optimal quantization of empirical distributions, to the case of continuous distributions. Crucially, our approach creates dynamics for MMD minimization that are invariant to the unknown normalizing constant; they also admit both gradient-free and gradient-informed implementations. The resulting mean shift interacting particle systems converge quickly, capture anisotropy and multi-modality, avoid mode collapse, and scale to high dimensions. We demonstrate their performance on a wide range of benchmark sampling problems, including multi-modal mixtures, Bayesian hierarchical models, PDE-constrained inverse problems, and beyond.

</details>


### [256] [On the Burden of Achieving Fairness in Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14260)
*Ziang Gao,Pengqi Liu,Archer Yi Yang,Mouloud Belbahri,Jesse C. Cresswell,Masoud Asgharian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了符合预测中的公平性问题，证明了通过单一阈值校准无法完全消除群体间的差异，且存在“等量覆盖”与“等量集大小”的公平性矛盾，为实践中的模型校准提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的池化校准（Pooled Calibration）会掩盖不同群体之间的分布差异，导致群体间覆盖率不均衡，缺乏对公平性与校准策略之间权衡的深入理解。

Method: 1. 基于种群得分分布理论，推导守恒定律与下界，量化分组校准中的覆盖率失真；2. 定义并证明“等量覆盖”与“等量集大小”两种公平性定义之间的内在冲突；3. 结合合成数据与真实数据实验，验证有限样本下的双向权衡。

Result: 研究证明了池化校准导致的群体覆盖率失真是不可避免的；揭示了在保持群体公平性的不同策略（单独校准与池化校准）之间存在明显的性能折衷。

Conclusion: 在符合预测中，不存在“无损”的选择；校准策略实质上是在“覆盖率偏差”与“预测集大小差异”之间进行权衡，不存在兼顾公平性的单一最优解。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is often calibrated with a single pooled threshold, but this can hide cross-group heterogeneity in score distributions and distort group-wise coverage. We study this phenomenon through the population score distributions underlying split conformal calibration. First, we derive a conservation law and lower bound showing that pooled calibration incurs irreducible group-wise coverage distortion at a scale set by cross-group quantile heterogeneity. Second, we demonstrate that the two leading fairness definitions for conformal prediction, Equalized Coverage and Equalized Set Size, are fundamentally in tension. Third, we quantify the cost of moving between policies which treat groups separately or pool them. Experiments on synthetic and real data confirm the same bidirectional trade-off after finite-sample calibration. Our results show that, for the policy families studied here, calibration choice does not remove cross-group heterogeneity; it determines whether the resulting distortion appears in the coverage or size dimension, providing a principled lens for analyzing fairness-oriented calibration choices in practice.

</details>


### [257] [Logging Policy Design for Off-Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15108)
*Connor Douglas,Joel Persson,Foster Provost*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何设计日志策略（logging policy）以最小化离线策略评估（OPE）的误差，通过分析奖励信号与探索范围之间的权衡，为数据驱动的决策提供了一套在不同信息条件下优化评估精度的理论框架与实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略评估（OPE）受到日志策略的影响巨大，当前缺乏针对降低OPE误差而进行日志策略设计的量化方法，现有研究主要关注如何从固定数据进行估算，而非如何主动采集数据以优化估算精度。

Method: 构建了一个统一的日志策略设计框架，通过数学建模分析了奖励与覆盖范围之间的权衡，并分别推导了在已知、未知及部分已知（通过先验或噪声估计）三种信息假设下的最优日志策略。

Result: 揭示了奖励-覆盖平衡（Reward-Coverage tradeoff）机制；在多种典型信息场景下导出了最优策略公式；提出了一套可供企业选择推荐系统时使用的实践准则，证明了主动优化策略可以显著提高评估的准确性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一套针对OPE任务优化日志策略的理论框架，证明了数据收集策略对评估精度至关重要，并给出了在不同信息假设下的最优策略及其实践指导原则。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) estimates the value of a target treatment policy (e.g., a recommender system) using data collected by a different logging policy. It enables high-stakes experimentation without live deployment, yet in practice accuracy depends heavily on the logging policy used to collect data for computing the estimate. We study how to design logging policies that minimize OPE error for given target policies. We characterize a fundamental reward-coverage tradeoff: concentrating probability mass on high-reward actions reduces variance but risks missing signal on actions the target policy may take. We propose a unifying framework for logging policy design and derive optimal policies in canonical informational regimes where the target policy and reward distribution are (i) known, (ii) unknown, and (iii) partially known through priors or noisy estimates at logging time. Our results provide actionable guidance for firms choosing among multiple candidate recommendation systems. We demonstrate the importance of treatment selection when gathering data for OPE, and describe theoretically optimal approaches when this is a firm's primary objective. We also distill practical design principles for selecting logging policies when operational constraints prevent implementing the theoretical optimum.

</details>


### [258] [Large Dimensional Kernel Ridge Regression: Extending to Product Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14524)
*Yang Zhou,Yicheng Li,Yuqian Cheng,Qian Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文定义了一类新的核函数，揭示了核岭回归中饱和效应和多重下降行为的普适性，不再局限于以往的特殊假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于KRR饱和效应和多重下降行为的研究多局限于特定设置（如球上的内积核或超压缩性假设），作者旨在探究这些现象是否在更广泛的核函数类别中具有普适性。

Method: 提出并构建了一个新的广义核函数族（large dimensional kernels），通过数学推导推导了该核族下核岭回归的泛化误差收敛速率，并分析了其在源条件（source condition）下的性质。

Result: 在新的核函数族中成功恢复了以下核心现象：1. 当源条件 $s \le 1$ 时的极大极小最优性；2. 当 $s > 1$ 时的饱和效应；3. 与样本大小 $n$ 相关联的周期性平台现象（periodic plateau）及多重下降行为。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了饱和效应、极大极小最优性以及多重下降行为（multiple descent）在更广泛的核函数族中是普遍存在的，从而扩展了对核岭回归（KRR）在高维设置下的理论理解。

Abstract: Recent studies have reported $\textit{saturation effects}$ and $\textit{multiple descent behavior}$ in large dimensional kernel ridge regression (KRR). However, these findings are predominantly derived under restrictive settings, such as inner product kernels on sphere or strong eigenfunction assumptions like hypercontractivity. Whether such behaviors hold for other kernels remains an open question. In this paper, we establish a broad, new family of large dimensional kernels and derive the corresponding convergence rates of the generalization error. As a result, we recover key phenomena previously associated with inner product kernels on sphere, including: $i)$ the $\textit{minimax optimality}$ when the source condition $s\le 1$; $ii)$ the $\textit{saturation effect}$ when $s>1$; $iii)$ a $\textit{periodic plateau phenomenon}$ in the convergence rate and a $\textit {multiple-descent behavior}$ with respect to the sample size $n$.

</details>


### [259] [K-Models: a Flexible and Interpretable Method for Ordinal Clustering with Application to Antigen-Antibody Interaction Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14828)
*Giulia Patanè,Alessandra Menafoglio,Alexander Krauth,Peter Fechner,Luca Dede',Bianca Maria Colosimo,Federica Nicolussi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: K-Models 是一种引入序数约束的函数型数据聚类框架，旨在平衡聚类准确性与结果的可解释性，特别适用于具有潜在序数结构的函数型数据分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的函数型数据聚类方法往往片面追求准确性，而忽视了可解释性，难以处理那些具有潜在生成结构或序数关系的函数型数据。

Method: 提出了一种名为“K-Models”的函数型数据聚类框架，该框架将序数约束（ordinal constraints）整合到聚类过程中，并估计了生成观测函数型剖面的随机过程的关键潜在要素。

Result: 通过仿真实验和生物分子相互作用监测（ROI曲线）的实证研究表明，K-Models 能够在保证聚类性能的前提下，有效提取更有意义的底层信号模式，提高了结果的可解释性。

Conclusion: K-Models 框架通过引入序数约束，在保持与现有聚类方法相当性能的同时，显着提升了函数型数据聚类的可解释性和结构识别能力，证明了其在具有潜在序数特征的数据分析中的价值。

Abstract: Existing clustering methods for functional data often prioritize partitioning accuracy over interpretability, making it challenging to extract meaningful insights when the data-generating process follows a specific underlying structure and an ordinal relationship among clusters is suspected. This work introduces K-Models, a novel framework that integrates ordinal constraints and estimates key underlying elements of the random process generating the observed functional profiles, improving both interpretability and structure identification. The proposed method is evaluated through simulations and real-world applications. In particular, it is tested on Region of Interest (ROI) curves, which represent reaction profiles from a reflectometric sensor monitoring biomolecular interactions, such as antigen-antibody binding. These curves represent changes in reflected light intensity over time at multiple measurement spots with immobilized antigens during analyte exposure, capturing the binding dynamics of the system. The goal is to identify intrinsic signal patterns solely from the observed dynamics, making this dataset an ideal benchmark for assessing the added interpretability of the proposed approach. By incorporating structural assumptions into the clustering process, K-Models enhances interpretability while maintaining performance comparable to state-of-the-art techniques, providing a valuable tool for analyzing functional data with an underlying ordinal structure.

</details>


### [260] [Average Gradient Outer Product in kernel regression provably recovers the central subspace for multi-index models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15082)
*Libin Zhu,Damek Davis,Dmitriy Drusvyatskiy,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上证明了通过核岭回归计算梯度外积（AGOP），可以在远低于预测所需样本量的条件下成功恢复数据的低维子空间，揭示了表示学习在样本复杂度上优于预测任务的内在机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 试图解释为何在某些情况下，机器学习模型即便在样本量不足以进行准确预测时，也能发现数据中潜在的有用低维结构，并旨在揭示预测任务与表示学习任务在样本复杂度上的差异。

Method: 结合核岭回归（KRR）与平均梯度外积（AGOP）算法。通过计算拟合预测器的AGOP，利用其前r维特征空间来恢复目标函数依赖的未知r维中央子空间。

Result: 在目标函数具有度数为p的低度分量时，子空间恢复仅需n≈d^{p+δ}个样本，远低于达到准确预测所需的n≈d^{p^*}个样本。这从理论上证明了在较少样本条件下实现表示学习的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究证明了“预测”与“表示学习”之间存在分离：即使在预测误差仍然较大的情况下，通过AGOP算法足以高效率地恢复底层低维结构（中央子空间）。该结论为递归特征机（RFM）等迭代核方法的样本高效性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We study a prototypical situation when a learned predictor can discover useful low-dimensional structure in data, while using fewer samples than are needed for accurate prediction. Specifically, we consider the problem of recovering a multi-index polynomial $f^*(x)=h(Ux)$, with $U\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times d}$ and $r\ll d$, from finitely many data/label pairs. Importantly, the target function depends on input $x$ only through the projection onto an unknown $r$-dimensional central subspace. The algorithm we analyze is appealingly simple: fit kernel ridge regression (KRR) to the data and compute the Average Gradient Outer Product (AGOP) from the fitted predictor. Our main results show that under reasonable assumptions the top $r$-dimensional eigenspace of AGOP provably recovers the central subspace, even in regimes when the prediction error remains large. Specifically, if the target function $f^*$ has degree $p^*$, it is known that $n\asymp d^{p^*}$ samples are necessary for KRR to achieve accurate prediction. In contrast, we show that if a low degree $p$ component of $f^*$ already carries all relevant directions for prediction, subspace recovery occurs in the much lower sample regime $n\asymp d^{p+δ}$ for any $δ\in(0,1)$. Our results thus demonstrate a separation between prediction and representation, and provide an explanation for why iterative kernel methods such as Recursive Feature Machines (RFM) can be sample-efficient in practice.

</details>


### [261] [From Data to Action: Accelerating Refinery Optimization with AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15085)
*Dániel Pfeifer,Ábrahám Papp,Tibor Bernáth,Tamás Zoltán Varga,Márk Czifra,Botond Szilágyi,Edith Alice Kovács*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对炼油行业大型LP优化模型结果可信度不足的问题，本研究通过引入改进的ECOD异常检测算法，结合历史数据与优化结果，有效识别了模型中的数据错误及潜在商业机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商用LP优化软件在处理海量炼油模型时，虽能保证数学求解正确，但因模型简化与原始数据输入错误，导致结果的实际可信度存疑，缺乏对历史数据的深度利用，急需机器学习方法辅助决策。

Method: 提出了一种基于改进型ECOD（无监督离群点检测）的异常检测框架，通过筛选信息量最大的特征对，并结合两种二维异常检测算法，处理炼油厂优化中的高维数据。

Result: 研究成功地在MOL炼油厂的调度与规划架构中识别出了多项业务优化机会及关键数据输入错误，验证了该辅助分析体系的有效性。

Conclusion: 将异常检测工具引入炼油厂调度与线性规划(LP)优化流程中，不仅能验证LP方案的可靠性，还能有效识别数据输入错误和潜在的商业机会，从而增强决策者对优化结果的信任。

Abstract: Nowadays refinery optimization utilizes sheer amounts of data, which can be handled with modern Linear Programming (LP) software, but the interpreting and applying the results remains challenging. Large petrochemical companies use massive models, with hundreds of thousands of input matrix elements. The LP solution is mathematically correct, but simplifications are made in the model, and data supply errors may occur. Therefore, further insight is needed to trust the results. The LP solver does not have a memory, so additional understanding could be gained by analyzing historical data and comparing it to the current plan. As such, machine learning approaches were suggested to support decision making based on the LP solution. Among these, Anomaly Detection tools are proposed to be used in tandem with the LP output. A transformed version of the popular ECOD methodology is applied. New methods are proposed to handle high-dimensional data: choosing the most informative pairs. Then, this is used alongside two 2D Anomaly Detection algorithms, revealing several business opportunities and data supply errors in the MOL refinery scheduling and planning architecture.

</details>


### [262] [RoSHAP: A Distributional Framework and Robust Metric for Stable Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15154)
*Lanxin Xiang,Liang Shi,Youhui Ye,Boyu Jiang,Dawei Zhou,Feng Guo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对机器学习特征归因中存在的随机不稳定性问题，提出了RoSHAP框架，通过统计分布建模实现特征的稳定排序，并在保证预测性能的同时显著精简了模型所需的特征数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习特征归因方法（如SHAP）存在严重的随机波动问题（如受训练数据划分、随机种子和模型拟合过程影响），导致特征排序不稳定，影响决策的可靠性。

Method: 构建了一个包含自助法（bootstrap）重采样和核密度估计的统计框架来建模归因分数的分布，并利用其分布特性推导提出了RoSHAP指标。理论上证明了聚合的归因分数渐进服从高斯分布，从而降低了计算成本。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据实验中，RoSHAP在识别有效特征方面优于传统的单次运行归因指标；利用RoSHAP筛选的特征子集在保持预测性能的同时，显著降低了所需特征的数量。

Conclusion: RoSHAP是一种稳定且可靠的特征重要性排序指标，能够有效应对特征归因中的随机波动问题，提升机器学习模型的可解释性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Feature attribution analysis is critical for interpreting machine learning models and supporting reliable data-driven decisions. However, feature attribution measures often exhibit stochastic variation: different train--test splits, random seeds, or model-fitting procedures can produce substantially different attribution values and feature rankings. This paper proposes a framework for incorporating stochastic nature of feature attribution and a robust attribution metric, RoSHAP, for stable feature ranking based on the SHAP metric. The proposed framework models the distribution of feature attribution scores and estimates it through bootstrap resampling and kernel density estimation. We show that, under mild regularity conditions, the aggregated feature attribution score is asymptotically Gaussian, which greatly reduces the computational cost of distribution estimation. The RoSHAP summarizes the distribution of SHAP into a robust feature-ranking criterion that simultaneously rewards features that are active, strong, and stable. Through simulations and real-data experiments, the proposed framework and RoSHAP outperform standard single-run attribution measures in identifying signal features. In addition, models built using RoSHAP-selected features achieve predictive performance comparable to full-feature models while using substantially fewer predictors. The proposed RoSHAP approach improves the stability and interpretability of machine learning models, enabling reliable and consistent insights for analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Towards Robotic Dexterous Hand Intelligence: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13925)
*Weiguang Zhao,Xihao Guo,Tian Liang,Rui Zhang,Irwin King,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本综述系统性地整合了机器人灵巧手在硬件设计、控制学习、数据资源及评估体系方面的研究进展，旨在消除行业壁垒，为未来灵巧手 manipulation 研究提供统一的视角和方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手研究在硬件、任务设定和评估标准上缺乏统一，导致研究碎片化，难以进行系统性对比及明确技术发展路径。

Method: 采用综述研究方法，从四个维度对灵巧手研究进行系统性梳理：1. 硬件设计（驱动、感知、集成度）；2. 控制方法（归纳演变范式）；3. 数据与评测（整合数据集与基准）；4. 局限性与未来展望探讨。

Result: 构建了一个全面的综述体系，明确了硬件性能与复杂性的权衡关系，梳理了控制算法的演进逻辑，整理了数据资源现状，并总结了当前研究的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 本文为灵巧手研究提供了结构化的框架，呼吁领域内应更关注硬件与算法的协同设计、标准化评估体系的建立，并指明了未来面临的核心挑战。

Abstract: Robotic dexterous hands are central to contact-rich manipulation, with rapid progress driven by advances in hardware, sensing, control, simulation, and data generation. However, existing studies are often developed under different assumptions regarding hand embodiments, sensory configurations, task settings, training data, and evaluation protocols, making systematic comparison difficult and obscuring the developmental trajectory of the field. This survey provides a holistic review of dexterous hand research from four complementary aspects. First, we present a hardware-level analysis covering actuation, transmission, perception, and representative hand designs, highlighting the key trade-offs in force capability, compliance, bandwidth, integration, and system complexity. Furthermore, we review control and learning methods for dexterous manipulation from a methodological perspective, grouping representative works by major paradigms and tracing their evolution in chronological order. In addition, we consolidate datasets, modality design, and evaluation practices, which enables methodological progress to be interpreted together with the ways in which it is trained, benchmarked, and assessed. Finally, we discuss the major limitations of current dexterous hand research and summarize the corresponding future directions. By connecting hardware analysis, methodological development, data resources, and evaluation, this survey aims to provide a structured understanding of dexterous hand research and to clarify the most important open challenges for future study.

</details>


### [264] [Ergodic Imitation for Adaptive Exploration around Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13996)
*Ziyi Xu,Cem Bilaloglu,Yiming Li,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对模仿学习在环境失配下的失败问题，本文提出了一种“自适应遍历模仿”方法，通过将演示轨迹转化为目标分布进行遍历控制，实现了跟踪演示与在线探索的平衡，提升了任务完成的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习中存在训练与部署环境失配的问题，导致机器人容易陷入局部最优或无法完成任务，需要一种既能保持对演示忠实度又能具备在线自适应探索能力的策略。

Method: 提出了一种基于检索的滚动时域框架。该方法首先通过演示数据的几何特征构建目标分布，随后利用遍历控制（Ergodic Control）思想生成轨迹，在模仿演示（跟踪）与环境探索之间实现动态自适应插值。

Result: 通过将演示数据融入遍历控制，成功实现了在不确定环境下的在线策略调整，提升了机器人在任务执行中的鲁棒性与成功率。

Conclusion: 提出了一种自适应遍历模仿（Adaptive Ergodic Imitation）框架，通过结合演示信息与遍历控制，有效解决了模仿学习在环境失配下的泛化与任务执行能力。

Abstract: In robotics, a common challenge in imitation learning is the mismatch between training and deployment conditions, caused, for example, by environmental changes or imperfect observation and control. When a robot follows a nominal trajectory under such mismatch, it may become stuck and fail to complete the task. This calls for adaptive online exploration strategies that remain grounded in demonstrations. To this end, we propose an adaptive ergodic imitation approach that constructs a target distribution from the geometry of the retrieved demonstrations and uses it to generate trajectories that adaptively interpolate between tracking and exploration. Our method extends ergodic control beyond its traditional role in area-coverage and search by incorporating demonstrations into a retrieval-based receding-horizon framework for adaptive imitation.

</details>


### [265] [Behavior Cloning for Active Perception with Low-Resolution Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14106)
*Anthony Bilic,Chen Chen,Ladislau Bölöni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究表明，行为克隆能够使机器人通过简单的视觉输入实现主动感知，通过闭环动作定位并抓取目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究行为克隆是否足以在结构化任务中产生主动感知（即通过动作改善后续观测以完成任务的能力）。

Method: 使用低成本机械臂，配备腕部安装的自中心RGB摄像头；采用行为克隆（Behavior Cloning）方法，通过闭环控制，根据低分辨率RGB图像直接预测关节指令（增量控制）。

Result: 低分辨率的自中心视觉足以实现可靠的任务完成；预测相对关节增量在性能上显著优于绝对关节位置预测。

Conclusion: 行为克隆足以在结构化对象寻找任务中实现主动感知，且预测相对关节增量（relative joint deltas）比绝对关节位置预测更有效。

Abstract: We investigate whether behavior cloning is sufficient to produce active perception in a structured object-finding task. A low-cost robot arm equipped with a wrist-mounted egocentric RGB camera must reposition to center a partially visible plant before triggering a grasp signal, requiring actions that improve future observations. The model predicts joint commands directly from low-resolution RGB images under closed-loop control. We show that low-resolution egocentric vision is sufficient for reliable task completion and that predicting relative joint deltas substantially outperforms absolute joint position prediction in our setting. These results demonstrate that visually grounded active perception can emerge from behavior cloning in a reproducible setting.

</details>


### [266] [Motion Planning for Autonomous Vehicles using Optimization over Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14199)
*Matheus Wagner,Antônio Augusto Fröhlich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于凸集图（GCS）的自动驾驶运动规划方法，通过将非凸任务转化为凸优化问题，在大规模场景下实现了比传统非线性最优控制更高效、鲁棒的轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性最优控制计算代价高且对初始化敏感，而几何规划方法通常将路径选择与轨迹优化解耦，导致次优解。本研究旨在探究GCS能否在保证实时性的前提下，准确逼近非线性运动规划问题的解。

Method: 利用凸集图（GCS）框架，将非凸的自由空间分解为一组凸区域的有向图，结合贝塞尔曲线参数化路径和多项式时间缩放。在小滑移及线性轮胎假设下，利用简化的自行车模型将动态可行性转化为凸约束。

Result: 在CommonRoad模拟场景下的测试表明，GCS方法生成的轨迹在避障和换道任务中与非线性规划结果高度一致，且计算效率更高，对初始化的依赖性显著降低。

Conclusion: 凸集图（GCS）提供了一种高效的运动规划方法，能够作为非线性控制问题的有效凸近似，在处理复杂环境时既保证了计算效率，又具备良好的动态一致性。

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles requires generating collision-free and dynamically feasible trajectories in complex environments under real-time constraints. While nonlinear optimal control formulations provide high-fidelity solutions, they are computationally demanding and sensitive to initialization, whereas geometric planning methods scale well but often decouple path selection from trajectory optimization. This paper studies the extent to which optimization over Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS) can approximate solutions of nonlinear optimal control problems in the context of autonomous driving. The free space is represented as a finite union of convex regions organized as a directed graph, allowing nonconvex geometry to be handled through discrete connectivity decisions while maintaining convex trajectory constraints within each region. Vehicle motion is parameterized using Bezier curves for the spatial path and a polynomial time-scaling function for temporal evolution. Under small-slip and linear tire assumptions, a simplified dynamic bicycle model enables approximate enforcement of dynamic feasibility through convex constraints on trajectory derivatives. The approach is evaluated in CommonRoad scenarios involving static obstacle avoidance and lane-changing maneuvers, and is compared against a nonlinear discrete-time optimal control formulation. The results indicate that the GCS-based method generates collision-free and dynamically consistent trajectories that closely match those obtained from the nonlinear program, while exhibiting improved computational efficiency and reduced sensitivity to initialization. These findings suggest that GCS provides a structured approximation of nonlinear motion planning problems, capturing dominant geometric and dynamic effects while preserving convexity in the continuous relaxation.

</details>


### [267] [MAPLE: Latent Multi-Agent Play for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14201)
*Rajeev Yasarla,Deepti Hegde,Hsin-Pai Cheng,Shizhong Han,Yunxiao Shi,Meysam Sadeghigooghari,Hanno Ackermann,Litian Liu,Pranav Desai,Fatih Porikli,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Hong Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAPLE 提出了一种在 VLA 模型潜空间内进行反应式多智能体演练的闭环训练框架，无需外部模拟器即可通过强化学习优化自动驾驶策略，在实现驾驶性能提升的同时增强了系统的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型采用传统的模仿学习，使其在闭环评估中表现脆弱；而现有的闭环监督方法不仅缺乏可扩展性，且难以有效模拟动态环境下的智能体反应。

Method: 提出 MAPLE 框架，核心在于潜空间内的反应式多智能体演练。分为两个阶段：一是基于真实轨迹的潜空间演练进行监督微调；二是采用包含安全、进度、交互真实性及行为多样性的多目标强化学习。该方法无需昂贵的外部模拟器，实现了可扩展的闭环训练。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 基准测试中实现了最优（SOTA）的驾驶性能，并验证了其在可扩展、闭环多智能体交互中的有效性，显著提升了端到端自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MAPLE 框架通过潜空间内的多智能体交互式演练，克服了现有 VLA 模型在自动驾驶闭环评估中的脆弱性，无需外部模拟器，实现了更具鲁棒性的端到端自动驾驶训练。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are effective as end-to-end motion planners, but can be brittle when evaluated in closed-loop settings due to being trained under traditional imitation learning framework. Existing closed-loop supervision approaches lack scalability and fail to completely model a reactive environment. We propose MAPLE, a novel framework for reactive, multi-agent rollout of a dynamic driving scenario in the latent space of the VLA model. The ego vehicle and nearby traffic agents are independently controlled over multi-step horizons, while being reactive to other agents in the scene, enabling closed-loop training. MAPLE consists of two training stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on the latent rollouts based on ground-truth trajectories, followed by (2) reinforcement learning with global and agent -specific rewards that encourage safety, progress, and interaction realism. We further propose diversity rewards that encourage the model to generate planning behaviors that may not be present in logged driving data. Notably, our closed-loop training framework is scalable and does not require external simulators, which can be computationally expensive to run and have limited visual fidelity to the real-world. MAPLE achieves state-of-the-art driving performance on Bench2Drive and demonstrates scalable, closed-loop multi-agent play for robust E2E autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [268] [Reactive Planning based Control for Mobile Robots in Obstacle-Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14232)
*Li Tan,Junlin Xiong,Yan Wang,Wei Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对移动机器人在已知部分环境信息情况下的无碰撞运动控制策略（RPCS），通过结合局部反应式轨迹规划与自适应跟踪控制，实现了复杂环境下的高效运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在复杂障碍环境且仅拥有部分环境信息的情况下，难以在实现避障的同时保持对目标点的高效运动控制。

Method: 提出了一种结合“反应式规划策略 (RPS)”与“自适应跟踪控制策略 (ATCS)”的混合控制架构。其中，RPS 利用局部环境信息对参考轨迹进行实时修正以规避障碍物，ATCS 则通过离散化技术实现对（修正后）参考轨迹的精确跟踪。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了 RPCS 在复杂环境中实现机器人无碰撞运动控制的有效性与优越性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为 RPCS 的反应式规划控制策略，能够有效解决移动机器人在部分环境信息条件下的避障与轨迹跟踪问题。

Abstract: This paper addresses the motion control problem for mobile robots in obstacle-cluttered environments. The mobile robot has partial environment information only, and aims to move from an initial position to a target position without collisions. For this purpose, a reactive planning based control strategy (RPCS) is proposed. First, the initial and target positions are connected as a reference trajectory. Then, a reactive planning strategy (RPS) is developed to ensure the collision avoidance by modifying the reference trajectory locally based on the partial environment information. Next, an adaptive tracking control strategy (ATCS) is proposed to track the reference trajectory with potentially local modifications via the discretization techniques. Finally, the RPS and ATCS are combined to establish the RPCS, whose efficacy and advantages are illustrated by numerical examples.

</details>


### [269] [Distill: Uncovering the True Intent behind Human-Robot Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14262)
*Ting Li,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人交互中自然语言歧义和代码编写过于死板的问题，本文提出 Distill 框架，通过语义泛化和约束优化来提升系统对用户意图的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自然语言（存在歧义）和终端用户编程（过度具体）方式难以准确捕捉用户意图，导致人机协作效率受限。

Method: 提出 Distill 框架，包含三个核心步骤：1. 移除无关步骤；2. 泛化单个步骤的语义；3. 放宽步骤间的顺序约束。并通过 Web 界面与众包实验进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，Distill 能够有效地从用户初始的任务指令中提取并精炼出真实意图。

Conclusion: Distill 方法通过对原始任务指令进行去冗、泛化和约束放松，能够有效提升人机交互中机器人对用户意图的理解准确性。

Abstract: As robots become increasingly integrated into everyday environments, intuitive communication paradigms such as natural language and end-user programming have become indispensable for specifying autonomous robot behavior. However, these mechanisms are ineffective at fully capturing user intent: natural language is imprecise and ambiguous, whereas end-user programming can be overly specific. As a result, understanding what users truly mean when they interact with robots remains a central challenge for human-AI communication systems. To address this issue, we propose the Distill approach for human-robot communication interfaces. Given a task specification provided by the user, Distill (1) removes unnecessary steps; (2) generalizes the meaning behind individual steps; and (3) relaxes ordering constraints between steps. We implemented Distill on a web interface and, through a crowdsourcing study, demonstrated its ability to elicit and refine user intent from initial task specifications.

</details>


### [270] [Energy-Efficient Quadruped Locomotion with Compliant Feet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14411)
*Pramod Pal,Shishir Kolathaya,Ashitava Ghosal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过强化学习验证了足部柔性对四足机器人能效的影响，发现合理配置足部刚度可降低约17%的机械能消耗，实现了运动效率提升与稳定性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四足机器人多采用刚性足，虽然控制简单，但无法吸收冲击力或回收弹性势能，导致移动过程中能量消耗较高。为了研究柔性足是否能改善能量效率并探索其对稳定性的影响。

Method: 1. 将足部柔性（不同弹簧刚度）集成到强化学习（RL） locomotion 控制器中；2. 在仿真环境下训练8种不同刚度对应的控制策略；3. 进行交叉评估，计算单位距离的机械能消耗；4. 在真实四足机器人硬件上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验结果显示，通过选择中间刚度的弹簧，相比于过硬或过软的足部设计，能量消耗降低了约17%；仿真结果表现出相似的趋势。

Conclusion: 引入适当的足部柔性（弹性）可以显著提升四足机器人的运动效率，且不会牺牲运动稳定性。研究表明存在一个最优的刚度范围，能有效降低机械能消耗。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are often designed with rigid feet to simplify control and maintain stable contact during locomotion. While this approach is straightforward, it limits the ability of the legs to absorb impact forces and reuse stored elastic energy, leading to higher energy expenditure during locomotion. To explore whether compliant feet can provide an advantage, we integrate foot compliance into a reinforcement learning (RL) locomotion controller and study its effect on walking efficiency. In simulation, we train eight policies corresponding to eight different spring stiffness values and then cross-evaluate their performance by measuring mechanical energy consumed per meter traveled. In experiments done on a developed quadruped, the energy consumption for the intermediate stiffness spring is lower by ~ 17% when compared to a very stiff or a very flexible spring incorporated in the feet, with similar trends appearing in the simulation results. These results indicate that selecting an appropriate foot compliance can improve locomotion efficiency without destabilizing the robot during motion.

</details>


### [271] [Before the Body Moves: Learning Anticipatory Joint Intent for Language-Conditioned Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14417)
*Haozhe Jia,Honglei Jin,Yuan Zhang,Youcheng Fan,Shaofeng Liang,Lei Wang,Shuxu Jin,Kuimou Yu,Zinuo Zhang,Jianfei Song,Wenshuo Chen,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DAJI 是一种为人形机器人设计的分层控制框架，通过引入预见性“意图块”生成与动作扩散策略，使机器人能根据自然语言实现更具物理前瞻性的全身动作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人语言控制系统大多依赖被动的低级跟踪器，或者缺乏对即将发生的接触切换、支撑转移及平衡准备等物理状态的显式预测，导致在流式指令执行中缺乏前瞻性。

Method: 提出了 DAJI (Dynamics-Aligned Joint Intent) 分层框架：1. DAJI-Flow：利用自回归模型将语言与意图历史映射为未来的“意图块”；2. DAJI-Act：一种基于扩散策略的策略，通过蒸馏具备未来意识（future-aware）的教师模型，构建可部署的闭环控制动作策略。

Result: 在 HumanML3D 数据集上实现了 94.42% 的 Rollout 成功率，在 BABEL 数据集上实现了 0.152 的子序列 FID，证明了该方法在潜在意图学习与流式指令跟随方面的优越性。

Conclusion: DAJI 通过引入分层结构，成功桥接了自然语言指令与人形机器人动力学控制之间的鸿沟，实现了能够主动规划接触变化与平衡的运动生成，为具身智能提供了更具前瞻性的控制范式。

Abstract: Natural language is an intuitive interface for humanoid robots, yet streaming whole-body control requires control representations that are executable now and anticipatory of future physical transitions. Existing language-conditioned humanoid systems typically generate kinematic references that a low-level tracker must repair reactively, or use latent/action policies whose outputs do not explicitly encode upcoming contact changes, support transfers, and balance preparation. We propose \textbf{DAJI} (\emph{Dynamics-Aligned Joint Intent}), a hierarchical framework that learns an anticipatory joint-intent interface between language generation and closed-loop control. DAJI-Act distills a future-aware teacher into a deployable diffusion action policy through student-driven rollouts, while DAJI-Flow autoregressively generates future intent chunks from language and intent history. Experiments show that DAJI achieves strong results in anticipatory latent learning, single-instruction generation, and streaming instruction following, reaching 94.42\% rollout success on HumanML3D-style generation and 0.152 subsequence FID on BABEL.

</details>


### [272] [SeaVis: Modeling and Control of a Remotely Operated Towed Vehicle for Seabed Visualization and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14683)
*Abdelhakim Amer,Aske Alstrup,Frederik Rasmussen,Yury Brodskiy,Andriy Sarabakha,Erdal Kayacan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于拖曳式水下机器人（ROTV）的增益调度LQR控制方案，在保证系统鲁棒性的同时提升了姿态控制效率，并通过开源仿真验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率海底测绘对水下机器人的定位精度和稳定性要求极高，现有控制方法在应对复杂环境扰动及多航速工况时存在不足。

Method: 1. 建立了针对SeaVis拖曳式水下机器人（ROTV）的数学建模；2. 设计了基于增益调度的线性二次型调节器（LQR）；3. 在高保真仿真环境中将其与传统PID控制器进行对比验证。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与PID控制器相比，LQR控制器在面对复杂海底地形时表现出更优的追踪性能，显著增强了系统鲁棒性，且大幅减少了襟翼（flap）执行器的动作频率。

Conclusion: 通过增益调度LQR控制策略，有效提升了ROTV在复杂工况下的深度与姿态控制性能，显著降低了执行器负载并增强了系统对扰动的鲁棒性，为高分辨率海底测绘提供了可靠的控制方案。

Abstract: High-resolution seafloor mapping necessitates stable and precise positioning for underwater robots. This paper introduces a novel mathematical model for SeaVis remotely operated towed vehicles (ROTVs) and develops a gain-scheduled linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) for robust depth and attitude control. We validate the approach in a high-fidelity simulation, benchmarking the LQR against a conventional PID controller over a challenging seabed profile. The presented results demonstrate the LQR's superior performance, with significantly enhanced robustness to disturbances, greater control efficiency, and substantially reduced flap actuation. The gain scheduling also confirms the controller's effectiveness across the full operational velocity range. The complete simulation environment and controller are open-sourced.

</details>


### [273] [SR-Platform: An Agentic Pipeline for Natural Language-Driven Robot Simulation Environment Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14700)
*Ben Wei Lim,Minh Duc Le,Thang Truong,Thanh Nguyen Canh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SR-Platform是一个自动化机器人仿真环境生成系统，利用LLM和多智能体工作流，将自然语言指令转化为可直接运行的MuJoCo仿真场景，极大地提高了机器人训练环境的构建效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人训练环境生成存在技术门槛高、耗时长等瓶颈，需手工进行3D建模与MJCF配置，限制了开发效率。

Method: 提出SR-Platform智能体系统，将场景生成解构为四个阶段：1. LLM调度器（意图转场景计划）；2. 资产锻造（生成或检索3D几何）；3. 布局架构师（分配位姿与约束验证）；4. 桥接层（整合MJCF与机器人模型）。系统基于九服务微服务架构，利用LLM-to-CadQuery、语义检索与缓存机制。

Result: 在30天的生产环境验证中，该系统完成611次成功调用。生成五对象场景的中位端到端延迟约为50秒（缓存加速后30-40秒），资产锻造的首次尝试重试率仅为11.3%。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了基于Agent的场景合成方法能够显著降低创建机器人训练环境的人力成本，使用户能够在不到一分钟的时间内从简单的英语提示生成可执行的MuJoCo场景。

Abstract: Generating robot simulation environments remains a major bottleneck in simulation-based robot learning. Constructing a training-ready MuJoCo scene typically requires expertise in 3D asset modeling, MJCF specification, spatial layout, collision avoidance, and robot-model integration. We present SR-Platform, a production-deployed agentic system that converts free-form natural language descriptions into executable, physically valid MuJoCo environments. SR-Platform decomposes scene synthesis into four stages: an LLM-based orchestrator that converts user intent into a structured scene plan; an asset forge that retrieves cached assets or generates new 3D geometry through LLM-to-CadQuery synthesis; a layout architect that assigns object poses and verifies industrial constraints; and a bridge layer that assembles the final MJCF scene and merges the selected robot model. The system is deployed as a nine-service Docker stack with WebSocket progress streaming, MinIO-backed mesh storage, Qdrant-based semantic asset retrieval, Redis job state, and InfluxDB telemetry. Using 30 days of production telemetry covering 611 successful LLM calls, SR-Platform generates five-object scenes with a median end-to-end latency of approximately 50 s, while cache-accelerated scenes complete in approximately 30-40 s. The asset forge shows an 11.3% first-attempt retry rate with automatic recovery, and cached asset retrieval removes per-object LLM calls for previously generated object types. These results show that agentic scene synthesis can reduce the manual effort required to create diverse robot training environments, enabling users to produce executable MuJoCo scenes from plain English prompts in under one minute.

</details>


### [274] [IntentVLA: Short-Horizon Intent Modeling for Aliased Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14712)
*Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Xiaopeng Lin,Zhaolong Shen,Laurence Tianruo Yang,Yurun Jin,Haishan Liu,Changti Wu,Hang Yuan,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多模态机器人模仿学习中的意图歧义问题，本文提出了 IntentVLA。该方法通过历史上下文编码来学习稳定的动作意图，显著提升了机器人作业的执行稳定性，并在多个主流机器人基准测试中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的帧级别 VLA 模型在面对多模态数据时，由于缺乏对短期任务意图或上下文的记忆，容易在相邻决策步产生意图漂移，导致动作冲突和执行不稳定。

Method: 提出 IntentVLA 框架，核心是利用历史观测编码生成短时意图表征（short-horizon intent representation）来引导动作预测；同时构建了 AliasBench 评测集，专门用于评估短期观测混淆环境下的模型性能。

Result: 在 AliasBench、SimplerEnv、LIBERO 和 RoboCasa 等多个测试平台上，IntentVLA 在执行稳定性和整体任务成功率上均显著优于现有的 VLA 基线模型。

Conclusion: IntentVLA通过引入短时意图表征有效解决了机器人 imitation learning 中的多模态歧义和动作不一致性问题，为实现更稳定的长序列机器人操作提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Robot imitation data are often multimodal: similar visual-language observations may be followed by different action chunks because human demonstrators act with different short-horizon intents, task phases, or recent context. Existing frame-conditioned VLA policies infer each chunk from the current observation and instruction alone, so under partial observability they may resample different intents across adjacent replanning steps, leading to inter-chunk conflict and unstable execution. We introduce IntentVLA, a history-conditioned VLA framework that encodes recent visual observations into a compact short-horizon intent representation and uses it to condition chunk generation. We further introduce AliasBench, a 12-task ambiguity-aware benchmark on RoboTwin2 with matched training data and evaluation environments that isolate short-horizon observation aliasing. Across AliasBench, SimplerEnv, LIBERO, and RoboCasa, IntentVLA improves rollout stability and outperforms strong VLA baselines

</details>


### [275] [Exploring Bottlenecks in VLM-LLM Navigation: How 3D Scene Understanding Capability Impacts Zero-Shot VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14801)
*Ziyi Xia,Chaoran Xiong,Litao Wei,Xinhao Hu,Ling Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对零样本VLN中感知精度与计算效率的矛盾，本文量化了3D场景理解对导航的影响，提出了感知饱和的概念，并主张VLN领域的视觉感知模型设计应弱化像素级精度，侧重于导航核心语义和空间比例的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的零样本VLN研究过度集中于高精度的像素级3D场景建模，这与具身导航对实时性、低算力消耗的实际需求存在冲突。作者旨在探究场景理解精度与导航性能之间的真实相关性，以消除感知瓶颈。

Method: 1. 基于VLM-LLM框架定义了两个核心子系统（LLM规划器和快速响应导航器）的统计成功率（SR）上限；2. 利用前沿3D场景理解模型对所提边界进行验证；3. 量化3D感知能力对VLN性能的实际影响。

Result: 证实了感知饱和现象，即更高级的视觉感知模型并不总是能显著提升导航任务的成功率。导航系统的成功更多依赖于导航相关的核心词汇理解和准确的边界框定位，而非像素级的细粒度分析。

Conclusion: 在VLN任务中，3D场景理解的视觉精度存在“饱和现象”，超越一定阈值后性能提升边际效应递减。开发应从追求像素级精度转向关注导航相关的核心语义、边界框比例及实时计算效率。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-and-language navigation (VLN) has gained significant attention due to its minimal data collection costs and inherent generalization. This paradigm is typically driven by the integration of pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and Large Language Models (LLMs), where VLMs construct 3D scene graphs while LLMs handle high-level reasoning and decision-making. However, a critical bottleneck exists in this system: current 3D perception models prioritize pixel-level accuracy, directly conflicting with the strict computational limits and real-time efficiency demanded by embodied navigation. To address this gap, this paper quantifies the actual impact of 3D scene understanding capability on VLN performance. Based on typical VLM-LLM frameworks, we propose statistical success rate (SR) upper bounds for two core subsystems: 1) the slow LLM planner, which relies on topological mapping semantics, and 2) the fast reactive navigator, which utilizes spatial coordinates and bounding boxes to execute LLM decisions. Evaluations using state-of-the-art 3D scene understanding models validate our proposed bounds and reveal a perception saturation phenomenon, indicating that improvements in perception accuracy beyond a certain threshold yield diminishing returns in navigation success. Our findings suggest that 3D scene understanding for VLN should pivot away from strict pixel-level precision, prioritizing instead navigation-relevant core vocabularies and accurate bounding box proportions.

</details>


### [276] [Learning Cross-Coupled and Regime Dependent Dynamics for Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14805)
*Rishabh Dev Yadav,Samaksh Ujjawal,Sihao Sun,Spandan Roy,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对空中操作系统的复杂动力学建模难题，提出了一种结构化残差学习框架，由非线性编码器捕捉时序耦合，结合线性贝叶斯解码器实现在线快速自适应，显著提升了模型预测控制的性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中操纵系统由于四旋翼与机械臂的强耦合、气动延迟以及载荷/配置变化引起的动态非平稳性，导致传统分析模型或离线学习模型在实际部署中性能下降。

Method: 提出了一种结构化的编码器-解码器框架：(1) 使用非线性潜空间编码器从状态-输入历史中捕捉交叉变量耦合与时间依赖性；(2) 使用轻量级线性层作为解码器，支持在非平稳动态下进行在线贝叶斯参数更新，并引入一致性驱动的协方差膨胀机制以提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实空中操作平台上的实验表明，该方法提升了残差预测精度，在动态操作条件下实现了更快的模型适应能力，并显著增强了基于MPC的轨迹跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 联合建模耦合时序动力学与部署阶段的非平稳性，对于实现可靠的空中操作至关重要。该方法通过结合深度学习的表征能力与贝叶斯在线更新的灵活性，有效解决了空中操作系统的复杂动态建模难题。

Abstract: Accurate dynamics models are critical for aerial manipulators operating under complex tasks such as payload transport. However, modeling these systems remains fundamentally challenging due to strong quadrotor-manipulator coupling, delayed aerodynamic interactions, and regime-dependent dynamics variations arising from payload changes and manipulator reconfiguration. These effects produce residual dynamics that are simultaneously cross-coupled, history-dependent, and nonstationary, causing both analytical models and purely offline learned models to degrade during deployment. To address these challenges, we propose a structured encoder-decoder framework for adaptive residual dynamics learning in aerial manipulators. The proposed nonlinear latent encoder captures cross-variable coupling and temporal dependencies from state-input histories, while a lightweight linear latent decoder enables online adaptation under regime-dependent nonstationary dynamics. The linear-in-parameter decoder structure permits closed-form Bayesian adaptation together with consistency-driven covariance inflation, enabling rapid and stable adaptation to both transient and slowly varying dynamics changes while remaining compatible with real-time model predictive control (MPC). Experimental results on a real aerial manipulation platform demonstrate improved residual prediction accuracy, faster adaptation under changing operating conditions, and enhanced MPC-based trajectory tracking performance. These results highlight the importance of jointly modeling coupled temporal dynamics and deployment-time nonstationarity for reliable aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [277] [CaMeRL: Collision-Aware and Memory-Enhanced Reinforcement Learning for UAV Navigation in Multi-Scale Obstacle Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14810)
*Hong Hong,Feiyu Liao,Yongheng Liang,Boning Zhang,Haitao Wang,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决无人机在多尺度避障中的难题，本文提出了 CaMeRL 框架，通过引入碰撞感知特征增强对小障碍物的识别，并利用时域记忆模块缓解大障碍物遮挡带来的信息缺失，显著提升了导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无人机避障方法通常只关注障碍物的数量或密度，忽略了尺度差异。现有方法在处理多尺度障碍物时，因仅依赖单帧几何特征，常导致遗漏小物体或在应对大障碍物遮挡时丢失空间上下文信息。

Method: 提出了 CaMeRL 框架：1. 碰撞感知潜空间表示（Collision-aware latent representation）：对深度信息进行风险敏感编码，以提取细粒度的障碍物特征；2. 时域记忆模块（Temporal memory module）：通过跨帧观测整合信息，克服大障碍物遮挡导致的局部观测缺失。

Result: 在多种尺度障碍物实验中，CaMeRL 均优于当前 SOTA 方法。在处理超小障碍物和超大障碍物场景下，成功率分别提升了 0.48 和 0.28，并在复杂的户外环境中实现了可靠导航。

Conclusion: CaMeRL 通过引入碰撞感知特征和时域记忆模块，有效解决了 UAV 在多尺度障碍物环境下的避障难题，显著提升了导航的稳健性和成功率，特别是在处理超小和超大障碍物方面表现优异。

Abstract: In obstacle avoidance navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), variations in obstacle scale have received strangely less attention than obstacle number or density. Existing methods typically extract purely geometric features from single-frame depth observations. Such representations tend to neglect small obstacles and lose spatial context under occlusions caused by large obstacles, leading to noticeable degradation in environments with multi-scale obstacles. To address this issue, we propose CaMeRL, a Collision-aware and Memory-enhanced Reinforcement Learning framework for UAV navigation. The collision-aware latent representation encodes risk-sensitive depth cues to preserve fine-grained obstacle structures, thereby improving sensitivity to small obstacles. The temporal memory module integrates observations across frames, mitigating partial observability caused by large-obstacle occlusions. We evaluate CaMeRL with multi-scale obstacles, including ultra-small and extra-large obstacle settings. Results show that CaMeRL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all scales, with success rate gains of 0.48 and 0.28 in the ultra-small and extra-large settings, respectively. More importantly, CaMeRL achieves reliable navigation in cluttered outdoor environments.

</details>


### [278] [Learning Direct Control Policies with Flow Matching for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14832)
*Marcello Ceresini,Federico Pirazzoli,Andrea Bertogalli,Lorenzo Cipelli,Filippo D'Addeo,Anthony Dell'Eva,Alessandro Paolo Capasso,Alberto Broggi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于流匹配的自动驾驶规划器，通过BEV视角和高效ODE积分提供低延迟、高泛化的实时闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决自动驾驶中低延迟实时闭环重规划的需求，并提高规划器在分布外（OOD）场景下的泛化能力。

Method: 提出一种流匹配（Flow-matching）规划器，输入为鸟瞰图（BEV）栅格，直接输出加速度和曲率剖面；采用少量常微分方程（ODE）积分步骤进行推断，训练于城市仿真环境。

Result: 模型在包括多车道高速公路及未见过的城市场景在内的分布外环境下，表现出稳定的闭环控制能力，成功完成各种复杂任务。

Conclusion: 基于流匹配（Flow-matching）的规划器表现出强大的泛化能力，能够通过BEV几何表示和光滑向量场学习，在未见过的复杂场景中实现稳定的闭环控制，证明了该方法在自动驾驶避障与导航中的有效性。

Abstract: We present a flow-matching planner for autonomous driving that directly outputs actionable control trajectories defined by acceleration and curvature profiles. The model is conditioned on a bird's-eye-view (BEV) raster of the surrounding scene and generates control sequences in a small number of Ordinary Differential Equations (ODE) integration steps, enabling low-latency inference suitable for real-time closed-loop re-planning. We train exclusively on urban scenarios (real urban city streets, intersections and roundabouts of the city of Parma, Italy) collected from a 2D traffic simulator with reactive agents, and evaluate in closed-loop on both in-distribution and markedly out-of-distribution environments, including multi-lane highways and unseen urban scenarios. Our results show that the model generalizes reliably to these unseen conditions, maintaining stable closed-loop control and successfully completing scenarios that differ substantially from the training distribution. We attribute this to the BEV representation, which provides a geometry-centric view of the scene that is inherently less sensitive to distributional shifts, and to the flow-matching formulation, which learns a smooth vector field that degrades gracefully under distribution shift. We provide video demonstrations of closed-loop behavior at https://marcelloceresini.github.io/DirectControlFlowMatching.

</details>


### [279] [Chrono-Gymnasium: An Open-Source, Gymnasium-Compatible Distributed Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14911)
*Bocheng Zou,Harry Zhang,Khailanii Slaton,Jingquan Wang,Derrick Ruan,Huzaifa Mustafa Unjhawala,Radu Serban,Dan Negrut*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Chrono-Gymnasium 是一个基于 Ray 的分布式框架，将 Project Chrono 高保真物理仿真与 Gymnasium 标准无缝整合，通过集群并行计算解决了复杂机器人任务中高计算开销带来的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真物理模拟对于缩短机器人领域“仿真到实物”（sim-to-real）的差距至关重要，但传统模拟引擎的高计算开销限制了其在强化学习（RL）和全局优化等数据密集型任务中的应用。

Method: 基于 Ray 分布式框架构建，将 Project Chrono 的多体动力学模拟扩展至分布式计算集群；提供标准的 Gymnasium 接口，支持分布式执行的同步与消息传递机制。

Result: 在机器人复杂地形自主导航强化学习及行星着陆器参数贝叶斯优化两个案例中，Chrono-Gymnasium 显著缩短了模拟任务的运行时间，且未牺牲物理精度。

Conclusion: Chrono-Gymnasium 成功将高保真物理引擎与分布式计算结合，不仅降低了复杂任务的计算时间，还保持了物理准确性，为复杂机器人系统的设计与控制提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity physics simulation is essential for closing the sim-to-real gap in robotics and complex mechanical systems. However, the computational overhead of high-fidelity engines often limits their use in data-intensive tasks like Reinforcement Learning (RL) and global optimization. We introduce Chrono-Gymnasium, a distributed computing framework that scales the high-fidelity multi-body dynamics of Project Chrono across large-scale computing clusters. Built upon the Ray framework, Chrono-Gymnasium provides a standardized Gymnasium interface, enabling seamless integration with modern machine learning libraries while providing built-in synchronization and messaging primitives for distributed execution. We demonstrate the framework's capabilities through two distinct case studies: (1) the training of an RL agent for autonomous robotic navigation in complex terrains, and (2) the Bayesian Optimization of a planetary lander's design parameters to ensure landing stability. Our results show that Chrono-Gymnasium reduces wall-clock time for high-fidelity simulations without sacrificing physical accuracy, offering a scalable path for the design and control of complex robotic systems.

</details>


### [280] [Behavioral Data-Driven Optimal Trajectory Generation for Rotary Cranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14944)
*Iskandar Khemakhem,Manuel Zobel,Johannes Schüle,Oliver Sawodny,Naoki Uchiyama,Abdallah Farrage*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需精确模型的数据驱动轨迹生成框架，利用有限的输入输出数据通过凸优化实现旋转起重机的减摆控制，在显著提升性能（减摆、提速、精度）的同时降低了人工调试成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对建筑行业自动化需求，传统基于模型的控制方法依赖精确模型且调参复杂，而现有机器学习方法又过于依赖大规模数据集，因此亟需一种能降低专家知识依赖、适用于欠驱动系统且高效的数据驱动轨迹生成方法。

Method: 基于Willems基本引理及其推广，提出了一种行为数据驱动的非参数化框架。该方法直接利用测量到的输入输出数据进行建模，避开了显式动力学建模；通过凸优化技术，在有限数据条件下生成平滑且满足负载减摆要求的开环旋转轨迹。

Result: 在实验室起重机平台上进行验证，实验结果表明：与传统的基于模型的方法相比，负载摆动减少了35%，跟踪误差降低了43%，运行时间缩短了50%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过数据驱动策略有效解决了起重机非线性、欠驱动系统的轨迹生成问题，在无需人工精确建模的情况下，显著提升了控制精度、运动效率及负载稳定性，证明了基于行为的数据驱动控制在实际工业应用中的优越性。

Abstract: With the growth of the construction industry and the global shortage of skilled labor, the automation of crane control has become increasingly important for safe and efficient operations. A central challenge in automatic crane control is the reduction of load oscillations during motion, which is primarily addressed through appropriate slewing trajectories. In this context, classical model-based control methods rely on accurate dynamical models and expert tuning, and often struggle to meet safety and precision requirements, while many learning-based approaches require large data sets and significant computational resources. This paper proposes a behavioral data-driven framework for generating open-loop slewing trajectories for rotary cranes that suppress load sway while reducing operation time and energy consumption. The approach builds on Willems' fundamental lemma and its generalizations, to bypass explicit system modeling and operate directly on measured input-output data. A practical workflow is presented in this paper to reduce the need for expert knowledge. Despite the underactuated nature of the crane dynamics, the method identifies a nonparametric representation of the system behavior and generates smooth, optimal trajectories using limited data and convex optimization. The proposed trajectory generation method is validated on a laboratory crane setup and compared against an established model-based approach, achieving up to 35% reduction in load sway, 43% reduction in tracking error, and 50% reduction in travel time.

</details>


### [281] [A Prototyping Framework for Distributed Control of Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15049)
*Junaid Ahmed Memon,Allan Andre Do Nascimento,Kostas Margellos,Antonis Papachristodoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于SPMD范式的多机器人分布式控制原型验证框架，支持从仿真到硬件的跨层级测试，为分布式优化算法的验证提供了一套低成本且高效的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有研究中分布式优化算法理论与实际测试之间存在脱节的问题，旨在构建一个用于多机器人系统分布式控制的原型设计框架。

Method: 采用“单程序多数据”（SPMD）范式，在单台计算机上模拟分布式控制；支持点质量模型、高保真四旋翼模型以及基于Crazyflie四旋翼的硬件实验平台等多种动力学层级。

Result: 通过四旋翼位置交换任务对非合作博弈分布式算法进行了验证，对比了不同动力学层级的计算时间和轨迹数据，证明了该方法的一致性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种低成本且易于访问的方法，能够有效验证分布式控制算法，并成功实现了从算法理论到实际硬件测试的桥接。

Abstract: This paper presents a prototyping framework for distributed control of multi-robot systems, aimed at bridging theory and practical testing of distributed optimization algorithms. Using the Single Program, Multiple Data (SPMD) paradigm, the framework emulates distributed control on a single computer, with each core running the same algorithm using local states and neighbour-to-neighbour communication. We demonstrate the framework on a four-quadrotor position-swapping task using a non-cooperative game-theoretic distributed algorithm. Computational time and trajectory data are compared across the supported dynamics levels: a point-mass model, a high-fidelity quadrotor model, and an experimental hardware testbed using Crazyflie quadcopters. The results show that the framework provides a low-cost and accessible approach for validating distributed algorithms.

</details>


### [282] [SOCC-ICP: Semantics-Assisted Odometry based on Occupancy Grids and ICP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15074)
*Johannes Scherer,Sebastian Hirt,Henri Meeß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOCC-ICP 是一种将激光里程计与语义占据栅格映射统一起来的框架，通过在体素级别融合几何与语义信息，在保证鲁棒姿态估计的同时，直接生成适用于下游任务的地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LiDAR 里程计地图表征（如点、面、NDT）与下游任务常用的语义占据栅格地图之间存在割裂，导致地图结构冗余。旨在解决在未见环境下实现鲁棒姿态估计，并同时构建下游任务可用的地图的需求。

Method: 提出 SOCC-ICP 框架，采用语义占据栅格（Semantic Occupancy Grid）作为统一地图表征；每个体素编码几何与语义统计信息，实现自适应的 ICP（点对点或点对面），并通过射线投射（raycasting）机制进行动态物体滤除。

Result: 在多种场景下表现与先进的 LiDAR 里程计相当；在几何退化环境下鲁棒性更强；引入语义标签后，能够进一步提升匹配精度。

Conclusion: SOCC-ICP 通过整合语义占据栅格地图与 LiDAR 里程计，消除了冗余地图结构，并为机器人下游任务（如运动规划）提供了直接可用的统一表征，证明了该方法在处理几何退化环境及提升定位精度方面的有效性。

Abstract: Reliable pose estimation in previously unseen environments is a fundamental capability of autonomous systems. Existing LiDAR odometry methods typically employ point-, surfel-, or NDT-based map representations, which are distinct from the semantic occupancy grids commonly used for downstream tasks such as motion planning. We introduce SOCC-ICP, a semantics-assisted odometry framework that jointly performs Semantic OCCupancy grid mapping and LiDAR scan alignment. Each map voxel encodes geometric and semantic statistics, enabling adaptive point-to-point or point-to-plane ICP based on local planarity. Further, the occupancy grid naturally filters dynamic objects through raycasting-based free-space updates. Across diverse evaluation scenarios, SOCC-ICP achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art LiDAR odometry and remains robust in geometrically degenerate environments, even in the absence of semantic cues. When semantic labels are available, integrating them into map construction, downsampling, and correspondence weighting yields further accuracy gains. By unifying odometry and semantic occupancy grid mapping within a single representation, SOCC-ICP eliminates redundant map structures and directly provides a map suitable for downstream robotic applications.

</details>


### [283] [CLOVER: Closed-Loop Value Estimation \& Ranking for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15120)
*Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Canyu Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLOVER是一种端到端自动驾驶规划框架，通过闭环价值估计与评分器引导的轨迹重排序，解决了传统模仿学习中训练与评估指标不匹配的瓶颈，显著提升了自动驾驶决策的安全性和合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶存在“训练-评估不匹配”问题：模仿学习仅基于单一真实轨迹训练，而评估依赖多维规则指标。这导致模型在测试时无法识别偏离示范但依然安全合规的更优路径，限制了规划器的上限。

Method: 提出CLOVER架构：1. 采用生成器-评分器两阶段流水线；2. 利用评估器筛选高质量轨迹并使用集合覆盖监督训练生成器；3. 执行闭环自蒸馏（Closed-loop Self-distillation），即评分器训练于真实评估指标，生成器向评分器选出的Top-k及Pareto最优目标靠拢，并保持保守更新以保证稳定性。

Result: 在NAVSIM基准测试上达到94.5 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS（SOTA）；在NavHard困难场景下取得48.3 EPDMS；在nuScenes开环评估中实现最低L2误差和碰撞率。

Conclusion: CLOVER通过引入闭环价值估计与排序框架，有效解决了端到端自动驾驶中训练与评估标准不一致的问题。实验证明，在闭环机制下利用评估器评分筛选和优化轨迹，能显著提升规划性能及其在复杂场景下的泛化能力，确立了新的SOTA表现。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving planners are commonly trained by imitating a single logged trajectory, yet evaluated by rule-based planning metrics that measure safety, feasibility, progress, and comfort. This creates a training--evaluation mismatch: trajectories close to the logged path may violate planning rules, while alternatives farther from the demonstration can remain valid and high-scoring. The mismatch is especially limiting for proposal-selection planners, whose performance depends on candidate-set coverage and scorer ranking quality. We propose CLOVER, a Closed-LOop Value Estimation and Ranking framework for end-to-end autonomous driving planning. CLOVER follows a lightweight generator--scorer formulation: a generator produces diverse candidate trajectories, and a scorer predicts planning-metric sub-scores to rank them at inference time. To expand proposal support beyond single-trajectory imitation, CLOVER constructs evaluator-filtered pseudo-expert trajectories and trains the generator with set-level coverage supervision. It then performs conservative closed-loop self-distillation: the scorer is fitted to true evaluator sub-scores on generated proposals, while the generator is refined toward teacher-selected top-$k$ and vector-Pareto targets with stability regularization. We analyze when an imperfect scorer can improve the generator, showing that scorer-mediated refinement is reliable when scorer-selected targets are enriched under the true evaluator and updates remain conservative. On NAVSIM, CLOVER achieves 94.5 PDMS and 90.4 EPDMS, establishing a new state of the art. On the more challenging NavHard split, it obtains 48.3 EPDMS, matching the strongest reported result. On supplementary nuScenes open-loop evaluation, CLOVER achieves the lowest L2 error and collision rate among compared methods. Code data will be released at https://github.com/WilliamXuanYu/CLOVER.

</details>


### [284] [Pelican-Unified 1.0: A Unified Embodied Intelligence Model for Understanding, Reasoning, Imagination and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15153)
*Yi Zhang,Yinda Chen,Che Liu,Zeyuan Ding,Jin Xu,Shilong Zou,Junwei Liao,Jiayu Hu,Xiancong Ren,Xiaopeng Zhang,Yechi Liu,Haoyuan Shi,Zecong Tang,Haosong Sun,Renwen Cui,Kuishu Wu,Wenhai Liu,Yang Xu,Yingji Zhang,Yidong Wang,Senkang Hu,Jinpeng Lu,Nga Teng Chan,Yechen Wu,Yong Dai,Jian Tang,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pelican-Unified 1.0 是首个基于统一原则的具身基础模型，通过单一 VLM 实现感知、推理、想象和动作的联合建模与优化，在保持各领域高性能的同时实现了系统的深度整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统具身智能中感知、推理、想象和行动被拆分为独立专家系统所导致的通信损耗和系统复杂性问题，提出“统一（Unification）”原则。

Method: Pelican-Unified 1.0 架构：
1. 统一理解与推理：利用单个视觉语言模型（VLM）将场景、指令、视觉上下文和动作历史映射到共享语义空间，并进行自回归推理，生成思维链。
2. 统一未来生成：采用统一未来生成器（UFG），基于推理锁定的潜变量，在同一去噪过程中联合生成未来视频和未来动作。
3. 联合优化：将语言、视频和动作损失函数反向传播至共享表示层，实现多任务协同优化。

Result: 在 VLM 基准测试中获得 64.7 分（同规模最佳），在 WorldArena 排名第一（66.03），在 RoboTwin 系统中获得第二名的优秀行动表现。

Conclusion: 统一范式（Unification Paradigm）能够成功在单一模型中融合感知、推理、想象和行动等能力，且不会牺牲单一领域的专家性能。

Abstract: We present Pelican-Unified 1.0, the first embodied foundation model trained according to the principle of unification. Pelican-Unified 1.0 uses a single VLM as a unified understanding module, mapping scenes, instructions, visual contexts, and action histories into a shared semantic space. The same VLM also serves as a unified reasoning module, autoregressively producing task-, action-, and future-oriented chains of thought in a single forward pass and projecting the final hidden state into a dense latent variable. A Unified Future Generator (UFG) then conditions on this latent variable and jointly generates future videos and future actions through two modality-specific output heads within the same denoising process. The language, video, and action losses are all backpropagated into the shared representation, enabling the model to jointly optimize understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action during training, rather than training three isolated expert systems.
  Experiments demonstrate that unification does not imply compromise. With a single checkpoint, Pelican-Unified 1.0 achieves strong performance across all three capabilities: 64.7 on eight VLM benchmarks, the best among comparable-scale models; 66.03 on WorldArena, ranking first; and 93.5 on RoboTwin, the second-best average among compared action methods. These results show that the unified paradigm succeeds in preserving specialist strength while bringing understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action into one model.

</details>


### [285] [Hand-in-the-Loop: Improving Dexterous VLA via Seamless Interventional Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15157)
*Zhuohang Li,Liqun Huang,Wei Xu,Zhengming Zhu,Nie Lin,Xiao Ma,Xinjun Sheng,Ruoshi Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决机器人灵巧手交互式模仿学习中接管时的手势跳变问题，作者提出了 HandITL 方法，通过平滑融合人类意图与自主策略，大幅提升了操作稳定性与策略微调的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型在处理高维度、接触丰富的灵巧操作时，容易因策略偏差产生复合误差；而传统的交互式模仿学习在人类接管时会导致机器人手势发生剧烈突变（Gesture jumps），影响操作稳定性。

Method: 提出 Hand-in-the-Loop (HandITL) 方法。该方法在人类接管时，不直接切换控制权，而是将人类的校正指令与自动策略进行平滑融合（blending），防止控制状态突变（Gesture jumps）。

Result: 相比于直接接管，HandITL 将接管抖动降低了 99.8%，抓取失败率降低 87.5%，完成时间缩短 19.1%；利用 HandITL 收集的数据进行策略微调，在长序列灵巧任务中的表现比标准遥操作数据高出 19%。

Conclusion: HandITL 通过融合人类校正意图与自主策略，有效解决了高自由度（DoF）机器人灵巧操作中的“手势跳变”问题，显著提升了交互式模仿学习的安全性和策略微调效果。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are prone to compounding errors in dexterous manipulation, where high-dimensional action spaces and contact-rich dynamics amplify small policy deviations over long horizons. While Interactive Imitation Learning (IIL) can refine policies through human takeover data, applying it to high-degree-of-freedom (DoF) robotic hands remains challenging due to a command mismatch between human teleoperation and policy execution at the takeover moment, which causes abrupt robot-hand configuration changes, or "gesture jumps". We present Hand-in-the-Loop (HandITL), a seamless human-in-the-loop intervention method that blends human corrective intent with autonomous policy execution to avoid gesture jumps during bimanual dexterous manipulation. Compared with direct teleoperation takeover, HandITL reduces takeover jitter by 99.8% and preserves robust post-takeover manipulation, reducing grasp failures by 87.5% and mean completion time by 19.1%. We validate HandITL on tasks requiring bimanual coordination, tool use, and fine-grained long-horizon manipulation. When used to collect intervention data for policy refinement, HandITL yields policies that outperform those trained with standard teleoperation data by 19% on average across three long-horizon dexterous tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [Thinking Ahead: Prospection-Guided Retrieval of Memory with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14177)
*Harshita Chopra,Krishna Kant Chintalapudi,Suman Nath,Ryen W. White,Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对长程对话检索中语义关联弱的问题，该论文提出 Prospection-Guided Retrieval (PGR) 方法，通过利用检索前的多步逻辑推演（前瞻）来引导更精准的事实获取，显著优于传统的相似度检索基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RAG 和 GraphRAG 系统过于依赖嵌入语义相似度，难以处理长程交互中“与查询语义距离远但对用户至关重要”的事实检索挑战。

Method: 提出“前瞻引导检索”（Prospection-Guided Retrieval, PGR）：利用思维树（ToT）或思维链对用户查询进行推演，生成多个可能的后续步骤作为检索探针，并将检索到的事实整合进下一轮前瞻中，实现迭代式检索。引入 MemoryQuest 基准测试进行验证。

Result: 在 MemoryQuest 上实现了近 3 倍的召回率提升；在 1,625 个查询场景中，PGR 生成的响应在 LLM-as-judge 和人类盲测中表现出压倒性优势（89-98% 的偏好率）。

Conclusion: 显式的前瞻思维（Prospection）能显著提升对话助手在长程交互中的事实检索能力与响应质量，克服了传统语义相似度检索的局限性。

Abstract: Long-horizon personalization requires dialogue assistants to retrieve user-specific facts from extended interaction histories. In practice, many relevant facts often have low semanticsimilarity to the query under dense retrieval. Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and GraphRAG systems are still largely retrospective: they rely on embedding similarity to the query or on fixed graph traversals, so they often miss facts that matter for the user's needs but lie far from the query in embedding space. Inspired by prospection, the human ability to use imagined futures as cues for recall, we introduce Prospection-Guided Retrieval (PGR), which decouples retrieval from how memories are stored. Given a user query, PGR first expands the goal into a short Tree-of-Thought (ToT) or linear chain of plausible next steps, and uses these steps as retrieval probes rather than relying on the original query alone. The facts retrieved by these probes are then used to personalize the next round of prospection, enabling PGR to uncover additional memories that become relevant only after the simulation is grounded in the user's history. We also introduce MemoryQuest, a challenging multi-session benchmark in which each query is annotated with 3--5 dated reference facts subject to a low query-reference similarity constraint. Across 1,625 queries spanning 185 user profiles from 3 publicly available datasets, PGR-TOT substantially improves retrieval, including nearly 3x recall on MemoryQuest over the strongest baseline. In pairwise LLM-as-judge comparisons against baselines, PGR-generated responses are preferred on 89--98% of queries, with blinded human annotations on held-out subsets showing the same trend. Overall, the results demonstrate that explicit prospection yields large gains in long-horizon retrieval and response quality relative to similarity-only baselines.

</details>


### [287] [Efficient Generative Retrieval for E-commerce Search with Semantic Cluster IDs and Expert-Guided RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14434)
*Jianbo Zhu,Xing Fang,Jing Wang,Mingmin Jin,Bokang Wang,Guangxin Song,Zhenyu Xie,Junjie Bai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CQ-SID和EG-GRPO的生成式召回框架，专门适配电商搜索场景，通过分层语义ID编码降低计算开销，并利用强化学习对齐下游排序目标，有效解决了生成式检索在工业生产环境落地难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式检索方案虽然能够实现端到端检索，但在工业级电商搜索场景中，面临大规模动态商品池、高延迟限制以及与下游目标不一致等挑战，难以直接替代传统多阶段检索流程。

Method: 1. CQ-SID：采用类别感知与查询-商品对比学习，结合残差量化变分自编码器（RQ-VAE）将商品编码为分层语义ID，降低束搜索复杂度；2. EG-GRPO：一种专家引导的组相对策略优化方法，通过引入真实样本注入，在稀疏奖励环境下对齐生成式召回与下游排序目标。

Result: 1. 离线实验：CQ-SID在语义和个性化点击命中率上较RQ-VAE基线分别提升26.76%和11.11%，束搜索空间减少50%；2. 在线A/B测试：GMV提升1.15%，UCTCVR提升0.40%；3. 生产价值：该通道贡献了超过50%的曝光、58%的点击和72%的购买量。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了将生成式检索作为工业级电商搜索中召回阶段补充手段的可行性与优越性。通过CQ-SID和EG-GRPO，模型成功解决了大规模动态商品目录下的搜索延迟和下游排序对齐问题，在线上生产环境（天猫APP）中实现了GMV和购买转化率的显著提升。

Abstract: Generative retrieval offers a promising alternative by unifying the fragmented multi-stage retrieval process into a single end-to-end model. However, its practical adoption in industrial e-commerce search remains challenging, given the massive and dynamic product catalogs, strict latency requirements, and the need to align retrieval with downstream ranking goals. In this work, we propose a retrieval framework tailored for real-world recall scenarios, positioning generative retrieval as a recall-stage supplement rather than an end-to-end replacement. Our method, CQ-SID (Category-and-Query constrained Semantic ID), employs category-aware and query-item contrastive learning along with Residual Quantized VAEs to encode items into hierarchical semantic cluster identifiers, significantly reducing beam search complexity. Additionally, we develop EG-GRPO (Expert-Guided Group Relative Policy Optimization), a reinforcement learning approach that aligns generative recall with downstream ranking under sparse rewards by injecting ground-truth samples to stabilize training. Offline experiments on TmallAPP search logs show that CQ-SID achieves up to 26.76% and 11.11% relative gains in semantic and personalized click hitrate over RQ-VAE baselines, while halving beam search size. EG-GRPO further improves multi-objective performance. Online A/B tests confirm gains in GMV (+1.15%) and UCTCVR (+0.40%). The generative recall channel now contributes substantially in production, accounting for over 50.25% of exposures, 58.96% of clicks, and 72.63% of purchases, demonstrating a viable path for deploying generative retrieval in real-world e-commerce systems.

</details>


### [288] [Stop Overthinking: Unlocking Efficient Listwise Reranking with Minimal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14450)
*Danyang Liu,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 重排序中 CoT 推理冗余导致计算成本过高的问题，提出一种通过 Pareto 过滤机制进行的自蒸馏方法，在保证检索效果的同时显著压缩了推理长度与系统延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于思维链（CoT）的 LLM 重排序虽然效果好，但存在“过度思考（overthinking）”现象，即过长的推理会导致边际效用递减，且带来高昂的计算成本。

Method: 提出 Length-Regularized Self-Distillation（长度正则化自蒸馏）框架：1. 从教师模型（Rank-K）采集多样化推理轨迹；2. 使用 Pareto 最优化准则过滤出兼顾性能与低消耗的高质量推理链；3. 对学生模型进行微调，使其内化高效的推理逻辑。

Result: 在 TREC Deep Learning 和 NeuCLIR 基准测试中，该方法能够在维持教师模型检索效果的前提下，将推理 Token 消耗降低 34%-37%。

Conclusion: 通过 Length-Regularized Self-Distillation 框架，模型能够学习高效的推理模式，在保持检索精度的同时显著降低推理 Token 消耗，为推理增强的重排序模型在延迟敏感场景下的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Listwise reranking utilizing Large Language Models (LLMs) has achieved state-of-the-art retrieval effectiveness. Recently, reasoning-enhanced models have further pushed these boundaries by employing Chain-of-Thought (CoT) to perform deep comparative analysis of candidate documents. However, this performance gain comes at a prohibitive computational cost, as models often generate thousands of reasoning tokens before producing a final ranking. In this work, we investigate the relationship between reasoning length and ranking quality, revealing an overthinking phenomenon where extended reasoning yields diminishing returns. To address this, we propose a Length-Regularized Self-Distillation framework. We synthesize a dataset by sampling diverse reasoning traces from a teacher model (Rank-K) and applying a Pareto-inspired filter to select traces that achieve high ranking performance with minimal token usage. By fine-tuning on these concise, high-quality rationales, the student model learns to internalize efficient reasoning patterns, effectively pruning redundant deliberation. Experiments on TREC Deep Learning and NeuCLIR benchmarks demonstrate that our method maintains the teacher's effectiveness while reducing inference token consumption by 34%-37% across different retrieval settings, offering a practical solution for deploying reasoning-enhanced rerankers in latency-sensitive applications.

</details>


### [289] [Asymmetric Generative Recommendation via Multi-Expert Projection and Multi-Faceted Hierarchical Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14512)
*Bin Huang,Xin Wang,Junwei Pan,Yongqi Zhou,Yifeng Zhou,Zhixiang Feng,Shudong Huang,Haijie Gu,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 为解决生成式推荐模型中语义丢失及监督质量受限的问题，本文提出了 AsymRec 框架，通过非对称设计解耦输入连续表征与输出离散目标，显著提升了推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式推荐（GenRec）模型在输入端存在因量化导致的语义丢失和流行度偏差问题（输入瓶颈），在输出端存在离散目标限制监督质量的问题（输出瓶颈）。

Method: 提出了 AsymRec 框架，包含：1.多专家语义投影(MSP)：将连续嵌入映射到 Transformer 隐藏空间；2.多面分层量化(MHQ)：构建高容量、结构化的离散目标并引入语义正则化。

Result: 实验表明，AsymRec 在多个数据集上平均性能提升了 15.8%，有效解决了生成式推荐中的双重信息瓶颈。

Conclusion: AsymRec 通过非对称的连续-离散框架解耦了输入和输出表征，有效缓解了生成式推荐系统中的信息瓶颈，在性能上显著优于现有的生成式推荐模型。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GenRec) models reformulate recommendation as a sequence generation task, representing items as discrete Semantic IDs used symmetrically as both inputs and prediction targets. We identify a critical dual-stage information bottleneck in this design: (1) the Input Bottleneck, where lossy quantization degrades fine-grained semantics, while popularity bias skews the learned representations toward frequent items, and (2) the Output Bottleneck, where imprecise discrete targets limit supervision quality. To address these issues, we propose AsymRec, an asymmetric continuous-discrete framework that decouples input and output representations. Specifically, Multi-expert Semantic Projection (MSP) maps continuous embeddings into the Transformer's hidden space via expert-specialized projections, preserving semantic richness and improving generalization to infrequent items. Multi-faceted Hierarchical Quantization (MHQ) constructs high-capacity, structured discrete targets through multi-view and multi-level quantization with semantic regularization, preventing dimensional collapse while retaining fine-grained distinctions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AsymRec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art generative recommenders by an average of 15.8 %. The code will be released.

</details>


### [290] [Discrimination Is Generation: Unifying Ranking and Retrieval from a Tokenizer Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14853)
*Shuli Wang,Junwei Yin,Changhao Li,Senjie Kou,Chi Wang,Yinqiu Huang,Yinhua Zhu,Haitao Wang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对生成式推荐中语义ID（SID）训练与个性化目标脱节的问题，本文提出了DIG框架，通过将分词器嵌入判别式排序模型进行端到端联合训练，使排序与检索任务实现协同进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义ID（SID）分词器多为独立训练，缺乏个性化信号，导致生成式检索在性能上落后于经典的判别式排序模型。作者认为检索与排序本质上是同一问题的不同粒度处理，旨在通过端到端训练弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 提出了DIG（Discrimination Is Generation）框架，将分词器（Tokenizer）嵌入判别式排序模型进行联合端到端训练；采用特征分配分类法（feature assignment taxonomy），将物品静态特征编码入SID，利用用户-物品交叉特征（u2i）引导码本边界，并引入MLP_u2t蒸馏模块以在推理阶段在标记层面近似u2i。

Result: 在三个公共基准测试和两个工业数据集上的实验表明，DIG显著提升了推荐系统的排序效果、检索质量以及统一检索-排序的整体性能。

Conclusion: DIG成功通过端到端训练将判别式排序融入语义ID（SID）的生成过程，打破了以往生成式推荐中SID训练与个性化目标解耦的局限，实现了排序与检索性能的双重提升。

Abstract: Semantic IDs (SIDs) define the generation space of generative recommendation and directly determine its personalization ceiling. However, existing tokenizers are trained independently with retrieval objectives, leaving personalization signals fully decoupled from the SID construction process -- a fundamental gap that causes generative retrieval to persistently lag behind discriminative ranking. In this paper, we rethink the essence of SIDs: \emph{ranking seeks argmax in item space while retrieval seeks argmax in token space; both are the same problem solved at different granularities.} Based on this insight, we propose \DIG (\textbf{D}iscrimination \textbf{I}s \textbf{G}eneration), which embeds the tokenizer inside a discriminative ranking model for end-to-end training -- the ranker naturally becomes a retrieval model, yielding two models from a single training run. \DIG is organized around a \emph{feature assignment taxonomy}: item-intrinsic static features are encoded into SIDs, user-item cross features (u2i) implicitly drive codebook boundaries toward recommendation decision boundaries during training, and an MLP$_\mathrm{u2t}$ distillation module approximates u2i at the token level for inference. Experiments on three public benchmarks and two industrial datasets demonstrate that \DIG simultaneously improves ranking, retrieval, and unified retrieval-ranking quality.

</details>
